TL;DR: 提出DuST（双重自训练）框架，通过在模型自身生成的候选程序构成的"判断空间"中利用执行正确性进行排序训练，在不直接奖励正确代码的情况下提升代码生成的测试时扩展性能。在5个模型（4B-30B）上验证，Qwen3-30B-Thinking在LiveCodeBench v6上Best-of-4提升+4.1，单次rollout即可匹配基座模型的Best-of-4性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统代码生成训练只利用稀疏的执行反馈（成功/失败），而测试时扩展中采样多个候选并比较产生的丰富对比信息在推理后被丢弃。论文认为这些比较信息定义了"双重判断空间"，能提供比单一成功/失败更丰富的训练信号，让模型从自身多种尝试的相对正确性结构中学习。

Method: DuST框架包含三个步骤：1）从模型自身分布采样候选程序；2）通过沙箱执行标注这些候选，保留同时包含成功和失败的组；3）使用GRPO训练模型按执行正确性对候选排序。目标是纯判别性的，模型从不因生成正确程序而直接获得奖励。通过on-policy RL将双重空间的学习迁移回原始生成空间。

Result: 在5个模型（涵盖两种架构家族、三个尺度4B-30B）上，DuST在LiveCodeBench上持续提升Best-of-4测试时扩展性能。Qwen3-30B-Thinking上：判断质量提升+6.2 NDCG，单样本pass@1提升+3.1，Best-of-4准确率提升+4.1。训练后的单次rollout匹配基座模型Best-of-4的性能。SFT在相同排序数据上仅提升判断而不提升生成，证明on-policy RL是跨空间迁移的关键机制。

Conclusion: 双重判断空间中的排序训练可以有效提升代码生成的生成质量和判断质量，on-policy RL是实现从判别空间到生成空间知识迁移的必要条件。

Abstract: Code generation is typically trained in the primal space of programs: a model produces a candidate solution and receives sparse execution feedback, often a single pass/fail bit. Test-time scaling enriches the inference procedure by sampling multiple candidates and judging among them, but the comparative information this process reveals is discarded after inference. We argue that this information defines a dual judgment space that provides a far richer training signal: the model learns not from an isolated success or failure, but from the relative correctness structure across its own plausible attempts, identifying which succeed, which fail, and what distinguishes them. We introduce DuST (Dual Self-Training), a framework for self-training from the dual judgment space. DuST samples candidate programs from the model's own distribution, labels them through sandbox execution, retains groups containing both successes and failures, and trains the model to rank candidates by execution correctness using GRPO. The objective is purely discriminative: the model is never directly rewarded for generating correct programs. Dual self-training improves both judgment and generation. Across five models spanning two families and three scales (4B to 30B), DuST consistently improves Best-of-4 test-time scaling on LiveCodeBench. For Qwen3-30B-Thinking on LiveCodeBench v6, judgment quality improves by +6.2 NDCG, single-sample pass@1 improves by +3.1, and Best-of-4 accuracy improves by +4.1. The trained model's single rollout matches the base model's Best-of-4 performance. SFT on the same ranking data improves judgment without improving generation, confirming that on-policy RL is the mechanism that transfers dual-space learning back into primal generation.

</details>


### [116] [A Theory of Time-Sensitive Language Generation: Sparse Hallucination Beats Mode Collapse](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.11302)
*Atul Ganju,Travis McVoy,Shaddin Dughmi,Shang-Hua Teng*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究在全局偏好序下具有时间约束的语言生成问题。证明了最终一致生成器无法实现及时生成，但允许幻觉率随时间消失的松弛条件下，可以对任意超线性截止函数实现最优密度，同时证明线性截止函数下即使允许幻觉率消失也无法实现及时生成。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在机器学习中，归纳偏置倾向于优先输出"更简单"或"更合理"的结果，因此需要研究在偏好序下，高排名字符串能否在截止期限前被生成，以实现及时性这一关键要求。

Method: 理论分析：首先证明最终一致生成器无法实现及时生成；然后引入幻觉率随时间消失的松弛一致性条件，证明该条件下可对任意超线性截止函数实现最优密度；最后通过紧性下界证明线性截止函数下即使允许幻觉率消失也无法做到。

Result: 1) 最终一致生成器无法实现及时生成（不可能性结果）；2) 在幻觉率随时间消失的松弛条件下，可对任意超线性截止函数达到最优密度；3) 线性截止函数下即使允许幻觉率消失也无法实现及时生成（紧性下界）。

Conclusion: 及时语言生成在强意义下对最终一致生成器是不可能的，但通过松弛一致性条件（允许幻觉率随时间消失）可以绕过这一不可能性结果，且超线性截止函数是最优可实现的条件。

Abstract: We study language generation in the limit under a global preference ordering on strings, as introduced by Kleinberg and Wei. As in [arXiv:2504.14370, arXiv:2511.05295], we aim for \emph{breadth}, but impose an additional requirement of timeliness: higher-ranked strings should be generated earlier. A string is then only credited if it is generated before a deadline, where its deadline is defined by a function that maps a string's rank in the target language to the time by which it must be produced. This is in keeping with a central consideration in machine learning, where inductive bias favors ``simpler'' or ``more plausible'' outputs, all else being equal. We show that timely generation is impossible in a strong sense for eventually consistent generators -- the protagonists of most prior related work. Under what is perhaps the mildest natural relaxation of consistency, a hallucination rate that vanishes over time, we show that we can circumvent our impossibility result. In particular, we can achieve optimal density with respect to any superlinear deadline function. We also show this is tight by ruling out timely generation with linear deadlines and vanishing hallucination rate.

</details>


### [117] [Couple to Control: Joint Initial Noise Design in Diffusion Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.11311)
*Jing Jia,Liyue Shen,Guanyang Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种通过设计初始噪声的依赖结构（耦合）来替代传统独立高斯噪声的扩散模型生成框架，能在不增加采样成本的情况下提升生成多样性和控制能力，实验证明该方法在多种模型和任务上优于或持平现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统扩散模型假设各生成样本的初始噪声相互独立，但这种独立性假设只是更广泛的联合噪声设计中的一种特例。作者旨在通过设计样本间噪声的耦合结构，在不改变预训练模型接收的边缘分布的前提下，实现对生成结果多样性和可控性的提升。

Method: 提出耦合噪声框架：每个噪声保持标准高斯边缘分布（与预训练模型兼容），但通过设计样本间的依赖结构（如排斥性高斯耦合、子空间耦合）来控制生成样本间的相互关系。该方法可作为独立生成的无额外成本替代方案，也可作为优化管线的结构化初始化。

Result: 在SD1.5、SDXL、SD3上，排斥性高斯耦合提升了生成画廊的多样性，同时基本保持提示对齐和图像质量；在多个多样性指标上以相同采样成本匹配或超越最新的测试时噪声优化基线；子空间耦合支持固定前景的多样化背景生成，与专用修补基线相比生成更自然的背景，并具有可调节的前景保真度权衡。

Conclusion: 初始噪声的耦合设计是传统独立噪声假设的有效泛化，能在不增加采样成本的情况下提升扩散模型生成的多样性和可控性，为噪声设计提供了统一的理论框架和实用工具。

Abstract: Diffusion models typically generate image batches from independent Gaussian initial noises. We argue that this independence assumption is only one choice within a broader class of valid joint noise designs. Instead, one can specify a coupling of the initial noises: each noise remains marginally standard Gaussian, so the pretrained diffusion model receives the same single-sample input distribution, while the dependence across samples is chosen by design. This reframes initial-noise control from selecting or optimizing individual seeds to designing the dependence structure of a multi-sample gallery. This view gives a general framework for initial-noise design, covering several existing methods as special cases and leading naturally to new coupled-noise constructions. Coupled noise can improve generation on its own without adding sampling cost, and it is flexible enough to serve as a structured initialization for optimization-based pipelines when additional computation is available. Empirically, repulsive Gaussian coupling improves gallery diversity on SD1.5, SDXL, and SD3 while largely preserving prompt alignment and image quality. It matches or outperforms recent test-time noise-optimization baselines on several diversity metrics at the same sampling cost as independent generation. Subspace couplings also support fixed-object background generation, producing diverse, natural backgrounds compared with specialized inpainting baselines, with a tunable trade-off in foreground fidelity.

</details>


### [118] [$\varepsilon$-Good Action Identification in Fixed-Budget Monte Carlo Tree Search](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.11324)
*Yinan Li,Tuan Nguyen,Kwang-Sung Jun*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究了深度2最大-最小树中固定预算下的最大-最小动作识别问题，提出了一种ε-无关算法，其误识别概率以exp(-Θ̃(T/H₂(ε)))衰减，并在理论上给出了上下界分析，这是首个可证明的固定预算最大-最小动作识别算法保证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究动机源于近似规划（approximate planning）的需求，即在蒙特卡洛树搜索（MCTS）这一重要特例中，学习者需要在固定预算下高效识别出最优或近似最优的动作（子树），而不依赖先验的ε知识。

Method: 提出了一种ε-无关（ε-agnostic）算法，该算法不需要将ε作为输入参数，但能够对每个有意义的ε实现实例相关的误差界。算法通过对样本的序列化分配，利用交叉子树间隙和子树内部间隙联合刻画复杂度H₂(ε)。

Result: 误识别概率衰减率为exp(-Θ̃(T/H₂(ε)))，其中H₂(ε)同时捕捉了跨子树和子树内部的间隙结构。当每个子树退化为单叶子节点时，问题退化为固定预算最佳臂识别，本文分析恢复（加速因子意义上）了已知的halving类方法的ε-好保证，并给出了Successive Rejects的新的ε-好保证。下界方面，互补的正反结果表明最大-最小识别具有与标准K臂赌博机不同的难度结构。

Conclusion: 本文首次为最大-最小动作识别问题提供了可证明的固定预算算法保证，揭示了该问题与经典K臂赌博机在难度结构上的本质差异，为更一般MCTS场景的理论分析奠定了基础。

Abstract: We study the fixed-budget max-min action identification problem in depth-2 max-min trees, an important special case of Monte Carlo Tree Search. A learner sequentially allocates $T$ samples to leaves and then recommends a subtree whose minimum leaf value is largest. Motivated by approximate planning, we focus on $\varepsilon$-good subtree identification, where any subtree whose min value is within $\varepsilon$ of the optimal maximin value is acceptable.
  Our main contribution is an $\varepsilon$-agnostic algorithm: it does not require $\varepsilon$ as input, but achieves instance-dependent error bounds for every meaningful $\varepsilon$. We show that the misidentification probability decays as $\exp(-\widetildeΘ(T/H_2(\varepsilon)))$, where $H_2(\varepsilon)$ captures both cross-subtree and within-subtree gaps. When each subtree has a single leaf, the problem reduces to standard fixed-budget best-arm identification, and our analysis recovers, up to accelerating factors, known $\varepsilon$-good guarantees for halving-style methods while giving a new $\varepsilon$-good guarantee for Successive Rejects.
  On the lower-bound side, we provide complementary positive and negative results showing that max-min identification has a different hardness structure from standard $K$-armed bandits. To our knowledge, this is the first provable fixed-budget algorithmic guarantee for max-min action identification.

</details>


### [119] [Neural Statistical Functions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.11327)
*Daniel Xu,Yuxin Xie,Minghao Guo,Haixu Wu,Wojciech Matusik*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出神经统计函数（Neural Statistical Functions），一种从预训练单样本预测器和稀疏数据样本中学习的新模型家族，能够在不进行显式采样的条件下直接推断连续运行条件范围内的统计量（如积分、分位数、最大值），通过前缀统计量将多样统计函数统一到区间条件框架中，在复杂物理过程（动力系统能量累积、气动响应分位数、碰撞过程最大应力）上实现高达100倍的模型评估次数缩减。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典深度学习处理单样本推断表现优异，但在实际应用中常需重复推理以估计统计量（如不确定性分析、极值分析），导致显著延迟。为此，本文旨在设计一种能直接推断统计量、避免显式采样的高效方法。

Method: 提出神经统计函数，引入前缀统计量概念，将积分、分位数、最大值等多样统计函数转化为区间条件框架；利用前缀统计量与单样本回归之间的原理性恒等式作为学习目标，模型从预训练的单样本预测器和稀疏数据样本中学习。

Result: 在动力系统累积能量、气动响应分位数、碰撞过程最大应力等复杂物理过程上取得优异统计量估计性能，模型评估次数减少高达100倍。

Conclusion: 神经统计函数为高效统计推断提供了一种统一框架，在保持估计精度的同时大幅降低计算成本，适用于复杂物理系统的统计量估计任务。

Abstract: Classical deep learning typically operates on individual cases. Despite its success, real-world usage often requires repeated inference to estimate statistical quantities for complex decision-making tasks involving uncertainty or extreme-value analysis, resulting in substantial latency. We introduce neural statistical functions, a new family of models learned from pre-trained single-sample predictors and scattered data samples, which can directly infer statistics over continuous operating condition ranges without explicit sampling. By introducing the notion of prefix statistics, we transform and unify diverse statistical functions (e.g., integrals, quantiles, and maxima) into an interval-conditional framework, in which a principled identity between the prefix statistics and the individual-case regression serves as the learning objective. Neural statistical functions achieve strong performance in estimating essential statistics of complex physical processes, including accumulated energy in dynamical systems, quantiles of aerodynamic responses, and maximum stress in crash processes, while achieving up to a 100$\times$ reduction in model evaluations.

</details>


### [120] [Epistemic Uncertainty for Test-Time Discovery](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.11328)
*Kainat Riaz,Muhammad Ahmed Mohsin,Ahsan Bilal,Muhammad Umer,Ayesha Mohsin,Aqib Riaz,Ali Subhan,John M. Cioffi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: UG-TTT通过维持一组低秩适配器的小型集成、利用token级互信息量化认知不确定性作为探索奖励，并结合核范数正则化保持适配器多样性，解决了强化学习在科学发现中因惩罚高方差变异而导致的奖励平台化问题，在四个基准测试中提升了最大奖励和解决方案多样性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准强化学习在科学发现中会惩罚高方差变异，导致策略偏好熟悉模式，最大奖励随平均奖励增加而趋于平台化。现有方法无法区分未探索区域与本质困难问题，需要一种信号来识别真实的知识边界。

Method: 在冻结基模型上维护一组低秩适配器（LoRA）的小型集成；计算token级互信息量化集成预测与权重假设之间的认知不确定性；将该度量作为探索奖励纳入策略梯度；使用核范数正则化确保适配器彼此保持差异。

Result: 在四个科学发现基准测试中，UG-TTT在三个任务上提升了最大奖励，保持了显著更高的解决方案多样性。消融实验证实核范数正则化对维持探索行为至关重要。

Conclusion: 通过集成适配器的不一致性来量化认知不确定性，并将其作为探索信号，可以有效引导策略探索训练覆盖不足的真实可发现区域，克服标准RL在科学发现中的探索瓶颈。

Abstract: Automated scientific discovery using large language models relies on identifying genuinely novel solutions. Standard reinforcement learning penalizes high-variance mutations, which leads the policy to prioritize familiar patterns. As a result, the maximum reward plateaus even as the average reward increases. Overcoming this limitation requires a signal that distinguishes unexplored regions from intrinsically difficult problems. This necessitates measuring disagreement across independently adapted weight hypotheses rather than relying on a single network's confidence. UG-TTT addresses this challenge by maintaining a small ensemble of low-rank adapters over a frozen base model. The per-token disagreement, quantified as the mutual information between ensemble predictions and weight hypotheses, isolates epistemic uncertainty and identifies positions where insufficient coverage leads to adapter divergence rather than intrinsic problem difficulty. This measure is incorporated as an exploration bonus into the policy gradient, directing the policy toward positions where persistent adapter disagreement signals low training coverage, the same frontier where genuine discovery is possible. A nuclear norm regularizer ensures the adapters remain distinct from one another, thereby preserving the exploration signal throughout training. Across four scientific discovery benchmarks, UG-TTT increases the maximum reward on three tasks, maintains substantially higher solution diversity, and an ablation study confirms that the regularizer is essential for sustaining this behavior.

</details>


### [121] [Gradient-Free Noise Optimization for Reward Alignment in Generative Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.11347)
*Jeongsol Kim,Hongeun Kim,Jian Wang,Jong Chul Ye*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: ZeNO是一种基于零阶优化的无梯度框架，将噪声优化建模为路径积分控制问题，仅需奖励评估即可实现扩散/流模型的推理时对齐，适用于不可微场景（如蛋白质结构生成）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有奖励对齐方法依赖多步随机轨迹，难以扩展到确定性生成器；而噪声空间优化需要反向传播，限制了在非可微环境中的应用。

Method: 将噪声优化形式化为路径积分控制问题，仅使用零阶奖励评估来估计更新；以Ornstein-Uhlenbeck参考过程实例化时，更新隐式联系到针对奖励倾斜分布的Langevin动力学。

Result: ZeNO实现了有效的推理时缩放，在多种生成器和奖励函数上表现优异，包括反向传播不可行的蛋白质结构生成任务。

Conclusion: ZeNO作为一种无梯度噪声优化框架，在无需反向传播的条件下，成功将奖励对齐扩展至确定性生成器和非可微场景，具有广泛的适用性。

Abstract: Existing reward alignment methods for diffusion and flow models rely on multi-step stochastic trajectories, making them difficult to extend to deterministic generators. A natural alternative is noise-space optimization, but existing approaches require backpropagation through the generator and reward pipeline, limiting applicability to differentiable settings. To address this, here we present ZeNO (Zeroth-order Noise Optimization), a gradient-free framework that formulates noise optimization as a path-integral control problem, estimable from zeroth-order reward evaluations alone. When instantiated with an Ornstein--Uhlenbeck reference process, the update connects to Langevin dynamics implicitly targeting a reward-tilted distribution. ZeNO enables effective inference-time scaling and demonstrates strong performance across diverse generators and reward functions, including a protein structure generation task where backpropagation is infeasible.

</details>


### [122] [gym-invmgmt: An Open Benchmarking Framework for Inventory Management Methods](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.11355)
*Reza Barati,Qinmin Vivian Hu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了gym-invmgmt基准测试平台，用于在统一契约下公平比较优化、启发式和学习的库存管理控制器。通过22个核心场景评估，发现随机规划（非预言机）性能最强但计算成本高，PPO-Transformer在学习控制器中表现最佳，而LLM更适合作为诊断工具而非自主优化器。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 库存策略比较常因评估合同差异而难以解释，拓扑、需求机制、信息访问、可行性约束、短缺处理方式和KPI定义的不同都会改变方法排名。现有基准缺乏统一、可审计的跨范式评估框架。

Method: 构建gym-invmgmt基准，基于Gymnasium兼容的OR-Gym库存管理扩展，提供共享的CoreEnv状态转移、奖励、动作边界和KPI合同，通过22个核心场景网格（加上4个MARL模式补充行）施加不同压力条件，对优化、启发式和学习控制器进行统一评估。

Result: 1) 随机规划（有预测信息）是非预言机参考中最强的，但在线计算成本高；2) PPO-Transformer在学习控制器中策略质量最好且推理速度快；3) 残差强化学习（Residual RL）提供有竞争力的混合性能；4) PPO-GNN在默认发散拓扑上极具竞争力，但在串行拓扑上鲁棒性较差；5) 模仿学习在平稳需求下表现良好，但需求漂移时性能下降；6) LLM基线更适合作为诊断控制器而非自主库存优化器。

Conclusion: 基准识别了场景条件下的领先方法，表明性能同时依赖于信息访问、需求漂移、拓扑和策略表示。没有单一方法在所有场景中占优，评估框架为跨范式库存管理研究提供了可审计的统一基础。

Abstract: Inventory-policy comparisons are often difficult to interpret because performance depends on the evaluation contract as much as on the policy itself. Differences in topology, demand regime, information access, feasibility constraints, shortage treatment, and Key Performance Indicator (KPI) definitions can change method rankings. We present gym-invmgmt, a Gymnasium-compatible extension of the OR-Gym inventory-management lineage for auditable cross-paradigm evaluation. The benchmark evaluates optimization, heuristic, and learned controllers under a shared CoreEnv transition, reward, action-bound, and KPI contract, while varying stress conditions through a 22-scenario core grid plus four supplemental MARL-mode rows. Within these released scenarios, informed stochastic programming provides the strongest non-oracle reference, reflecting the value of scenario hedging under forecast access, but at substantially higher online computational cost. Among learned controllers, the Proximal Policy Optimization Transformer variant (PPO-Transformer) achieves the strongest learned-policy quality at fast inference, while Residual Reinforcement Learning (Residual RL) provides competitive hybrid performance. The graph neural network variant (PPO-GNN) is highly competitive on the default divergent topology but less robust on the serial topology. Imitation learning performs well in stationary regimes but degrades under demand shift, and the bounded Large Language Model (LLM) policy-parameter baseline is best interpreted as a diagnostic controller rather than an autonomous inventory optimizer. Overall, the benchmark identifies scenario-conditioned leaders while showing that performance depends jointly on information access, demand shift, topology, and policy representation.

</details>


### [123] [The tractability landscape of diffusion alignment: regularization, rewards, and computational primitives](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.11361)
*Ankur Moitra,Andrej Risteski,Dhruv Rohatgi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于原语的推理时奖励对齐方法，针对KL散度和Wasserstein距离两种分布距离，分别给出了线性指数倾斜和近端传输预言两类计算原语，并分析了它们对不同类别奖励函数的适用性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 预训练扩散模型在推理时需要进行奖励对齐，但缺乏规范的分布距离来定义"接近性"约束，不同选择会导致不同的对齐分布和算法问题。本文旨在通过原语化的方法，探索哪些简单算法原语足以实现非平凡奖励类别的对齐。

Method: 1. KL距离下：目标分布为 q(x) ∝ p(x) exp(λ⁻¹r(x))，采用线性指数倾斜原语 q(x)∝p(x)exp(⟨θ, x⟩) 进行对齐，适用于凸低维奖励。2. Wasserstein距离下：目标分布需要通过近端传输预言实现，即求解 argmax_y {r(y)- λc(x,y)}，适用于凹函数或低维Lipschitz奖励 r(x)=f(Ax)。

Result: KL距离下，线性指数倾斜原语足以对齐广泛的凸低维奖励类；Wasserstein距离下，近端传输预言可高效实现凹函数或低维Lipschitz奖励的对齐。

Conclusion: 分布距离的选择直接影响对齐所需的计算原语以及可处理的奖励类别，这为扩散模型推理时奖励对齐提供了理论指导。

Abstract: Inference-time reward alignment asks how to turn a pre-trained diffusion model with base law $p$ into a sampler that favors a reward $r$ while remaining close to $p$. Since there is no canonical distributional distance for this closeness constraint, different choices lead to different "reward-aligned" laws and, just as importantly, different algorithmic problems. We develop a primitive-based approach to reward alignment: rather than assuming arbitrary reward-aligned laws can be sampled, we ask which simple algorithmic primitives suffice to implement alignment for non-trivial reward classes. If closeness is measured in KL distance, the target law is $q(x) \propto p(x) \exp(λ^{-1}r(x))$. For this setting, we show that linear exponential tilts of the form $q(x)\propto p(x)\exp(\langle θ, x \rangle)$ -- which according to recent work [MRR26] can be efficiently sampled from -- are a sufficient primitive for aligning to a very broad class of convex low-dimensional rewards. If closeness is measured in Wasserstein distance, the corresponding primitive is a proximal transport oracle: given $x$, solve $\mbox{argmax}_y \{r(y)- λc(x,y)\}$. This oracle can be efficiently implemented for concave or low-dimensional Lipschitz rewards $r(x)=f(Ax)$. Together, these results illustrate that the choice of distribution distance for alignment affects the computational primitive and the tractable reward class.

</details>


### [124] [Causal Fairness for Survival Analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.11362)
*Drago Plecko*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种用于生存/时间-事件分析的因果公平性框架，通过非参数方法将生存差异分解为直接、间接和虚假路径的贡献，并在ICU入院后的种族差异时间演化分析中验证了该方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的公平机器学习工作大多关注静态场景，而公平生存分析仍相对未被充分探索；现有方法采用统计公平性定义，即便有无限数据也无法解开产生差异的因果机制。

Method: 四步非参数方法：(1) 用图模型形式化关于删失和缺乏混杂的必要假设；(2) 恢复给定协变量的条件生存函数；(3) 应用因果约简定理将问题重新表述为适合因果路径分解的形式；(4) 高效估计效应。

Result: 该方法能够将生存分析中的差异分解为直接、间接和虚假路径的贡献，并在ICU入院后的种族差异时间演化分析中展示了其应用效果。

Conclusion: 该因果框架为生存/时间-事件分析中的公平性提供了可解释的因果分解方法，能够揭示差异产生的原因及其随时间演化的机制。

Abstract: In the data-driven era, large-scale datasets are routinely collected and analyzed using machine learning (ML) and artificial intelligence (AI) to inform decisions in high-stakes domains such as healthcare, employment, and criminal justice, raising concerns about the fairness behavior of these systems. Existing works in fair ML cover tasks such as bias detection, fair prediction, and fair decision-making, but largely focus on static settings. At the same time, fairness in temporal contexts, particularly survival/time-to-event (TTE) analysis, remains relatively underexplored, with current approaches to fair survival analysis adopting statistical fairness definitions, which, even with unlimited data, cannot disentangle the causal mechanisms that generate disparities. To address this gap, we develop a causal framework for fairness in TTE analysis, enabling the decomposition of disparities in survival into contributions from direct, indirect, and spurious pathways. This provides a human-understandable explanation of why disparities arise and how they evolve over time. Our non-parametric approach proceeds in four steps: (1) formalizing the necessary assumptions about censoring and lack of confounding using a graphical model; (2) recovering the conditional survival function given covariates; (3) applying the Causal Reduction Theorem to reframe the problem in a form amenable to causal pathway decomposition; (4) estimating the effects efficiently. Finally, our approach is used to analyze the temporal evolution of racial disparities in outcome after admission to an intensive care unit (ICU).

</details>


### [125] [LPDP: Inference-Time Reward Control for Variable-Length DNA Generation with Edit Flows](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.11368)
*Jeongchan Kim,Yunkyung Ko,Jong Chul Ye*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出LPDP方法，一种无需训练、基于局部离散编程的推理时奖励控制框架，用于变长DNA序列生成，在增强子优化和外显子-内含子修复两种场景下验证有效。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有奖励引导的DNA生成框架多操作于固定长度序列空间，缺乏生成变长DNA（通过插入、删除、替换等生物学合理操作）的能力。

Method: 提出LPDP（局部扰动离散编程），一种无需训练、感知中间状态与动作的局部解析算子。在每个引导 rollout 步骤中：对一步根编辑评分、保留近优根带、通过求解有界局部离散程序重排序每个保留根。该程序利用编辑动作的类型几何结构聚焦于替换/插入/删除子图，并使用硬Max或软LSE聚合局部延续。

Result: 在两种机制中实例化LPDP：前加载奖励倾斜用于增强子优化（早期编辑关键），后加载奖励倾斜用于外显子-内含子-外显子修复（晚期编辑微调剪接边界上下文）。

Conclusion: Edit Flows结合LPDP能够有效实现变长DNA序列的推理时奖励控制，相较于固定长度方法更具生物学合理性。

Abstract: We study the application of recent Edit Flows for inference-time reward control for DNA sequence generation. Unlike most reward-guided DNA generation frameworks, which operate on fixed-length sequence spaces, Edit Flows have a potential to generate variable-length DNA through biologically plausible insertion, deletion, and substitution operations. In particular, we propose Local Perturbation Discrete Programming (LPDP), a training-free, intermediate-state and action-aware local re-solving operator for variable-length DNA edit-action generators at inference time. More specifically, at each guided rollout step, LPDP scores one-step root edits, retains a near-best root band, and re-ranks each retained root by solving a bounded local discrete program around its child sequence. This local program uses the typed geometry of edit actions to focus on coherent substitution, insertion, or deletion subgraphs, and aggregates local continuations with either a hard Max backup or a soft log-sum-exponential (LSE) backup. We instantiate LPDP in two regimes: front-loaded reward tilting for enhancer optimization, where early edits are critical for establishing global regulatory sequence structure, and back-loaded reward tilting for exon-intron-exon inpainting, where late edits fine-tune splice-boundary contexts.

</details>


### [126] [Test-Time Compute for Dense Retrieval: Agentic Program Generation with Frozen Embedding Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.11374)
*Han Xiao*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文发现，测试时计算不仅对大型推理模型有益，也能提升小型嵌入模型的性能。通过一个智能体式程序搜索循环，作者在冻结的嵌入API上探索了259个候选推理程序，发现最有效的策略是一个简单的代数运算：将局部前K个文档的softmax加权质心与查询向量进行插值。这一无参数默认策略在7个嵌入模型家族（参数范围跨越10倍）上显著提升了nDCG@10，并在完整BEIR验证集上得到确认。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前普遍认为测试时计算仅对大型推理模型有益，本文试图验证这一假设是否也适用于小型嵌入模型。由于大多数现代嵌入检查点是从大型LLM主干蒸馏而来，继承了其表示空间，因此冻结的嵌入模型应该能从额外的推理计算中获益而无需重新训练。

Method: 使用智能体式程序搜索循环，在冻结的嵌入API上跨越90代探索259个候选推理程序，寻找最优的测试时计算策略。

Result: 整个Pareto前沿收敛于一个简单的代数运算：softmax加权的局部前K个文档质心与查询的插值。该无参数默认方法在7个嵌入模型家族（参数范围10倍）上统计显著地提升了nDCG@10，并在完整BEIR验证集上得到验证。

Conclusion: 测试时计算并非大型推理模型的专利，小型嵌入模型同样能从中受益。一个简单的插值策略就能在多种嵌入模型上取得一致的性能提升。

Abstract: Test-time compute is widely believed to benefit only large reasoning models. We show it also helps small embedding models. Most modern embedding checkpoints are distilled from large LLM backbones and inherit their representation space; a frozen embedding model should therefore benefit from extra inference compute without retraining. Using an agentic program-search loop, we explore 259 candidate inference programs over a frozen embedding API across ninety generations. The entire Pareto frontier collapses onto a single algebra: a softmax-weighted centroid of the local top-K documents interpolated with the query. This parameter-free default lifts nDCG@10 statistically significantly across seven embedding-model families spanning a tenfold parameter range, with held-out full-BEIR validation confirming the lift on every model tested.

</details>


### [127] [TRACE: Temporal Routing with Autoregressive Cross-channel Experts for EEG Representation Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.11380)
*Fan Ma,Qier An,Peng Chen,Lingfei Qian,Xiang Lan,Mingyang Jiang,Zhiling Gu,Xenophon Papademetris,Hua Xu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: TRACE是一个自回归EEG预训练框架，通过时间自适应和跨通道一致的计算，利用因果上下文预测未来EEG片段，在多个下游基准测试中取得最优或竞争性结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: EEG信号具有多通道和非平稳特性，现有架构要么对所有时间步施加统一计算，要么独立处理每个通道的片段，无法很好地匹配EEG的时空结构。作者旨在设计一种能同时处理时间动态性和跨通道相关性的预训练框架。

Method: 提出TRACE框架：在每个时间步，从因果的跨通道历史中推导出专家路由决策，并将其联合应用于该时间步的所有通道，从而保持瞬时跨通道一致性，同时允许不同时间区域激活不同的计算。该框架支持异构预训练，兼容不同通道数、导联布局、序列长度和记录域的数据。

Result: 在8个EEG下游基准测试上，TRACE在多个任务上取得最佳结果，在运动想象和临床事件分类任务上具有竞争力。消融实验验证了跨通道时间路由的重要性。

Conclusion: 跨通道时间自适应路由对于学习可迁移的EEG表示至关重要，TRACE通过自回归预测和联合通道路由有效解决了EEG信号的多通道和非平稳特性带来的挑战。

Abstract: Learning transferable representations for electroencephalography (EEG) remains challenging because EEG signals are inherently multi-channel and non-stationary. Channels observed at the same time provide coupled measurements of neural activity, while the relevant temporal dynamics vary across contexts. This structure is poorly matched by architectures that apply uniform computation across time or route each channel patch independently. To this end, we propose TRACE, an autoregressive EEG pre-training framework that predicts future EEG patches from causal context while performing temporally adaptive and cross-channel coherent computation. At each temporal step, TRACE derives an expert routing decision from the causal cross-channel history and applies it jointly to all channels at that step. This preserves instantaneous cross-channel coherence while allowing different temporal regimes to activate different computation. Since routing is defined over the available channel set and causal temporal context, TRACE is compatible with heterogeneous pre-training across corpora with different channel counts, montages, sequence lengths, and recording domains. Across eight downstream EEG benchmarks, TRACE is evaluated in both settings: when downstream domains are seen only as unlabeled pre-training data and when downstream datasets are completely unseen during pre-training. It obtains the best results on several benchmarks while remaining competitive on motor imagery and clinical event classification tasks, with ablations supporting the importance of cross-channel temporal routing.

</details>


### [128] [Behavioral Mode Discovery for Fine-tuning Multimodal Generative Policies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.11387)
*Alberta Longhini,David Emukpere,Jean-Michel Renders,Seungsu Kim*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出一种无监督模式发现框架，在强化学习微调生成式策略（如扩散策略）时，通过互信息作为内在奖励来保持动作分布的多模态性，避免行为多样性坍塌。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有RL微调生成式策略的方法虽然提升了任务性能，但往往导致多样化的行为坍缩到单一奖励最大化模式，丧失了生成式策略固有的多模态优势。

Method: 提出无监督模式发现框架，先挖掘生成式策略中潜在的潜在行为模式，然后利用互信息作为内在奖励来正则化RL微调过程，在提升任务成功率的同时保持行为多样性。

Result: 在机器人操作任务上，该方法相比传统微调方法取得了更高的成功率和更丰富的多模态动作分布。

Conclusion: 无监督模式发现结合互信息正则化能有效缓解RL微调中的模式坍塌问题，兼顾任务性能与行为多样性。

Abstract: We address the problem of fine-tuning pre-trained generative policies with reinforcement learning (RL) while preserving the multimodality of their action distributions. Existing methods for RL fine-tuning of generative policies (e.g., diffusion policies) improve task performance but often collapse diverse behaviors into a single reward-maximizing mode. To mitigate this issue, we propose an unsupervised mode discovery framework that uncovers latent behavioral modes within generative policies. The discovered modes enable the use of mutual information as an intrinsic reward, regularizing RL fine-tuning to enhance task success while maintaining behavioral diversity. Experiments on robotic manipulation tasks demonstrate that our method consistently outperforms conventional fine-tuning approaches, achieving higher success rates and preserving richer multimodal action distributions.

</details>


### [129] [MuonQ: Enhancing Low-Bit Muon Quantization via Directional Fidelity Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.11396)
*Yupeng Su,Ruijie Zhang,Ziyue Liu,Yequan Zhao,Zheng Zhang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: MuonQ 是一个面向 Muon 优化器的低比特（4-bit）训练框架，通过定向保真优化、预量化归一化、结构分解和 μ-law 压扩量化等技术，在保持训练效果的同时将优化器状态内存降低最高 7.3 倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Muon 优化器通过梯度正交化大幅节省计算，但其优化器状态对量化误差高度敏感：正交化过程丢弃了奇异值幅度信息，导致奇异向量方向上的微小量化误差在更新中被放大。

Method: 1) 预量化归一化；2) 基于幂迭代的结构分解，对主导奇异分量单独量化；3) μ-law 压扩量化，提高密集区域动量值的分辨率。

Result: 在 GPT 和 LLaMA 风格模型的预训练实验中，4-bit MuonQ 在训练损失和下游任务准确率上均接近全精度 Muon，同时优化器状态内存减少最高 7.3 倍。

Conclusion: MuonQ 实现了 Muon 优化器的高效低比特量化训练，为大规模语言模型的训练提供了内存高效的替代方案。

Abstract: The Muon optimizer has emerged as a compelling alternative to Adam for training large language models, achieving remarkable computational savings through gradient orthogonalization. However, Muon's optimizer state is more sensitive to quantization errors: because the orthogonalization discards the magnitudes of singular values and retains only directional information, even small quantization errors in singular vector directions are amplified in the update. In this work, we propose MuonQ, a low-bit Muon training framework built on the principle of directional fidelity optimization. First, we apply a pre-quantization normalization so that each step introduces quantization errors of the same magnitude, preventing the accumulated error from developing a preferred direction. Second, we introduce a structural decomposition that separately quantizes the dominant singular components via power iteration, ensuring that quantization errors perturb only singular value magnitudes rather than rotating singular vector directions. Third, we adopt $μ$-law companding quantization to allocate higher resolution to densely packed momentum values, shifting the quantization objective from outlier preservation to dense-region distinguishability. Together, these techniques enable stable 4-bit quantization of Muon's optimizer states. Pre-training experiments on GPT-style and LLaMA-style models demonstrate that MuonQ at 4-bit precision closely matches full-precision Muon in both training loss and downstream task accuracy, while reducing optimizer state memory by up to 7.3 $\times$. Our code is available at https://github.com/YupengSu/MuonQ.

</details>


### [130] [More Than Meets the Eye: A Semantics-Aware Traffic Augmentation Framework for Generalizable Website Fingerprinting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.11402)
*Youquan Xian,Xueying Zeng,Lingjia Meng,Lei Cui,Runhan Song,Wei Wang,Zhengquan Ding,Peng Liu,Zhiyu Hao*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: SATA是一个语义感知的流量增强框架，通过应用层语义增强和跨层特征对齐，解决深度学习网站指纹识别在跨地理/时间分布时的泛化问题，在开放场景下ACC提升90.81%，AUROC提升48.37%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有深度学习网站指纹识别方法在封闭世界数据集上表现良好，但在真实环境（地理和时间偏移）下泛化能力差。根本原因是应用层资源组成可变性和跨层封装导致的可观测特征不稳定性相互耦合，引起底层应用语义与可观测流量特征之间的系统性偏移。

Method: 提出SATA框架，包含两个核心模块：(1) 应用层语义增强：基于协议规则扩展每个流内的资源组成模式和帧序列模式；(2) 跨层特征对齐：通过知识蒸馏对齐增强后的帧序列与数据包长度序列特征，实现跨层特征对齐。

Result: SATA能够生成训练集中不存在但在测试集中真实存在的流量模式。在开放世界场景下，ACC提升90.81%，AUROC提升48.37%。在多样化复杂场景下显著提升主流模型的性能。

Conclusion: SATA通过语义感知的数据增强有效解决了跨域泛化问题，代码已开源。

Abstract: Deep learning-based website fingerprinting has emerged as an effective technique for inferring the websites users visit. Although existing methods achieve strong performance on closed-world datasets, they often fail to generalize to real-world environments, especially under geographic and temporal shifts. This limitation fundamentally stems from the coupled effects of two key challenges: application-layer resource composition variability and observable feature instability induced by cross-layer encapsulation. Intertwined, these factors induce systematic shifts between underlying application semantics and observable traffic features. To address the above challenges, we propose SATA , a semantics-aware traffic augmentation framework. Specifically, SATA first performs application-layer semantic augmentation based on protocol rules, expanding the resource composition patterns within each flow and frame sequence patterns under protocol constraints. Based on these augmented frame sequences, we further introduce a cross-layer feature alignment mechanism via knowledge distillation. It aligns frame sequence with packet-length sequence features, enabling cross-layer feature alignment between enhanced semantics and observable sequences. Extensive experiments show that SATA successfully generates traffic patterns that are absent from the training set but genuinely exist in the test set, and significantly improves the performance of mainstream models across diverse and complex scenarios. In particular, in open-world settings, SATA improves ACC by 90.81% and AUROC by 48.37%. The source code of the prototype system is available at https://anonymous.4open.science/r/SATA-B6C2/.

</details>


### [131] [fg-expo: Frontier-guided exploration-prioritized policy optimization via adaptive kl and gaussian curriculum](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.11403)
*Mingxiong Lin,Zhangquan Gong,Maowen Tang,Qian Li,Chuangchuang Wang,Jian Ma,Sutian Huang,Kai Tang,Haonan Lu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出FG-ExPO，通过自适应KL系数调整（AKL）和高斯课程采样（GCS）两个轻量级组件，改进了GRPO在LLM数学推理中的两大效率问题：固定KL系数限制探索和均匀采样忽略中等难度问题的信息价值。在多个数学推理基准上，FG-ExPO显著优于原始GRPO，尤其在pass@32指标上提升明显，表明其扩大了模型在固定推理预算下的有效探索空间。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: GRPO存在两个被忽视的效率问题：(1) 固定KL系数在模型需要显著偏离参考策略时过度限制了策略探索；(2) 均匀采样问题忽略了中等难度问题能提供最具信息量的梯度信号这一事实。因此需要针对这两个问题进行优化。

Method: 提出FG-ExPO框架，包含两个轻量级组件：(1) Accuracy-Conditioned KL Scaling (AKL) - 通过批次平均准确率的平滑非线性函数动态调整KL惩罚强度，模型表现差时放松约束，表现好时加强约束；(2) Gaussian Curriculum Sampling (GCS) - 以约0.5的适中准确率为中心，按高斯分布为问题分配采样权重，使模型训练聚焦于学习前沿。

Result: 在DeepSeek-R1-Distill-Qwen-1.5B和Qwen3-8B-Base上，跨越六个数学推理基准的评估表明FG-ExPO始终优于原始GRPO。在AIME 2025 pass@32指标上绝对提升13.34（从63.33%到76.67%），8B模型上pass@32平均提升2.66。pass@32的提升幅度显著大于pass@1，验证了FG-ExPO扩大了模型在固定推理预算下的有效探索空间。

Conclusion: FG-ExPO通过自适应KL调整和基于课程的问题采样，有效解决了GRPO中固定KL系数和均匀采样的效率问题，扩大了模型的有效探索空间，在数学推理任务上取得了显著性能提升。

Abstract: Reinforcement Learning with Verifiable Rewards (RLVR) has become the standard paradigm for LLM mathematical reasoning, with Group Relative Policy Optimization (GRPO) serving as the dominant algorithm. We identify two overlooked inefficiencies inherent in GRPO. First, a fixed KL coefficient overly restricts policy exploration at moments when the model needs to diverge significantly from the reference policy. Second, uniform question sampling overlooks that moderately difficult problems produce the most informative gradient signals. We propose FG-ExPO, short for Frontier-Guided Exploration-Prioritized Policy Optimization, which integrates two lightweight components. Accuracy-Conditioned KL Scaling (AKL) adjusts the KL penalty strength through a smooth nonlinear function of batch average accuracy, loosening the constraint when the model performs poorly and strengthening it when the model achieves satisfactory results. Gaussian Curriculum Sampling (GCS) assigns sampling weights to questions following a Gaussian distribution centered at a moderate accuracy level around 0.5, focusing model training on its learning frontier. We conduct evaluations on DeepSeek-R1-Distill-Qwen-1.5B and Qwen3-8B-Base across six mainstream mathematical reasoning benchmarks. Experimental results demonstrate that FG-ExPO consistently outperforms vanilla GRPO. It delivers an absolute improvement of 13.34 on the AIME 2025 pass@32 metric, rising from 63.33 percent to 76.67 percent, and obtains an average pass@32 gain of 2.66 on the 8B model. The substantially larger performance gains observed on pass@32 compared to pass@1 verify that FG-ExPO enlarges the model's effective exploration space under a fixed inference budget.

</details>


### [132] [20/20 Vision Language Models: A Prescription for Better VLMs through Data Curation Alone](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.11405)
*Siddharth Joshi,Haoli Yin,Rishabh Adiga,Haakon Mongstad,Alvin Deng,Aldo Carranza,Alex Fang,Amro Abbas,Anshuman Suri,Brett Larsen,Daniel Zayas,Darren Teh,David Schwab,Diego Kiner,Fan Pan,Jack Urbanek,Jason Lee,Jason Telanoff,Josh Wills,Kaleigh Mentzer,Luke Merrick,Maximilian Böther,Parth Doshi,Paul Burstein,Pratyush Maini,Ties Robroek,Tony Jiang,Vidhi Jain,Vineeth Dorna,Zhengping Wang,Bogdan Gaza,Ari Morcos,Matthew Leavitt*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文系统研究了数据筛选（Data Curation）对视觉语言模型（VLM）性能的影响，在固定架构、训练配方和计算量的条件下，仅通过优化训练数据，在20个公开VLM基准上平均提升+11.7pp，在DatBench的9个能力轴上平均提升+11.3pp。2B模型以约17倍更少的训练计算量超越InternVL3.5-2B达9.9pp，以约87倍更少的计算量将差距缩小至与Qwen3-VL-2B仅差1.8pp。此外，数据筛选还带来可靠性（标准差降低~67%）、OOD泛化（+7.2pp）、行为质量（更诚实、更具体、更简洁）和推理成本Pareto优势等额外收益。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 数据筛选已显著提升了语言模型和对比图像-文本预训练的质量-计算量前沿，但其在视觉语言模型（VLM）中的作用尚未被充分探索。本文旨在回答：在固定架构、训练配方和计算量的前提下，仅通过数据筛选能将VLM性能提升到何种程度。

Method: 采用MAmmoTH-VL单图像子集作为基础数据，通过数据筛选管道（Data Curation Pipeline）优化训练数据，保持架构、训练配方和计算量不变，仅改变训练数据。在20个公开VLM基准和自建高保真评估套件DatBench（9个能力轴）上进行评估。

Result: 1) 在20个公开VLM基准上平均提升+11.7pp，DatBench的9个能力轴上平均提升+11.3pp；2) 2B模型以~17x更少训练计算量超越InternVL3.5-2B达9.9pp，以~87x更少计算量接近Qwen3-VL-2B（仅差1.8pp）；3) 可靠性：训练种子的能力标准差降低~67%，4k-16k上下文长度增益保持不变；4) OOD泛化：9项评估OOD平均+7.2pp，单图训练下多图BLINK +3.09pp，Visual Correspondence +11.8pp；5) 行为改善：更诚实、更具体、更简洁、更少拒绝；6) 推理成本Pareto优势：各尺度（1B/2B/4B）均提升准确率同时降低响应FLOPs，4B模型以3.3x更低FLOPs匹配近前沿准确率。

Conclusion: 数据筛选是构建更好VLM的高杠杆工具，仅通过预训练阶段的数据优化，即可在高达~150倍更少的训练计算量下达到近前沿的准确率。

Abstract: Data curation has shifted the quality-compute frontier for language-model and contrastive image-text pretraining, but its role for vision-language models (VLMs) is far less established. We ask how far data curation alone can take VLM performance, holding architecture, training recipe, and compute fixed and varying only the training data. Our pipeline, applied to the MAmmoTH-VL single-image subset, lifts performance by +11.7pp on average across 20 public VLM benchmarks (spanning grounding, VQA, OCR/documents, captioning, spatial/3D, counting, charts, math, brand-ID, and multi-image reasoning) and by +11.3pp on average across all nine capability axes of DatBench, our high-fidelity VLM eval suite. At 2B, our curated model surpasses InternVL3.5-2B by 9.9pp at ~17x less training compute and closes the gap to Qwen3-VL-2B to within 1.8pp at ~87x less compute, from pretraining alone. Beyond accuracy, curation delivers four further properties: (1) Reliability: per-capability std across training seeds drops by ~67% and the lift survives a 4k-to-16k context-length sweep; (2) OOD generalization: the 9-eval OOD average rises by +7.2pp, and multi-image BLINK rises by +3.09pp despite single-image-only training, with Visual Correspondence gaining +11.8pp; (3) Behavioral gains beyond benchmarks: across ~1,100 open-ended queries the curated 2B is more honest and more specific than the matched-compute baseline, and more concise and less refusal-prone than a frontier 2B reference; (4) Pareto-dominance on inference cost: at every scale (1B, 2B, 4B) the curated model raises accuracy while lowering response FLOPs vs. the matched-compute baseline, and the curated 4B matches near-frontier accuracy at 3.3x lower response FLOPs than Qwen3-VL-4B. Data curation is a high-leverage tool for building better VLMs, reaching near-frontier accuracy at up to ~150x less training compute.

</details>


### [133] [A Boundary-Aware Non-parametric Granular-Ball Classifier Based on Minimum Description Length](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.11406)
*Zeqiang Xian,Caihui Liu,Yong Zhang,Wenjing Qiu,Duoqian Miao,Witold Pedrycz*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于最小描述长度原理的粒度球分类器(MDL-GBC)，将类条件粒度球构建形式化为局部模型选择问题，通过统一描述长度准则比较单球模型、双球模型和核心-边界模型，实现边界感知、非参数且可解释的分类。在18个基准数据集上，该方法在Accuracy、Macro-F1和平均排名上均取得最优结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有粒度球分类方法依赖手工设计的质量度量、邻域规则或启发式分裂与停止准则，导致局部构建决策缺乏透明度，且难以显式建模边界敏感区域。

Method: 基于MDL原理，将类条件粒度球构建形式化为局部模型选择问题：目标类样本提供正类证据，其余类样本提供负边界证据；对当前粒度球，在统一描述长度准则下比较单球模型、双球模型和核心-边界模型三种候选解释，选择结果决定球的保留、几何分裂或精细化拆分；预测时采用类级混合编码规则聚合同类稳定球，通过比较类间编码代价分配测试样本。

Result: 在18个基准数据集上，MDL-GBC在Accuracy、Macro-F1和平均排名上优于经典分类器和代表性粒度球方法，取得最佳平均性能。

Conclusion: MDL-GBC为传统的启发式粒度球分类策略提供了一种有效且可解释的替代方案。

Abstract: Existing granular-ball classification methods are often driven by handcrafted quality measures, neighborhood rules, or heuristic splitting and stopping criteria, which may reduce the transparency of local construction decisions and hinder explicit modeling of boundary-sensitive regions. To address this issue, this paper proposes a Minimum Description Length based Granular-Ball Classifier (MDL-GBC), a boundary-aware non-parametric and interpretable granular-ball classifier. MDL-GBC formulates class-conditional granular-ball construction as a local model selection problem under the Minimum Description Length principle. For each class, samples from the target class provide positive class evidence, while samples from the remaining classes provide negative boundary evidence. For each current granular ball, three candidate explanations are compared under a unified description-length criterion: a single-ball model, a two-ball model, and a core-boundary model. The selected model determines whether the ball is retained, geometrically split, or refined into core and boundary-sensitive child balls, thereby making local construction decisions consistent with the MDL-based classification mechanism. During prediction, a class-level mixture coding rule aggregates stable granular balls of the same class and assigns the test sample by comparing class-wise coding costs. Experiments on 18 benchmark datasets show that MDL-GBC achieves competitive classification performance against classical classifiers and representative granular-ball-based methods, obtaining the best average Accuracy, Macro-F1, and average rank. These results indicate that MDL-GBC provides an effective and interpretable alternative to conventional heuristic granular-ball classification strategies.

</details>


### [134] [Generative Diffusion Prior Distillation for Long-Context Knowledge Transfer](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.11414)
*Nilushika Udayangani,Kishor Nandakishor,Marimuthu Palaniswami*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出生成式扩散先验蒸馏（GDPD）框架，利用扩散模型的迭代恢复能力，将完整序列教师模型的特征知识蒸馏到部分序列学生模型中，解决因数据不完整导致的泛化差距问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 实际应用中时间序列分类器只能使用部分前缀数据，导致缺乏判别性模式而难以泛化。现有知识蒸馏方法主要针对参数容量差距，当泛化差距源于训练数据差异（完整vs部分）时，教师的完整上下文特征对学生来说是压倒性的目标信号。

Method: 提出GDPD框架：将学生短上下文特征视为目标完整上下文特征的退化观测，学习教师特征的扩散生成先验；基于此先验进行后验采样，得到最能解释学生特征中缺失的长程信息的教师表示；优化学生特征使其相对于这些目标最小化退化程度。

Result: 在多种早停设置、数据集和架构上的大量实验表明，GDPD在全到部分蒸馏任务上表现有效。

Conclusion: GDPD通过为每个学生特征提供任务相关的长上下文知识分布，有效改善了部分序列分类任务的学习效果。

Abstract: While traditional time-series classifiers assume full sequences at inference, practical constraints (latency and cost) often limit inputs to partial prefixes. The absence of class-discriminative patterns in partial data can significantly hinder a classifier's ability to generalize. This work uses knowledge distillation (KD) to equip partial time series classifiers with the generalization ability of their full-sequence counterparts. In KD, high-capacity teacher transfers supervision to aid student learning on the target task. Matching with teacher features has shown promise in closing the generalization gap due to limited parameter capacity. However, when the generalization gap arises from training-data differences (full versus partial), the teacher's full-context features can be an overwhelming target signal for the student's short-context features. To provide progressive, diverse, and collective teacher supervision, we propose Generative Diffusion Prior Distillation (GDPD), a novel KD framework that treats short-context student features as degraded observations of the target full-context features. Inspired by the iterative restoration capability of diffusion models, we learn a diffusion-based generative prior over teacher features. Leveraging this prior, we posterior-sample target teacher representations that could best explain the missing long-range information in the student features and optimize the student features to be minimally degraded relative to these targets. GDPD provides each student feature with a distribution of task-relevant long-context knowledge, which benefits learning on the partial classification task. Extensive experiments across earliness settings, datasets, and architectures demonstrate GDPD's effectiveness for full-to-partial distillation.

</details>


### [135] [FastUMAP: Scalable Dimensionality Reduction via Bipartite Landmark Sampling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.11428)
*Hongmin Li*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: FastUMAP是一种基于地标的快速降维方法，适用于高维数据的重复探索性分析，在保持合理精度的同时实现近一个数量级的加速。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在高维数据探索分析中，分析师经常反复运行降维（改变预处理、子集或超参数），而非线性方法容易成为瓶颈，因此需要一种专为重复使用场景设计的高效降维方法。

Method: FastUMAP构建稀疏的点-地标模糊图，从诱导的地标亲和矩阵计算Nyström谱热启动，然后使用双部图上的UMAP风格目标函数优化所有样本坐标。通过地标比例r=m/n在运行时间和保真度之间进行权衡。

Result: 在9个基准数据集上的实验（样本量178到70,000），FastUMAP在7个数据集上取得最低运行时间。在MNIST和Fashion-MNIST（n=70000）上仅需约4.6秒，而Barnes-Hut t-SNE需73-75秒；FastUMAP的kNN准确率为91.4%，最强基线为94.6%。

Conclusion: FastUMAP最适合作为重复探索性嵌入的快速选项，而非替代精度优先的方法。

Abstract: Exploratory analysis of high-dimensional data rarely stops at a single embedding. In practice, analysts rerun dimensionality reduction after changing preprocessing, subsets, or hyperparameters, and standard nonlinear methods can quickly become the bottleneck. We introduce FastUMAP (Bipartite Manifold Approximation and Projection), a landmark-based method designed for this repeated-use setting. FastUMAP builds a sparse point-landmark fuzzy graph, computes a Nystrom spectral warm start from the induced landmark affinity, and then refines all sample coordinates with a UMAP-style objective on the bipartite graph. The landmark ratio r = m/n provides a direct way to trade runtime against fidelity. On 9 benchmark datasets spanning 178 to 70,000 samples, FastUMAP has the lowest runtime on 7 datasets in our reported default-implementation comparison on one workstation. On MNIST and Fashion-MNIST (n=70000), it runs in about 4.6 seconds, compared with about 73--75 seconds for Barnes--Hut t-SNE, while reaching 91.4% mean kNN accuracy versus 94.6% for the strongest accuracy baseline. FastUMAP is therefore best viewed as a fast option for repeated exploratory embedding, rather than as a replacement for accuracy-first methods.

</details>


### [136] [Deep Minds and Shallow Probes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.11448)
*Su Hyeong Lee,Risi Kondor*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出神经表示在重参数化下具有对称性，并基于此对称性原理推导出坐标稳定的探针层次结构（线性探针为度-1成员），以及跨模型探针迁移中应使用探针可见商空间而非完整隐藏状态。实验验证了度-2探针的优势和基于商空间的迁移方法在跨模型族监控器可移植性上的有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 神经表示并非唯一对象，不同系统实现相同下游计算时其隐藏坐标可能相差重参数化。现有探针方法未能考虑这种表示对称性，导致探针与特定基系绑定，缺乏稳定性。

Method: 在最终读出层的精确可解设定下，将等价实现间的仿射坐标变换视为群作用，推导出坐标稳定的探针层次结构（度-k多项式探针族），并定义探针可见商空间作为跨模型迁移的自然对象。

Result: 合成和真实任务实验表明：度-2探针在非线性场景下优于线性探针；基于商空间的迁移方法实现了跨模型族的覆盖感知监控器可移植性。

Conclusion: 该工作为神经探针建立了更广泛的几何表示理论框架，覆盖感知的监控器迁移是其具体操作层面的应用成果。

Abstract: Neural representations are not unique objects. Even when two systems realize the same downstream computation, their hidden coordinates may differ by reparameterization. A probe family intended to reveal structure already present in a representation should therefore be stable under the relevant representation symmetries rather than be tied to a particular basis. We study this group action in the tractable exact setting of the final readout layer, where equivalent realizations induce affine changes of hidden coordinates. The resulting symmetry principle singles out a unique hierarchy of shallow coordinate-stable probes, with linear probes as its degree-1 member. We also show that a natural object for cross-model probe transfer is a shared probe-visible quotient--the representation modulo directions invisible to the probe family--rather than the full hidden state. Experiments on synthetic and real-world tasks support both predictions, showing where degree-2 probes help beyond linear ones and how quotient-based transfer enables coverage-aware monitor portability across model families. These results point toward a broader geometric representation theory of neural probing, with coverage-aware monitor transfer as a concrete operational consequence.

</details>


### [137] [Anti-Self-Distillation for Reasoning RL via Pointwise Mutual Information](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.11609)
*Guobin Shen,Xiang Cheng,Chenxiao Zhao,Lei Huang,Jindong Li,Dongcheng Zhao,Xing Yu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出AntiSD（反自蒸馏）方法，通过反转学生与教师之间的KL散度符号，解决自蒸馏在数学推理中失效的问题，在多个模型上以2-10倍更少训练步数达到GRPO基线准确率，并提升最终准确率最高11.5个百分点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自蒸馏方法在数学推理任务中效果不稳定，即使同一方法在其他领域取得成功。作者通过逐点互信息分析发现，特权上下文（如已验证的解决方案或反馈）会膨胀教师对结构连接词和可验证断言等已被解决方案隐含的token的置信度，同时降低对驱动多步搜索的思考token（如"Wait"、"Let"、"Maybe"）的置信度，导致自蒸馏在数学推理中失败。

Method: 提出Anti-Self-Distillation (AntiSD)，核心创新在于反转学生与教师之间散度的方向——即上升而非下降KL散度，从而反转每个token的梯度符号并产生天然有界优势。同时引入熵触发的门控机制，当教师熵崩塌时禁用该项，实现与默认自蒸馏的直接替换。本质上是最大化而非最小化学生与教师之间的差异，鼓励学生探索教师不自信的token。

Result: 在4B到30B参数的五个模型上，AntiSD以2-10倍更少的训练步数达到GRPO基线准确率，最终准确率提升最高达11.5个百分点。

Conclusion: AntiSD为语言模型的自举推理能力开辟了一条可扩展的自我改进路径，通过反直觉地放大而非缩小学生与教师之间的差异，更高效地提升数学推理能力。

Abstract: On-policy self-distillation, where a student is pulled toward a copy of itself conditioned on privileged context (e.g., a verified solution or feedback), offers a promising direction for advancing reasoning capability without a stronger external teacher. Yet in math reasoning the gains are inconsistent, even when the same approach succeeds elsewhere. A pointwise mutual information analysis traces the failure to the privileged context itself: it inflates the teacher's confidence on tokens already implied by the solution (structural connectives, verifiable claims) and deflates it on deliberation tokens ("Wait", "Let", "Maybe") that drive multi-step search. We propose Anti-Self-Distillation (AntiSD), which ascends a divergence between student and teacher rather than descending it: this reverses the per-token sign and yields a naturally bounded advantage in one step. An entropy-triggered gate disables the term once the teacher entropy collapses, completing a drop-in replacement for default self-distillation. Across five models from 4B to 30B parameters on math reasoning benchmarks, AntiSD reaches the GRPO baseline's accuracy in 2 to 10x fewer training steps and improves final accuracy by up to 11.5 points. AntiSD opens a path to scalable self-improvement, where a language model bootstraps its own reasoning through its training signal.

</details>


### [138] [Efficient Adjoint Matching for Fine-tuning Diffusion Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.11480)
*Jeongwoo Shin,Dongsoo Shin,Joonseok Lee,Jaewoong Choi,Jaemoo Choi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出Efficient Adjoint Matching (EAM)，通过将奖励微调重新表述为具有线性基础漂移和修正终端代价的随机最优控制问题，显著提升训练效率，收敛速度比Adjoint Matching快4倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Adjoint Matching (AM) 方法在奖励微调中计算成本高昂，原因有二：(i) 需要随机模拟完整生成轨迹，导致大量函数评估；(ii) 需要沿每条采样轨迹进行后向ODE伴随状态模拟。这两个瓶颈均源于预训练模型继承的非平凡基础漂移。

Method: EAM 将随机最优控制问题重新表述为具有线性基础漂移和相应修正的终端代价的形式。这使得训练时采样可用几步确定性ODE求解器完成，并得到闭式伴随解，从而消除后向伴随模拟。

Result: 在标准文本到图像奖励微调基准上，EAM收敛速度比AM快4倍，在PickScore、ImageReward、HPSv2.1、CLIPScore和Aesthetics等指标上达到或超越AM。

Conclusion: 通过重新设计基础漂移和终端代价，EAM有效解决了AM的计算效率瓶颈，为扩散模型和流模型的高效奖励微调提供了更实用的方案。

Abstract: Reward fine-tuning has become a common approach for aligning pretrained diffusion and flow models with human preferences in text-to-image generation. Among reward-gradient-based methods, Adjoint Matching (AM) provides a principled formulation by casting reward fine-tuning as a stochastic optimal control (SOC) problem. However, AM inevitably requires a substantial computational cost: it requires (i) stochastic simulation of full generative trajectories under memoryless dynamics, resulting in a large number of function evaluations, and (ii) backward ODE simulation of the adjoint state along each sampled trajectory. In this work, we observe that both bottlenecks are closely tied to the \textit{non-trivial base drift} inherited from the pretrained model. Motivated by this observation, we propose \textbf{Efficient Adjoint Matching (EAM)}, which substantially improves training efficiency by reformulating the SOC problem with a \textit{linear base drift} and a correspondingly modified \textit{terminal cost}. This reformulation removes both sources of inefficiency; it enables training-time sampling with a few-step deterministic ODE solver and yields a closed-form adjoint solution that eliminates backward adjoint simulation. On standard text-to-image reward fine-tuning benchmarks, EAM converges up to 4x faster than AM and matches or surpasses it across various metrics including PickScore, ImageReward, HPSv2.1, CLIPScore and Aesthetics.

</details>


### [139] [Slicing and Dicing: Configuring Optimal Mixtures of Experts](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.11689)
*Margaret Li,Sneha Kudugunta,Danielle Rothermel,Luke Zettlemoyer*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 通过对2000多次预训练实验的系统研究，发现MoE架构中专家数量和粒度是影响性能的关键因素，而共享专家、异构专家、负载均衡等其他设计选择对最终质量影响较小。性能随总MoE参数增加而持续提升，最优专家大小仅依赖于活跃参数数量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管MoE架构已成为大语言模型的标准，但许多核心设计选择（如专家数量、粒度、共享专家、负载均衡、令牌丢弃等）仅在小范围配置中被单独或成对研究，尚不清楚这些选择能否独立优化而不考虑交互作用。

Method: 对超过2000次预训练运行进行系统研究，模型总参数达6.6B，全面变化专家总数、专家维度、异构专家大小、共享专家大小和负载均衡机制。

Result: (1) 在每个活跃参数规模下，性能随总MoE参数增加持续提升，即使在128这样极端的活跃专家参数比下也成立；(2) 最优专家大小几乎不依赖于总参数数量，仅取决于活跃参数数量；(3) 共享专家、异构专家和负载均衡设置的影响远小于专家数量和粒度，但无丢弃路由能带来持续收益。

Conclusion: 提出更简单的MoE设计配方：专注于专家数量和粒度，其他选择对最终质量影响最小。

Abstract: Mixture-of-Experts (MoE) architectures have become standard in large language models, yet many of their core design choices - expert count, granularity, shared experts, load balancing, token dropping - have only been studied one or two at a time over narrow configuration ranges. It remains an open question whether these choices can be optimized independently, without considering interactions. We present the first systematic study of over 2,000 pretraining runs spanning models up to 6.6B total parameters, in which we exhaustively vary total experts, expert dimension, heterogeneous expert sizing within a single layer, shared expert size and load-balancing mechanisms. We find that at every active-parameter scale that we study, performance consistently improves with total MoE parameters even at extreme active expert parameter ratios like 128.Further, the optimal expert size is nearly invariant to total parameter count and depends only on active parameter count. Third, we see that other choices like shared experts, heterogeneous experts and load-balancing settings have small effects relative to expert count and granularity, although dropless routing yields a consistent gain. Overall, our results suggest a simpler recipe: focus on expert count and granularity, other choices have minimal effect on final quality.

</details>


### [140] [Adaptive Calibration in Non-Stationary Environments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.11490)
*Junyan Liu,Haipeng Luo,Lillian J. Ratliff*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一套自适应在线预测算法，能够在非平稳环境下自动插值i.i.d.与对抗性设定，实现校准误差随环境非平稳程度自适应变化，分别达到$\widetilde{O}(\sqrt{T}+(TC)^{\frac{1}{3}})$（$\ell_1$校准）和$\widetilde{O}((1+C)^{\frac{1}{3}})$（$\ell_2$与伪KL校准）的界，匹配平稳与完全对抗情形下的最优率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有在线预测的校准算法多假设完全对抗性环境（结果可任意），导致在更温和（如近似平稳）的场景下表现次优。作者希望设计一种能自动适应环境非平稳程度的校准算法，在i.i.d.与对抗性设定之间平滑过渡，消除"一刀切"的保守性。

Method: 提出基于epoch调度的在线校准算法，结合对预测空间的新型非均匀划分策略——在真实均值附近分配更精细的分辨率。该方法扩展了[Hu et al., 2026, Luo et al., 2025]的工作，通过引入未知非平稳度量$C$（定义为均值结果的最小$\ell_1$偏差）实现自适应。

Result: $\ell_1$校准误差达到$\widetilde{O}(\sqrt{T}+(TC)^{\frac{1}{3}})$，$\ell_2$与伪KL校准误差均达到$\widetilde{O}((1+C)^{\frac{1}{3}})$。在平稳情形（$C=0$）匹配最优率，在完全对抗情形（$C=T$）恢复已知保证。

Conclusion: 成功回答了能否设计自适应校准算法的问题，给出了肯定的答案，并提供了一套理论保证完备的算法框架，实现了从i.i.d.到对抗性环境的无缝自适应。

Abstract: Making calibrated online predictions is a central challenge in modern AI systems. Much of the existing literature focuses on fully adversarial environments where outcomes may be arbitrary, leading to conservative algorithms that can perform suboptimally in more benign settings, such as when outcomes are nearly stationary. This gap raises a natural question: can we design online prediction algorithms whose calibration error automatically adapts to the degree of non-stationarity in the environment, smoothly interpolating between i.i.d. and adversarial regimes? We answer this question in the affirmative and develop a suite of algorithms that achieve adaptive calibration guarantees under multiple calibration measures. Specifically, with $T$ being the number of rounds and $C\in[0,T]$ being an unknown non-stationary measure defined as the minimal $\ell_1$ deviation of the mean outcomes, our algorithms attain $\widetilde{O}(\sqrt{T}+(TC)^{\frac{1}{3}})$ for $\ell_1$ calibration error and $\widetilde{O}((1+C)^{\frac{1}{3}})$ for both $\ell_2$ and pseudo KL calibration error. These bounds match the optimal rates in the stationary case ($C=0$) and recover known guarantees in the fully adversarial regime ($C=T$). Our approach builds on and extends prior work [Hu et al., 2026, Luo et al., 2025], introducing an epoch-based scheduling together with a novel non-uniform partition of the prediction space that allocates finer resolution near the underlying ground truth.

</details>


### [141] [Understanding and Preventing Entropy Collapse in RLVR with On-Policy Entropy Flow Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.11491)
*Huimin Xu,Shuai Zhao,Xiaobao Wu,Anh Tuan Luu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文从token级熵流角度分析了RLVR算法中熵崩塌的原因——熵减token持续多于熵增token，并提出OPEFO（On-Policy Entropy Flow Optimization）方法，通过自适应熵流平衡机制重新缩放熵增与熵减更新，在严格on-policy条件下提升训练稳定性和数学推理性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有RLVR算法（如GRPO）广泛存在熵崩塌问题，导致过早确定性和优化不稳定；现有的熵正则化和比例裁剪等解决方案要么粗粒度控制熵，要么依赖近似on-policy训练，缺乏对熵动态平衡的深入理解。

Method: 1. 从token级熵流视角分析熵崩塌，揭示熵减token持续压倒熵增token导致熵流严重失衡；2. 提出OPEFO机制，根据每个token对熵变化的贡献自适应地重新缩放熵增和熵减更新，同时保持严格的on-policy训练。

Result: 在六个数学推理基准上，OPEFO相比基线方法在训练稳定性和最终性能上均有提升。

Conclusion: 熵流平衡是解决RLVR中熵崩塌的关键，OPEFO通过自适应token级熵流优化实现了更好的训练动态和推理能力。

Abstract: Reinforcement learning with verifiable rewards (RLVR) has become an effective paradigm for improving the reasoning ability of large language models. However, widely used RLVR algorithms, such as GRPO, often suffer from entropy collapse, leading to premature determinism and unstable optimization. Existing remedies, including entropy regularization and ratio-based clipping heuristics, either control entropy in a coarse-grained manner or rely on approximate on-policy training. In this paper, we revisit entropy collapse from a token-level entropy flow perspective. Our analysis reveals that entropy-decreasing tokens consistently outweigh entropy-increasing ones, resulting in a severely imbalanced entropy flow. This perspective provides a unified explanation of entropy collapse in existing RLVR algorithms and highlights the importance of balancing entropy dynamics. Motivated by this analysis, we propose On-Policy Entropy Flow Optimization (OPEFO), an adaptive entropy flow balancing mechanism that rescales entropy-increasing and entropy-decreasing updates according to their contributions to entropy change, while remaining strict on-policy. Experiments on six mathematical reasoning benchmarks demonstrate that OPEFO improves training stability and final performance. We will release the code and models upon publication.

</details>


### [142] [Entropy Polarity in Reinforcement Fine-Tuning: Direction, Asymmetry, and Control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.11775)
*Jiazheng Zhang,Ziche Fu,Junrui Shen,Yunbin Zhao,Yunke Zhang,Zhiheng Xi,Long Ma,Chenxin An,Zhihao Zhang,Shichun Liu,Dingwei Zhu,Shihan Dou,Shaofan Liu,Han Li,Wiggin Zhou,Aiden Adams,Tao Gui,Fei Huang,Qi Zhang,Xuanjing Huang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了熵极性（entropy polarity）概念，揭示了RLVR中token-level的熵变化机制，并基于此设计了PAPO算法，通过优势重加权自适应调节熵扩张与收缩更新，在数学推理和智能体任务上取得了显著提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有熵感知方法主要通过全局目标调节熵，但采样策略更新在token级别重塑策略熵的机制尚未被充分研究。

Method: 建立RLVR中熵力学的理论框架，推导出熵变化的一阶近似——熵极性，一种有符号的token级量，可预测采样更新是扩张还是收缩熵。基于此提出PAPO算法，保留极性分支并通过优势重加权实现熵控制，以经验熵轨迹作为在线阶段信号自适应调节优化压力。

Result: 熵极性可靠预测熵变化；正负极性分支在保留探索与强化利用中发挥互补作用；PAPO在数学推理和智能体基准测试中持续优于竞争基线，训练效率和奖励提升显著。

Conclusion: PAPO通过极性感知的策略优化，有效平衡了探索与利用，为RLVR中的熵控制提供了新的理论视角和实用方法。

Abstract: Policy entropy has emerged as a fundamental measure for understanding and controlling exploration in reinforcement learning with verifiable rewards (RLVR) for LLMs. However, existing entropy-aware methods mainly regulate entropy through global objectives, while the token-level mechanism by which sampled policy updates reshape policy entropy remains underexplored. In this work, we develop a theoretical framework of entropy mechanics in RLVR. Our analysis yields a first-order approximation of the entropy change, giving rise to entropy polarity, a signed token-level quantity that predicts how much a sampled update expands or contracts entropy. This analysis further reveals a structural asymmetry: reinforcing frequent high-probability tokens triggers contraction tendencies, whereas expansive tendencies typically require lower-probability samples or stronger distributional correction. Empirically, we show that entropy polarity reliably predicts entropy changes, and that positive and negative polarity branches play complementary roles in preserving exploration while strengthening exploitation. Building on these insights, we propose Polarity-Aware Policy Optimization (PAPO), which preserves both polarity branches and implements entropy control through advantage reweighting. With the empirical entropy trajectory as an online phase signal, PAPO adaptively reallocates optimization pressure between entropy-expanding and entropy-contracting updates. Experiments on mathematical reasoning and agentic benchmarks show that PAPO consistently outperforms competitive baselines, while delivering superior training efficiency and substantial reward improvements.

</details>


### [143] [ROMER: Expert Replacement and Router Calibration for Robust MoE LLMs on Analog Compute-in-Memory Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.11800)
*Wenyong Zhou,Yuannuo Feng,Yizhe Chen,Taiqiang Wu,Wendong Xu,Wenbo Qi,Zhengwu Liu,Wang Kang,Ngai Wong*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文首次系统研究了基于混合专家（MoE）架构的大语言模型在模拟存内计算（CIM）噪声环境下的表现，发现硬件噪声会严重破坏专家负载平衡并导致路由决策失效。为此提出后训练校准框架ROMER，通过替换欠激活专家和基于百分位数归一化重校准路由logits，在真实芯片噪声条件下将DeepSeek-MoE、Qwen-MoE和OLMoE的困惑度分别降低高达58.6%、58.8%和59.8%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: MoE架构的LLM在推理时存在频繁专家切换带来的内存带宽瓶颈，模拟CIM架构有望缓解该问题，但模拟CIM系统存在固有的硬件缺陷会扰动存储权重，而硬件噪声对基于MoE的LLM在噪声CIM环境中的负面影响尚未被探索。

Method: 提出ROMER后训练校准框架，包含两个核心步骤：(1) 将欠激活专家替换为高频专家以恢复负载平衡；(2) 通过基于百分位数归一化重校准路由器logits，以在噪声下稳定路由决策。

Result: 在真实芯片噪声条件下，ROMER在DeepSeek-MoE、Qwen-MoE和OLMoE上分别实现了高达58.6%、58.8%和59.8%的困惑度降低，验证了其在多种MoE架构中的有效性和泛化能力。

Conclusion: 硬件噪声会严重破坏MoE专家负载平衡并导致路由决策失效，ROMER通过专家替换和logits重校准能有效缓解该问题，为MoE模型在模拟CIM硬件上的部署提供了可行的后训练解决方案。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) with mixture-of-experts (MoE) architectures achieve remarkable scalability by sparsely activating a subset of experts per token, yet their frequent expert switching creates memory bandwidth bottlenecks that compute-in-memory (CIM) architectures are well-suited to mitigate. However, analog CIM systems suffer from inherent hardware imperfections that perturb stored weights, and its negative impact on MoE-based LLMs in noisy CIM environments remains unexplored. In this work, we present the first systematic investigation of MoE-based LLMs under noise model calibrated with real chip measurements, revealing that hardware noise critically disrupts expert load balance and renders clean-trained routing decisions consistently suboptimal. Based on these findings, we propose ROMER, a post-training calibration framework that (1) replaces underactivated experts with high-frequency ones to restore load balance, and (2) recalibrates router logits via percentile-based normalization to stabilize routing under noise. Extensive experiments across multiple benchmarks demonstrate that ROMER achieves up to 58.6\%, 58.8\%, and 59.8\% reduction in perplexity under real-chip noise conditions for DeepSeek-MoE, Qwen-MoE, and OLMoE, respectively, establishing its effectiveness and generalizability across diverse MoE architectures.

</details>


### [144] [EqOD: Symmetry-Informed Stability Selection for PDE Identification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.11524)
*Gnankan Landry Regis N'guessan,Bum Jun Kim*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出Equivariant Operator Discovery (EqOD)，一种结合伽利略对称性约简和随机LASSO稳定性选择的自动PDE识别方法，在多个基准测试中显著优于现有方法（如PySINDy、WSINDy），尤其在噪声条件下达到F1=1.000的完美性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于稀疏回归的PDE识别方法面临候选库规模的两难困境：库过大易在观测噪声下产生假阳性，库过小易遗漏真实项。需要一种自动化的库约简机制以平衡表达能力和鲁棒性。

Method: EqOD采用双路径自动约简机制：(1) 通过弱形式结构检验检测轨迹数据中的伽利略不变性，若成立则使用对称约简库（基于伽利略排除定理证明某些项必然不存在）；(2) 否则应用随机LASSO稳定性选择，结合经典假阳性界进行指导；(3) 设置基于残差的回退机制，防止性能低于全库基线。

Result: 在8个PDE的4种噪声水平下，EqOD在20%噪声的热方程上达到F1=1.000±0.000，而WF-LASSO为0.475±0.181，PySINDy 2.0为0.000，WSINDy为0.789。严格准则下EqOD赢得32个cell中的7个，WF-LASSO为0，其余为平局。EqOD在23/32个cell中优于PySINDy。在WeakIdent和PINN-SR外部数据集上，5个干净基准均达F1=1.000。

Conclusion: EqOD通过对称性约简和稳定性选择的结合，有效解决了PDE识别中库规模与噪声鲁棒性的矛盾。伽利略库约简在显式自洽性和库假设下得到理论保证，而稳定性选择步骤由经典假阳性界驱动。对相关PDE设计矩阵的正式保证仍属开放问题。

Abstract: Data-driven identification of partial differential equations (PDEs) relies on sparse regression over a candidate library of differential operators, where larger libraries inflate false positives under observation noise and smaller libraries risk missing true terms. We introduce Equivariant Operator Discovery (EqOD), a fully automatic method combining two library reduction mechanisms. When Galilean invariance is detected from trajectory data via a weak-form structural test, EqOD uses the symmetry-reduced library, eliminating terms that our Galilean exclusion result proves to be absent from the governing equation. Otherwise, it applies randomized LASSO stability selection guided by classical false-positive bounds. A residual-based fallback prevents degradation below the full-library baseline. On 8 PDEs at 4 noise levels, EqOD attains $F_1 = 1.000 \pm 0.000$ on Heat at $20\%$ noise, where WF-LASSO obtains $0.475 \pm 0.181$, official PySINDy 2.0 obtains $0.000$, and the WSINDy reimplementation obtains $0.789$. Under the strict criterion that the mean F1 difference exceeds the larger of the two standard deviations, EqOD wins 7 of 32 cells. WF-LASSO wins none, and the remaining 25 cells are ties. Across all 32 cells, EqOD outperforms PySINDy 2.0.0 in 23 of 32 cells, and all 5 PySINDy wins occur on reaction PDEs. External validation on WeakIdent and PINN-SR datasets gives $F_1 = 1.000$ on all 5 clean benchmarks. NLS, 2D, coupled-system, and cylinder-wake extensions are reported. The Galilean library reduction is proved under explicit autonomy and library assumptions. The stability-selection step is motivated by classical false-positive bounds, while formal guarantees for correlated PDE design matrices remain open.

</details>


### [145] [More Edits, More Stable: Understanding the Lifelong Normalization in Sequential Model Editing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.11836)
*Xin Ma,Wei Chen,Qi Liu,Derong Xu,Zhi Zheng,Tong Xu,Enhong Chen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文首次从理论上揭示了终身模型编辑中"终身归一化(LN)"机制的工作原理，证明了LN通过自强化稳定环与岭正则化结合产生渐近正交且有界范数的参数更新，从而缓解灾难性遗忘和模型崩溃。基于此提出StableEdit方法，通过显式预热阶段和完全白化增强稳定性，实验验证了理论的正确性和方法的有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 终身模型编辑面临灾难性遗忘和模型崩溃的严重问题。虽然实证发现"终身归一化(LN)"策略对长期稳定性至关重要，但LN的机制一直是一个"黑箱"，缺乏理论理解，这阻碍了更优编辑方法的进一步发展。

Method: 首先从理论上分析LN在终身编辑中的作用，揭示其形成自强化稳定环的机制，并证明LN与岭正则化回归结合时，参数更新具有渐近正交性和有界范数。基于理论洞见，提出StableEdit方法，通过显式预热阶段和完全白化（full whitening）来增强这一稳定环。

Result: 理论分析证明了LN通过自强化稳定环缓解遗忘和崩溃的机制；实验验证了理论预测的正确性，并展示了StableEdit在长期稳定性上的竞争优势。

Conclusion: 首次为终身模型编辑中的归一化策略提供了严格的理论基础，揭示了LN的稳定机制，并据此设计出更优的StableEdit方法，为终身模型编辑领域提供了理论和实践指导。

Abstract: Lifelong Model Editing aims to continuously update evolving facts in Large Language Models while preserving unrelated knowledge and general capabilities, yet it remains plagued by catastrophic forgetting and model collapse. Empirically, we find that recent editors resilient over long horizons share the same core strategy: Lifelong Normalization (LN), which normalizes value gradients using running statistics. Removing LN causes immediate performance collapse, and we observe a counter-intuitive positive cumulative effect where early edits can promote the success of future edits. Yet the mechanism of LN remains a "black box", leaving its precise role in lifelong stability poorly understood. In this work, we provide the first theoretical account of LN in the lifelong regime. Our analysis reveals a self-reinforcing stability loop and proves that, when combined with ridge-regularized regression, LN yields parameter updates with asymptotic orthogonality and bounded norms, directly mitigating forgetting and systemic collapse. Based on these insights, we derive StableEdit, which strengthens this stability loop via an explicit warm-up stage and full whitening, improving long-horizon stability at minimal overhead. Extensive experiments validate our theory and demonstrate competitive performance. Our code is available at https://github.com/MINE-USTC/StableEdit.

</details>


### [146] [OverNaN: NaN-Aware Oversampling for Imbalanced Learning with Meaningful Missingness](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.11525)
*Amanda S Barnard*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: OverNaN是一种轻量级的、对NaN值敏感的过采样框架，用于处理类不平衡问题，同时保留缺失值的结构信息，避免传统插补或删除方法带来的偏差。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统方法将缺失值视为缺陷并通过删除或插补消除，但缺失值本身可能携带重要信息，反映实验约束、测量选择或数据生成机制。消除或掩盖这些结构会扭曲分类边界、引入偏差并降低泛化能力，尤其在少数类本已不足的不平衡数据集中问题更为严重。

Method: OverNaN扩展了常见的合成过采样方法，使其能直接在不完整的特征向量上操作，允许根据明确定义的策略保留、传播或选择性插补缺失值，将缺失性作为特征空间的一部分用于生成合成样本。

Result: 通过软件附带的代表性示例证明，在有意义的缺失模式可以在过采样过程中被保留，而不会引入人为的确定性。

Conclusion: OverNaN适用于科学和工程领域中处理小型、不完整且不平衡的数据集，在这些领域中缺失值不可避免且往往包含信息。

Abstract: Missing values are routinely treated as defects to be eliminated through deletion or imputation prior to machine learning. In many applied domains, however, missingness itself carries information, reflecting experimental constraints, measurement choices, or systematic mechanisms tied to the data-generating process. Eliminating or masking this structure can distort class boundaries, introduce bias, and reduce generalisability; particularly in imbalanced datasets where minority classes are already under-represented. OverNaN is a lightweight, NaN-aware oversampling framework designed to address class imbalance without erasing missingness structure. It extends common synthetic oversampling methods to operate directly on incomplete feature vectors, allowing missing values to be preserved, propagated, or selectively interpolated according to explicitly defined strategies. Rather than repairing missing data, OverNaN treats missingness as part of the feature space over which synthetic samples are generated. This paper situates OverNaN within the broader landscape of imbalanced learning, missing-data handling, and NaN-tolerant algorithms. Using representative examples included with the software, we demonstrate that meaningful missingness can be retained during oversampling without introducing artificial certainty. OverNaN is intended for practitioners working with small, incomplete, and imbalanced datasets in scientific and engineering domains where missingness is unavoidable and often informative.

</details>


### [147] [GEAR: Granularity-Adaptive Advantage Reweighting for LLM Agents via Self-Distillation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.11853)
*Sijia Li,Yuchen Huang,Zifan Liu,Yanping Li,Jingjing Fu,Li Zhao,Jiang Bian,Ling Zhang,Jun Zhang,Rui Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: GEAR是一种自适应粒度的信用分配框架，通过从自蒸馏中获取的token级和片段级信号重塑GRPO优势函数，在数学推理和智能体工具使用基准上显著优于标准GRPO等方法，尤其对长程困难任务提升达20%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 强化学习作为LLM智能体的常用后训练方法，通常依赖粗粒度的结果级奖励信号，导致信用分配不精确。细粒度信用分配虽具潜力，但如何获取可靠的局部信用并将其正确分配给长程轨迹的对应部分仍是一个开放挑战。

Method: 提出GEAR框架，通过自蒸馏比较on-policy学生模型与ground-truth条件教师模型，获得参考引导的散度信号。该散度在语义偏离起始点出现尖峰，后续token可能回归低散度。GEAR将散度尖峰作为自适应信用区域的锚点：学生与教师对齐时保留token级精度；偏离时将对应续编区域分组为自适应片段，并用偏离点散度调节片段优势。

Result: 在Qwen3 4B和8B模型上，涵盖8个数学推理和智能体工具使用基准的实验表明，GEAR一致优于标准GRPO、仅自蒸馏基线以及token级或turn级信用分配方法。在GRPO基线准确率较低的基准上提升尤为显著，相对GRPO提升最高约20%。

Conclusion: GEAR通过自适应粒度的信用分配有效解决了长程轨迹中细粒度信用分配的难题，尤其在更具挑战性的长程任务中展现出显著优势，验证了所提自适应重加权方案的有效性。

Abstract: Reinforcement learning has become a widely used post-training approach for LLM agents, where training commonly relies on outcome-level rewards that provide only coarse supervision. While finer-grained credit assignment is promising for effective policy updates, obtaining reliable local credit and assigning it to the right parts of the long-horizon trajectory remains an open challenge. In this paper, we propose Granularity-adaptivE Advantage Reweighting (GEAR), an adaptive-granularity credit assignment framework that reshapes the trajectory-level GRPO advantage using token- and segment-level signals derived from self-distillation. GEAR compares an on-policy student with a ground-truth-conditioned teacher to obtain a reference-guided divergence signal for identifying adaptive segment boundaries and modulating local advantage weights. This divergence often spikes at the onset of a semantic deviation, while later tokens in the same autoregressive continuation may return to low divergence. GEAR therefore treats such spikes as anchors for adaptive credit regions: where the student remains aligned with the teacher, token-level resolution is preserved; where it departs, GEAR groups the corresponding continuation into an adaptive segment and uses the divergence at the departure point to modulate the segment' s advantage. Experiments across eight mathematical reasoning and agentic tool-use benchmarks with Qwen3 4B and 8B models show that GEAR consistently outperforms standard GRPO, self-distillation-only baselines, and token- or turn-level credit-assignment methods. The gains are especially strong on benchmarks with lower GRPO baseline accuracy, reaching up to around 20\% over GRPO, suggesting that the proposed adaptive reweighting scheme is especially useful in more challenging long-horizon settings.

</details>


### [148] [FERMI: Exploiting Relations for Membership Inference Against Tabular Diffusion Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.11527)
*Abtin Mahyar,Masoumeh Shafieinejad,Yuhan Liu,Xi He*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 现有成员推断攻击仅适用于单表场景，忽略了真实数据的关系结构。本文提出FERMI方法，利用目标表关联的父表等辅助信息增强特征，在推理时仅需目标记录属性值，实现了在多表关系数据上的成员推断攻击，在三种表格扩散模型和三个真实数据集上，TPR@0.1FPR最高提升53%（白盒）和22%（黑盒）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 扩散模型被广泛用于表格数据合成和敏感记录共享，但其隐私保护能力存疑。现有成员推断攻击仅针对单表场景，忽略了真实敏感数据中普遍存在的多表关系结构，无法有效评估多表环境下的隐私风险。

Method: 提出FERMI方法，在训练攻击模型时利用目标表关联的父表等辅助信息，将关系成员信号与单表特征进行特征映射与融合；推理时攻击者仅需观察目标表中目标记录的属性值即可进行成员推断。

Result: 在三种表格扩散架构和三个真实关系数据集上，FERMI相比单表基线持续提升攻击性能：白盒设置下TPR@0.1FPR提升高达53%，黑盒设置下提升22%。

Conclusion: FERMI有效解决了多表关系场景下成员推断攻击的挑战，证明了利用关系辅助信息可以显著提升隐私风险评估能力，揭示了现有扩散模型在多表设置下隐私保护的脆弱性。

Abstract: Diffusion models are the leading approach for tabular data synthesis and are increasingly used to share sensitive records. Whether they actually protect privacy has become a pressing question. Membership inference attacks are the standard tool for this purpose, yet existing attacks assume a single-table setting and ignore the multi-relational structure of real sensitive data. A core challenge in assessing privacy risks from membership inference attacks in multi-table settings is how to leverage auxiliary information from relations associated with the target table, such as its parent tables. Particularly, we study a practical setting in which such auxiliary information is available only when training the attack model. At inference time, the attacker observes only the attribute values of the target record from the target table. We propose FERMI (FEature-mapping for Relational Membership Inference), which resolves this gap by enriching single-table features with relational membership signal. Across three tabular diffusion architectures and three real-world relational datasets, FERMI consistently improves attack performance over single-table baselines, with TPR@$0.1$FPR rising by up to 53% over the single-table baseline in the white-box setting and 22% in the black-box setting.

</details>


### [149] [Not How Many, But Which: Parameter Placement in Low-Rank Adaptation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.12207)
*Arijit Sehanobish,Charles Lovering*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究了LoRA适配器中B矩阵的参数放置问题：在给定k个可训练条目预算下，不同选择策略对性能的影响。发现SFT下随机与知情选择效果相当，但GRPO下随机选择无法提升基础模型，而梯度知情选择可恢复标准LoRA精度。这种差异源于梯度结构：SFT梯度低秩且方向稳定，GRPO梯度高秩且跨步正交。所提评分方法可在10秒内、以不到0.5%训练成本识别关键参数，这些参数集中在残差流写入投影（V、O、Down）上，且跨模型族和规模（1.5B-8B）保持稳定。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究在LoRA适配器中，给定固定预算的可训练参数时，参数位置的选择是否会影响模型微调性能。

Method: 对比SFT和GRPO两种训练范式下随机参数选择与梯度知情参数选择的性能差异，分析梯度结构特征，提出高效的评分方法来识别关键参数。

Result: SFT下随机与知情选择性能相当；GRPO下随机选择无效，梯度知情选择恢复标准LoRA精度。关键参数集中在残差流写入投影（V、O、Down），跨模型族和规模稳定。

Conclusion: 参数放置问题存在训练范式依赖性，GRPO训练必须采用梯度知情的参数选择策略，所提方法可极低成本高效识别关键参数。

Abstract: We study the \textit{parameter placement problem}: given a fixed budget of $k$ trainable entries within the B matrix of a LoRA adapter (A frozen), does the choice of which $k$ matter? Under supervised fine-tuning, random and informed subsets achieve comparable performance. Under GRPO on base models, random placement fails to improve over the base model, while gradient-informed placement recovers standard LoRA accuracy. This regime dependence traces to gradient structure: SFT gradients are low-rank and directionally stable, so any subset accumulates coherent updates; GRPO gradients are high-rank and near-orthogonal across steps, so only elements with consistently signed gradients retain the learning signal. Our scoring procedure identifies these critical parameters in under 10 seconds at less than 0.5% of training cost.
