Selected parameters concentrate on residual-stream-writing projections (V, O, Down), stable across model families and scales (1.5B - 8B).

</details>


### [150] [Primal-Dual Policy Optimization for Linear CMDPs with Adversarial Losses](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.11535)
*Kihyun Yu,Seoungbin Bae,Dabeen Lee*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 针对在线有限时域对抗性线性CMDP问题，提出首个达到次线性遗憾和约束违背后界的原始-对偶策略优化算法，遗憾和约束违背均为$\widetilde{\mathcal{O}}(K^{3/4})$。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有线性CMDP工作主要针对固定或固定分布下的随机损失/成本，无法应对对抗性变化的动态环境。

Method: 提出原始-对偶策略优化算法，引入加权LogSumExp softmax策略类，结合周期性策略混合和正则化对偶更新两个新组件，并给出新的覆盖数论证。

Result: 算法在对抗性损失（全信息反馈）和随机成本（bandit反馈）设置下，实现了$\widetilde{\mathcal{O}}(K^{3/4})$的遗憾和约束违背上界。

Conclusion: 该算法是首个在此对抗性线性CMDP设定下取得理论保证的方法，数值实验验证了理论结果。

Abstract: Existing work on linear constrained Markov decision processes (CMDPs) has primarily focused on stochastic settings, where the losses and costs are either fixed or drawn from fixed distributions. However, such formulations are inherently vulnerable to adversarially changing environments. To overcome this limitation, we propose a primal-dual policy optimization algorithm for online finite-horizon {adversarial} linear CMDPs, where the losses are adversarially chosen under full-information feedback and the costs are stochastic under bandit feedback. Our algorithm is the \emph{first} to achieve sublinear regret and constraint violation bounds in this setting, both bounded by $\widetilde{\mathcal{O}}(K^{3/4})$, where $K$ denotes the number of episodes. The algorithm introduces and runs with a new class of policies, which we call weighted LogSumExp softmax policies, designed to adapt to adversarially chosen loss functions. Our main result stems from the following key contributions: (i) a new covering number argument for the weighted LogSumExp softmax policies, and (ii) two novel algorithmic components -- periodic policy mixing and a regularized dual update -- which allow us to effectively control both the covering number and the dual variable. We also report numerical results that validate our theoretical findings on the performance of the algorithm.

</details>


### [151] [Predicting Decisions of AI Agents from Limited Interaction through Text-Tabular Modeling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.12411)
*Eilam Shapira,Moshe Tennenholtz,Roi Reichart*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究AI智能体在与未知对手进行自然语言谈判时，如何通过少量交互预测对手的下一步决策。作者将问题建模为目标自适应文本-表格预测任务，提出LLM-as-Observer方法，利用冻结LLM的隐藏状态作为决策特征，在91个未见过的对手智能体上验证了方法的有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现实场景中AI智能体需要与陌生对手进行谈判交易，但对手的LLM、提示词、控制逻辑和规则回退机制均不可见，同时每个决策都可能带来金钱后果。因此需要研究如何从少量交互中预测陌生对手的下一步决策。

Method: 将问题形式化为目标自适应文本-表格预测任务：每个决策点为一个表格行，包含结构化游戏状态、报价历史和对话文本；在提示中提供K个同一对手之前的游戏作为标注示例。模型基于表格基础模型，使用游戏状态特征和基于LLM的文本表示构建行表示，并引入LLM-as-Observer：一个小型冻结LLM读取决策时刻的状态和对话，丢弃其输出，使用其隐藏状态作为决策导向特征。

Result: 在13个前沿LLM智能体上训练，在91个未见过的支架智能体上测试，完整模型优于直接LLM-as-Predictor提示和游戏+文本特征基线。在K=16时，Observer特征在两项任务上使响应预测AUC提升约4个点，报价预测误差降低14%。

Conclusion: 将对手预测建模为目标自适应文本-表格任务能够实现有效的适应，隐藏的LLM表示能够暴露直接提示无法获取的决策相关信号。

Abstract: AI agents negotiate and transact in natural language with unfamiliar counterparts: a buyer bot facing an unknown seller, or a procurement assistant negotiating with a supplier. In such interactions, the counterpart's LLM, prompts, control logic, and rule-based fallbacks are hidden, while each decision can have monetary consequences. We ask whether an agent can predict an unfamiliar counterpart's next decision from a few interactions. To avoid real-world logging confounds, we study this problem in controlled bargaining and negotiation games, formulating it as target-adaptive text-tabular prediction: each decision point is a table row combining structured game state, offer history, and dialogue, while $K$ previous games of the same target agent, i.e., the counterpart being modeled, are provided in the prompt as labeled adaptation examples. Our model is built on a tabular foundation model that represents rows using game-state features and LLM-based text representations, and adds LLM-as-Observer as an additional representation: a small frozen LLM reads the decision-time state and dialogue; its answer is discarded, and its hidden state becomes a decision-oriented feature, making the LLM an encoder rather than a direct few-shot predictor. Training on 13 frontier-LLM agents and testing on 91 held-out scaffolded agents, the full model outperforms direct LLM-as-Predictor prompting and game+text features baselines. Within this tabular model, Observer features contribute beyond the other feature schemes: at $K=16$, they improve response-prediction AUC by about 4 points across both tasks and reduce bargaining offer-prediction error by 14%. These results show that formulating counterpart prediction as a target-adaptive text-tabular task enables effective adaptation, and that hidden LLM representations expose decision-relevant signals that direct prompting does not surface.

</details>


### [152] [Fast MoE Inference via Predictive Prefetching and Expert Replication](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.11537)
*Ankit Jyothish,Ali Jannesari,Aishwarya Sarkar,Joseph Zuber*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种动态专家复制策略，通过预测过载专家并进行复制，使多个token能同时被处理，从而提升MoE推理的GPU利用率、减少负载不均和延迟，在Switch-base-128/256上实现近100% GPU利用率及最高3倍推理加速，同时保持约90-95%的基线性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: MoE推理存在GPU利用率低、负载不均衡和延迟高的问题，根源在于专家激活的稀疏性导致多个token等待同一专家处理。

Method: 提出动态专家复制策略：预测即将过载的专家，在后续批次中复制该专家，使复制后的专家能并行处理不同层的批量token，从而提升并行度、减少GPU空闲时间。

Result: 在大规模MoE模型（Switch-base-128和Switch-base-256）上验证，实现近乎完全的GPU利用率（约100%），推理速度最高提升3倍，同时保留基线架构约90-95%的性能。

Conclusion: 动态专家复制策略能有效缓解MoE推理中的负载不均和延迟问题，在保持模型性能的前提下大幅提升推理效率。

Abstract: The Mixture of Experts (MoE) architecture has become a fundamental building block in state-of-the-art large language models (LLMs), improving domain-specific expertise in LLMs and scaling model capacity without proportionally increasing their computational overhead. However, MoE inference often suffers from suboptimal GPU utilization, load imbalance, and elevated latency arising from multiple tokens waiting on the same experts for their computation which arises from sparsity of expert activation. To address these challenges, we propose a dynamic expert replication strategy that predicts which experts are likely to be overloaded and replicates them for upcoming batches of tokens. The replicated experts process batch tokens concurrently across layers, which leads to improved parallelism, shorter GPU idle time, and significantly faster inference. Experimental evaluations conducted on large-scale MoE models, including Switch-base-128 and Switch-base-256, demonstrate that our method achieves near-complete GPU utilization (approx 100%), leading to upto 3x improvement in inference speed while preserving approximately 90-95% of the performance of baseline architectures

</details>


### [153] [ORCE: Order-Aware Alignment of Verbalized Confidence in Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.12446)
*Chen Li,Xiaoling Hu,Songzhu Zheng,Jiawei Zhou,Chao Chen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出一种解耦且有序的框架用于语言模型口头置信度校准：先生成答案，再基于固定问答对估计置信度，通过采样替代和排序强化学习优化置信度排序，在保持答案准确性的同时提升校准与失败预测性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有口头置信度方法将答案生成与置信度生成联合优化，导致置信度对齐目标干扰答案准确性，需要一种解耦方案来独立优化置信度而不影响答案质量。

Method: 1) 解耦框架：先生成答案，再基于固定问答对估计置信度；2) 采样替代：从多次模型完成中构建正确性似然的替代信号；3) 排序强化学习：优化基于排名的强化学习目标，使正确性似然更高的响应获得更高的口头置信度。

Result: 在推理和知识密集型基准上，该方法改善了校准和失败预测性能，同时基本保持了答案准确性。

Conclusion: 口头置信度可以通过解耦置信度估计与答案生成，并优化响应间置信度的相对排序来更可靠地对齐。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) often produce answers with high certainty even when they are incorrect, making reliable confidence estimation essential for deployment in real-world scenarios. Verbalized confidence, where models explicitly state their confidence in natural language, provides a flexible and user-facing uncertainty signal that can be applied even when token logits are unavailable. However, existing verbalized-confidence methods often optimize answer generation and confidence generation jointly, which can cause confidence-alignment objectives to interfere with answer accuracy. In this work, we propose a decoupled and order-aware framework for verbalized confidence calibration. Our method first generates an answer and then estimates confidence conditioned on the fixed question--answer pair, allowing confidence optimization without directly perturbing the answer-generation process. To align confidence with correctness likelihood, we construct a sampling-based surrogate from multiple model completions and optimize rank-based reinforcement learning objectives that encourage responses with higher estimated correctness likelihood to receive higher verbalized confidence. Experiments on reasoning and knowledge-intensive benchmarks show that our method improves calibration and failure prediction performance while largely preserving answer accuracy. These results demonstrate that verbalized confidence can be more reliably aligned by decoupling confidence estimation from answer generation and optimizing the relative ordering of confidence across responses.

</details>


### [154] [Multi-Stream LLMs: Unblocking Language Models with Parallel Streams of Thoughts, Inputs and Outputs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.12460)
*Guinan Su,Yanwu Yang,Xueyan Li,Jonas Geiping*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出将大语言模型从传统的单流序列对话格式转变为多流并行计算架构，让模型同时处理多个输入和输出流，从而解决现有聊天模型在并行行动、思考和响应方面的瓶颈问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于ChatGPT等指令微调模型的AI智能体受限于单流消息交换格式，导致模型无法在生成输出的同时进行阅读，无法在写作时接收新信息，也无法在思考的同时行动或阅读，存在严重的计算瓶颈。

Method: 将传统的单流序列消息格式的指令微调转变为支持多个并行计算流的指令微调，将每个角色（用户、系统、工具、模型自身）拆分为独立的流。每个前向传播中，语言模型同时从多个输入流读取并在多个输出流生成token，所有流之间存在因果依赖关系。

Result: 多流并行架构能够解除传统单流模型的限制，提升模型效率（通过并行化）、改善安全性（通过更好的关注点分离）、增强可监控性。

Conclusion: 这种数据驱动的架构变革可以有效解决现有AI智能体的核心瓶颈，为下一代自主智能体系统提供更高效、更安全、更可监控的基础架构。

Abstract: The continued improvements in language model capability have unlocked their widespread use as drivers of autonomous agents, for example in coding or computer use applications. However, the core of these systems has not changed much since early instruction-tuned models like ChatGPT. Even advanced AI agents function on message exchange formats, successively exchanging messages with users, systems, with itself (i.e. chain-of-thought) and tools in a single stream of computation. This bottleneck to a single stream in chat models leads to a number of limitations: the agent cannot act (generate output) while reading, and in reverse, cannot react to new information while writing. Similarly, the agent cannot act while thinking and cannot think while reading or acting on information.
  In this work, we show that models can be unblocked by switching from instruction-tuning for sequential message formats to instruction-tuning for multiple, parallel streams of computation, splitting each role into a separate stream. Every forward pass of the language model then simultaneously reads from multiple input streams and generates tokens in multiple output streams, all of which causally depend on earlier timesteps. We argue that this data-driven change remedies a number of usability limitations as outlined above, improves model efficiency through parallelization, improves model security through better separation of concerns and can further improve model monitorability.

</details>


### [155] [Solve the Loop: Attractor Models for Language and Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.12466)
*Jacob Fein-Ashley,Paria Rashidinejad*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出吸引子模型（Attractor Models），通过隐式微分求解不动点实现迭代精炼，训练内存与深度解耦，推理时可自适应选择迭代次数。在语言建模中实现帕累托改进（困惑度降低46.6%，下游任务提升19.7%），770M模型超越1.3B标准Transformer；在推理任务中27M模型在Sudoku/Maze上达91.4%/93.1%准确率，超越Claude和GPT o3。还发现不动点训练使模型内化平衡态，推理时可移除求解器。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 循环Transformer存在训练不稳定、优化部署成本高、固定小深度等局限性。为克服这些问题，需要一种能自适应深度、训练内存恒定的迭代精炼机制。

Method: 提出吸引子模型：主干模块首先生成输出嵌入，吸引子模块通过隐式微分求解不动点进行精炼。训练内存与有效深度解耦，迭代次数由收敛性自适应决定。

Result: 1) 语言建模：帕累托改进，困惑度降低46.6%，下游准确率提升19.7%，训练成本降低。770M模型超越1.3B Transformer。2) 推理任务：27M模型在Sudoku-Extreme达91.4%，Maze-Hard达93.1%，超越Claude和GPT o3。3) 发现平衡态内化现象，推理时可移除求解器。

Conclusion: 吸引子模型通过将循环转化为可内化的计算，使迭代精炼变得可扩展，在效率和性能上均优于现有方法。

Abstract: Looped Transformers offer a promising alternative to purely feed-forward computation by iteratively refining latent representations, improving language modeling and reasoning. Yet recurrent architectures remain unstable to train, costly to optimize and deploy, and constrained to small, fixed recurrence depths. We introduce Attractor Models, in which a backbone module first proposes output embeddings, then an attractor module refines them by solving for the fixed point, with gradients obtained through implicit differentiation. Thus, training memory remains constant in effective depth, and iterations are chosen adaptively by convergence. Empirically, Attractor Models outperform existing models across two regimes, large-scale language-model pretraining and reasoning with tiny models. In language modeling, Attractor Models deliver a Pareto improvement over standard Transformers and stable looped models across sizes, improving perplexity by up to 46.6% and downstream accuracy by up to 19.7% while reducing training cost. Notably, a 770M Attractor Model outperforms a 1.3B Transformer trained on twice as many tokens. On challenging reasoning tasks, we show that our model with only 27M parameters and approximately 1000 examples achieves 91.4% accuracy on Sudoku-Extreme and 93.1% on Maze-Hard, scaling favorably where frontier models like Claude and GPT o3, fail completely, and specialized recursive reasoners collapse at larger sizes. Lastly, we show that Attractor Models exhibit a novel phenomenon, which we call equilibrium internalization: fixed-point training places the model's initial output embedding near equilibrium, allowing the solver to be removed at inference time with little degradation. Together, these results suggest that Attractor Models make iterative refinement scalable by turning recurrence into a computation the model can learn to internalize.

</details>


### [156] [KV-Fold: One-Step KV-Cache Recurrence for Long-Context Inference](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.12471)
*Alireza Nadali,Patrick Cooper,Ashutosh Trivedi,Alvaro Velasquez*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: KV-Fold提出了一种无需训练的长上下文推理协议，将KV缓存视为序列块上的fold累加器，通过重复的块到块递归实现稳定且精确的长距离信息检索，在128K上下文和511层链深度下实现100%精确匹配检索，且仅需单张40GB GPU。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有长上下文推理方法面临内存瓶颈或需要模型架构修改与重新训练，缺乏一种简单、无需训练且能保持长期信息完整性的高效方案。

Method: 借鉴函数式编程中foldl的概念，将KV缓存作为左折叠累加器：模型逐块处理序列，每次基于累积的KV缓存处理新块，并将新产生的键值追加到缓存中向前传递；利用KV缓存拼接原语实现块间递归，无需修改或重新训练模型。

Result: 在Llama-3.1-8B上，152次实验、16K-128K上下文、链深度达511的条件下实现100%精确匹配检索；每步漂移短暂上升后稳定在平坦平台，对数值精度变化（10,000倍范围）不敏感，跨块大小和模型族保持一致。

Conclusion: 冻结的预训练Transformer已经天然支持稳定的KV缓存递归形式，无需架构修改或训练即可实现实用的长上下文推理。

Abstract: We introduce KV-Fold, a simple, training-free long-context inference protocol that treats the key-value (KV) cache as the accumulator in a left fold over sequence chunks. At each step, the model processes the next chunk conditioned on the accumulated cache, appends the newly produced keys and values, and passes the enlarged cache forward; the same one-step update is applied repeatedly, analogous to foldl in functional programming. Building on the KV cache concatenation primitive introduced for latent multi-agent communication, we repurpose it as a chunk-to-chunk recurrence for long-context inference. When processing chunk t, the model attends to the KV cache carried from earlier chunks as a prefix, reusing its internal state across segments without modifying or retraining the model. Despite its simplicity, the induced recurrence is stable: per-step drift rises briefly and then saturates into a flat plateau that persists across deep chains. This plateau is insensitive to a 10,000x change in numerical precision, robust across chunk sizes, and consistent across model families. At the task level, KV-Fold preserves exact information over long distances. On a needle-in-a-haystack benchmark, it achieves 100% exact-match retrieval across 152 trials spanning contexts from 16K to 128K tokens and chain depths up to 511 on Llama-3.1-8B, while remaining within the memory limits of a single 40GB GPU. Compared to streaming methods, which trade fidelity for bounded memory, KV-Fold maintains long-range retrieval while operating as a sequence of tractable forward passes. Overall, our results show that frozen pretrained transformers already support a stable form of KV-cache recurrence, providing a practical route to long-context inference without architectural changes or training.

</details>


### [157] [FedOUI: OUI-Guided Client Weighting for Federated Aggregation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.11571)
*Alberto Fernández-Hernández,Jose I. Mestre,Cristian Pérez-Corral,Manuel F. Dolz,Jose Duato,Enrique S. Quintana-Ortí*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出FedOUI，一种基于过拟合-欠拟合指标（OUI）的联邦学习聚合规则，利用客户端模型内部激活结构信息进行加权聚合，在强非独立同分布和噪声客户端条件下优于FedAvg、FedProx等方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统联邦学习聚合方法仅依赖数据集大小或梯度级别的信息，忽略了客户端模型内部如何组织其输入空间的结构信号，这些内部信号可能反映客户端训练状态的健康程度。

Method: FedOUI的核心是基于激活值且无需标签的OUI指标。每个客户端在固定探针批次上计算OUI值并随本地更新一起发送；服务器估计每轮的OUI分布，通过平滑重加权规则对结构异常客户端分配较低权重。

Result: 在CIFAR-10上强非独立同分布和噪声客户端条件下评估，FedOUI在强异质性场景下表现出最显著的增益，提高了聚合质量，且保持轻量和可解释性。

Conclusion: 内部激活结构能够提供超越客户端规模和梯度几何的有用信息，用于改进联邦聚合。

Abstract: Federated learning usually aggregates client updates using dataset size or gradient-level criteria, while overlooking internal signals about how each client model is organizing its input space during training. We introduce FedOUI, a simple aggregation rule based on the Overfitting-Underfitting Indicator (OUI), an activation-based and label-free metric. Each participating client sends its local update together with a OUI value computed on a fixed probe batch, and the server estimates the round-wise OUI distribution to assign lower weights to structurally atypical clients through a smooth reweighting rule. We evaluate FedOUI on CIFAR-10 under strong non-IID partitioning and noisy-client conditions, comparing it with FedAvg, FedProx, and a gradient-alignment baseline. The clearest gains appear under strong heterogeneity, where OUI-based weighting improves aggregation quality while remaining lightweight and interpretable. These results show that internal activation structure can provide useful information for federated aggregation beyond client size and gradient geometry.

</details>


### [158] [Stargazer: A Scalable Model-Fitting Benchmark Environment for AI Agents under Astrophysical Constraints](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.15664)
*Xinge Liu,Terry Jingchen Zhang,Bernhard Schölkopf,Zhijing Jin,Kristen Menou*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: Stargazer是一个用于评估AI智能体在动态、迭代的物理建模任务中能力的可扩展环境，基于径向速度时间序列数据。研究发现前沿智能体在数值优化与物理约束遵循之间存在显著差距，且增加测试时计算量收益有限。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自主AI智能体的兴起需要动态基准环境，以便在科学基础任务上提供内置反馈，评估这些智能体在研究工作中的能力。

Method: 设计了Stargazer环境，包含120个任务（三个难度层级，含20个真实档案案例），覆盖从高信噪比单行星系统到复杂的低信噪比多行星配置。评估了八个前沿AI智能体在径向速度时间序列推断上的表现。

Result: 智能体在统计拟合上表现良好，但常未能恢复正确的物理系统参数；即使配备基础技能，该限制仍存在。增加测试时计算量仅带来边际收益，过多token消耗常反映递归失败循环而非有意义的探索。

Conclusion: Stargazer为训练、评估、构建和扩展AI智能体策略提供了实用研究场景，其模拟驱动环境设计方法可推广到其他科学领域的模型拟合问题。

Abstract: The rise of autonomous AI agents suggests that dynamic benchmark environments with built-in feedback on scientifically grounded tasks are needed to evaluate the capabilities of these agents in research work. We introduce Stargazer, a scalable environment for evaluating AI agents on dynamic, iterative physics-grounded model-fitting tasks using inference on radial-velocity (RV) time series data. Stargazer comprises 120 tasks across three difficulty tiers, including 20 real archival cases, covering diverse scenarios ranging from high-SNR single-planet systems to complex multi-planetary configurations requiring involved low-SNR analysis. Our evaluation of eight frontier agents reveals a gap between numerical optimization and adherence to physical constraints: although agents often achieve a good statistical fit, they frequently fail to recover correct physical system parameters, a limitation that persists even when agents are equipped with vanilla skills. Furthermore, increasing test-time compute yields only marginal gains, with excessive token usage often reflecting recursive failure loops rather than meaningful exploration. Stargazer presents an opportunity to train, evaluate, scaffold, and scale strategies on a model-fitting problem of practical research relevance today. Our methodology to design a simulation-driven environment for AI agents presumably generalizes to many other model-fitting problems across scientific domains. Source code and the project website are available at https://github.com/AIPS-UofT/Stargazer and https://aips-uoft.github.io/Stargazer/, respectively.

</details>


### [159] [SoK: Unlearnability and Unlearning for Model Dememorization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.11592)
*Mengying Zhang,Derui Wang,Ruoxi Sun,Xiaoyu Xia,Shuang Hao,Minhui Xue*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文首次系统整合了不可学习性（数据投毒）与机器遗忘两种模型去记忆方法，提出了统一分类体系，通过实验揭示了它们的脆弱性（浅层去记忆）与相互影响，并给出了首个针对认证遗忘的去记忆深度理论保证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管不可学习性和机器遗忘都是保护数据不被模型滥用的重要防御手段，但它们的脆弱性、共享基础以及相互之间的影响尚不明确，且缺乏形式化的理论保证来评估当前防御的有效性。

Method: 通过系统化知识梳理（SoK），提出了不可学习性与可扩展遗忘方法的统一分类法；对主流方法进行了鲁棒性、相互影响及浅层去记忆的实证评估；并给出了认证遗忘场景下去记忆深度的首个理论保证。

Result: 发现两种方法都存在浅层去记忆问题（如权重扰动下虚假声称的遗忘或不可学习）；输入扰动会影响下游遗忘效果，而遗忘可能意外恢复被不可学习性隐藏的领域知识；首次提供了去记忆深度的理论保证。

Conclusion: 该研究为统一机器学习生命周期中的去记忆机制奠定了基础，以实现对敏感知识的更深层遗忘状态。

Abstract: Advanced model dememorization methods, including availability poisoning (unlearnability) and machine unlearning, are emerging as key safeguards against data misuse in machine learning (ML). At the training stage, unlearnability embeds imperceptible perturbations into data before release to reduce learnability. At the post-training stage, unlearning removes previously acquired information from models to prevent unauthorized disclosure or use. While both defenses aim to preserve the right to withhold knowledge, their vulnerabilities and shared foundations remain unclear. Specifically, both unlearnability and unlearning suffer from issues such as shallow dememorization, leading to falsely claimed data learnability reduction or forgetting in the presence of weight perturbations. Moreover, input perturbations may affect the effectiveness of downstream unlearning, while unlearning may inadvertently recover domain knowledge hidden by unlearnability. This interplay calls for deeper investigation. Finally, there is a lack of formal guarantees to provide theoretical insights into current defenses against shallow dememorization. In this Systematization of Knowledge, we present the first integrated analysis of model dememorization approaches leveraging unlearnability and unlearning. Our contributions are threefold: (i) a unified taxonomy of unlearnability and scalable unlearning methods; (ii) an empirical evaluation revealing the robustness, interplay, and shallow dememorization of leading methods; and (iii) the first theoretical guarantee on dememorization depth for models processed through certified unlearning. These results lay the foundation for unifying dememorization mechanisms across the ML lifecycle to achieve a deeper immemor state for sensitive knowledge.

</details>


### [160] [EpiCastBench: Datasets and Benchmarks for Multivariate Epidemic Forecasting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.11598)
*Madhurima Panja,Danny D'Agostino,Huitao Li,Tanujit Chakraborty,Nan Liu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出EpiCastBench，一个包含40个多变量流行病数据集的基准框架，系统评估了15种多变量预测模型，旨在解决流行病预测领域缺乏高质量基准的问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 流行病预测是数据驱动的公共卫生决策的关键领域，但缺乏高质量的多变量数据集基准来推动鲁棒预测方法的开发。

Method: 构建了40个精心筛选的多变量流行病数据集，涵盖多种传染病和地理区域；建立了标准化的评估设置（统一预测时域、预处理流程、多种指标和统计显著性检验）；基于该框架对15种多变量预测模型（从统计基线到深度学习和基础模型）进行了全面评估。

Result: 提供了40个具有多样特征（时间粒度、序列长度、稀疏性）的公开数据集，通过标准化评估对15种模型进行了系统性比较。

Conclusion: EpiCastBench为流行病预测研究提供了一个可复现、标准化的多变量基准框架，所有数据和代码均已公开，有助于推动该领域的后续研究。

Abstract: The increasing adoption of data-driven decision-making in public health has established epidemic forecasting as a critical area of research. Recent advances in multivariate forecasting models better capture complex temporal dependencies than conventional univariate approaches, which model individual series independently. Despite this potential, the development of robust epidemic forecasting methods is constrained by the lack of high-quality benchmarks comprising diverse multivariate datasets across infectious diseases and geographical regions. To address this gap, we present EpiCastBench, a large-scale benchmarking framework featuring 40 curated (correlated) multivariate epidemic datasets. These publicly available datasets span a wide range of infectious diseases and exhibit diverse characteristics in terms of temporal granularity, series length, and sparsity. We analyze these datasets to identify their global features and structural patterns. To ensure reproducibility and fair comparison, we establish standardized evaluation settings, including a unified forecasting horizon, consistent preprocessing pipelines, diverse performance metrics, and statistical significance testing. By leveraging this framework, we conduct a comprehensive evaluation of 15 multivariate forecasting models spanning statistical baselines to state-of-the-art deep learning and foundation models. All datasets and code are publicly available on Kaggle (https://www.kaggle.com/datasets/aimltsf/epicastbench) and GitHub (https://github.com/aimltsf/EpiCastBench).

</details>


### [161] [From Generic Correlation to Input-Specific Credit in On-Policy Self Distillation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.11613)
*Guobin Shen,Lei Huang,Xiang Cheng,Chenxiao Zhao,Jindong Li,Dongcheng Zhao,Xing Yu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文分析了自蒸馏token奖励的贝叶斯滤波本质，发现其等价于响应的点互信息(pMI)，并进一步将其分解为输入特定和输入通用成分。基于此分析提出CREDIT方法，通过批对比基线提取输入特定成分，在多个基准上取得最佳性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管on-policy自蒸馏在语言模型后训练中取得了实证成功，但其奖励信号具体测量什么、分配何种信用仍不明确，缺乏理论层面的理解。

Method: 1) 在隐式奖励的后验兼容性解释框架下，证明自蒸馏token奖励是贝叶斯滤波增量，其轨迹和等于响应与反馈间的点互信息(pMI)。2) 将教师对数概率沿输入轴分解为输入特定成分和输入通用成分。3) 提出CREDIT方法，使用批对比基线隔离输入特定成分，序列层面成为对比pMI目标的教师侧替代。

Result: 在编码、科学推理和工具使用基准上，CREDIT在两个模型家族上以可忽略的额外计算成本实现了最强的聚合性能。

Conclusion: 自蒸馏奖励的本质是点互信息，CREDIT通过对比学习有效去除了输入通用捷径，提升了奖励信号的质量和模型性能。

Abstract: On-policy self-distillation has emerged as a promising paradigm for post-training language models, in which the model conditions on environment feedback to serve as its own teacher, providing dense token-level rewards without external teacher models or step-level annotations. Despite its empirical success, what this reward actually measures and what kind of credit it assigns remain unclear. Under a posterior-compatibility interpretation of feedback conditioning, standard in the implicit-reward literature, we show that the self-distillation token reward is a Bayesian filtering increment whose trajectory sum is exactly the pointwise mutual information between the response and the feedback given the input. This pMI can be raised by input-specific reasoning or by input-generic shortcuts, so we further decompose the teacher log-probability along the input axis. Based on this analysis, we propose CREDIT (Contrastive REward from DIsTillation), which isolates the input-specific component with a batch-contrastive baseline. At the sequence level, CREDIT is a teacher-side surrogate for a contrastive pMI objective that also penalizes responses remaining likely under unrelated inputs. Across coding, scientific reasoning, and tool-use benchmarks on two model families, CREDIT delivers the strongest aggregate performance at negligible additional compute.

</details>


### [162] [MIST: Reliable Streaming Decision Trees for Online Class-Incremental Learning via McDiarmid Bound](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.11617)
*Phu-Hoa Pham,Chi-Nguyen Tran,Nguyen Lam Phu Quy,Dao Sy Duy Minh,Huynh Trung Kiet,Long Tran-Thanh*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: MIST提出了一种基于McDiarmid不等式的流式决策树方法，通过K无关的置信半径、贝叶斯继承协议和KLL分位数草图三个组件，解决了传统流式决策树在在线类增量学习中的分裂准则不可靠和缺少知识迁移两个根本问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统流式决策树在在线类增量学习中失败的根本原因在于：信息增益的范围随类别数K呈log₂K缩放，导致Hoeffding置信半径随类别数增长，使得K无关的分裂准则在结构上不可能实现，从而同时引发分裂准则不可靠和分裂时缺少知识迁移两个问题。

Method: MIST包含三个集成组件：(1) 基于McDiarmid不等式的紧致、K无关的Gini分裂置信半径，作为结构正则化器；(2) 贝叶斯继承协议，通过截断高斯矩将父节点统计量投影到子节点，在分裂最保守时方差缩减保证最强；(3) 每叶KLL分位数草图，支持连续阈值评估和几何自适应叶预测。

Result: 在标准表格数据流和压力测试中，MIST在近高斯基准上与全局参数化方法性能相当，在非高斯几何结构上具有独特的鲁棒性，而SOTA基准方法在此类场景中性能崩溃。

Conclusion: MIST通过理论驱动的K无关置信半径设计，从根本上修复了流式决策树在开放世界持续学习中的结构性缺陷，使其在非高斯、复杂几何结构的流数据上具有显著优势。

Abstract: Streaming decision trees are natural candidates for open-world continual learning, as they perform local updates, enjoy bounded memory, and static decision boundaries. Despite these, they still fail in online class-incremental learning due to two coupled miscalibrations: (i) their split criterion grows unreliable as the class count K expands, and (ii) the absence of knowledge transfer at split time. Both failures share a common root: the range of Information Gain intrinsically scales with log2 K. Consequently, any Hoeffding-style confidence radius derived from it must inevitably grow with the class count, making a K-independent split criterion structurally impossible, taking away the potential benefits of applying streaming decision trees to continual learning. To fix this issue, we present MIST (McDiarmid Incremental Streaming Tree), which resolves both failures through three integrated components: (i) a tight, K-independent McDiarmid confidence radius for Gini splitting that acts as a structural regulariser; (ii) a Bayesian inheritance protocol that projects parent statistics to child nodes via truncated-Gaussian moments, with variance reduction guarantees strongest precisely when splitting is most conservative; and (iii) per-leaf KLL quantile sketches that support both continuous threshold evaluation and geometry-adaptive leaf prediction from a single data structure. On standard and stress-test tabular streams, MIST is competitive with global parametric methods on near-Gaussian benchmarks and uniquely robust on non-Gaussian geometry where SOTA benchmarks collapse.

</details>


### [163] [Evolutionary Task Discovery: Advancing Reasoning Frontiers via Skill Composition and Complexity Scaling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.11666)
*Liqin Ye,Yanbin Yin,Michael Galarnyk,Yuzhao Heng,Sudheer Chava,Chao Zhang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出EvoTD框架，通过将数据合成视作算法技能与复杂度属性的双轴流形上的有向搜索，利用结构化进化算子（交叉与参数变异）及动态最近发展区过滤器，系统性地扩展LLM的推理前沿。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM后训练方法（如RLVR）受限于训练数据的多样性与复杂度，而当前数据合成方法（如非结构化变异或探索）存在同质化坍塌问题，无法系统性扩展推理前沿。

Method: 提出EvoTD框架：(1) 将数据合成建模为算法技能(Algorithmic Skills)与复杂度属性(Complexity Attributes)双轴流形上的有向搜索；(2) 设计结构化进化算子：交叉算子合成新技能组合增强多样性，参数变异算子缩放结构约束（如输入规模、树深度）驱动泛化；(3) 集成动态最近发展区(Zone of Proximal Development)过滤器，确保任务处于模型可学习范围内。

Result: EvoTD在多种模型架构、预训练范式及规模上 consistently 提升了推理能力，表明结构化进化课程能有效支持推理改进。

Conclusion: 结构化进化课程能够系统性扩展推理前沿，为LLM后训练中的数据合成提供了有效范式。

Abstract: The reasoning frontier of Large Language Models (LLMs) has advanced significantly through modern post-training paradigms (e.g., Reinforcement Learning from Verifiable Rewards (RLVR)). However, the efficacy of these methods remains fundamentally constrained by the diversity and complexity of the training data. One practical solution is data synthesis; yet, prevalent methods relying on unstructured mutation or exploration suffer from homogeneity collapse, failing to systematically expand the reasoning frontier. To overcome this, we propose Evoutionary Task Discovery (EvoTD), a framework that treats data synthesis as a directed search over a dual-axis manifold of Algorithmic Skills and Complexity Attributes. We introduce structured evolutionary operators to navigate this space: a Crossover operator that synthesizes novel skill compositions to enhance diversity, and a Parametric Mutation operator that scales structural constraints (e.g., input size, tree depth) to drive robust generalization. Crucially, we integrate a dynamic Zone of Proximal Development filter, ensuring tasks lie within the learnable region of the model. Empirically, EvoTD delivers substantial reasoning gains that generalize consistently across model architectures, pretraining regimes, and scales, demonstrating that structured evolutionary curricula can effectively support reasoning improvement. We release our code on https://github.com/liqinye/EvoTD.

</details>


### [164] [Partial Model Sharing Improves Byzantine Resilience in Federated Conformal Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.11684)
*Ehsan Lari,Reza Arablouei,Stefan Werner*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了一种拜占庭鲁棒的联邦共形预测方法，通过部分模型共享和基于直方图的不符合度评分压缩，同时抵御训练和校准阶段的攻击，实现了更紧致的预测区间和接近名义覆盖率的鲁棒不确定性量化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有鲁棒联邦共形预测方法主要加固校准阶段，但忽视了训练阶段的攻击；且缺乏通信高效的鲁棒方案。

Method: 训练阶段采用部分模型共享缩小攻击面并衰减中毒更新；校准阶段将不符合度评分压缩为直方图表征向量，基于距离计算恶意度分数检测拜占庭客户端，仅用良性客户端估计共形分位数。

Result: 在多种拜占庭攻击场景下，相比标准FCP方法，实现了更接近名义覆盖率的覆盖率和更紧致的预测区间。

Conclusion: 该方法为联邦不确定性量化提供了一种鲁棒且通信高效的方案，同时防护了训练和校准阶段的拜占庭攻击。

Abstract: We propose a Byzantine-resilient federated conformal prediction (FCP) method that leverages partial model sharing, where only a subset of model parameters is exchanged each round. Unlike existing robust FCP approaches that primarily harden the calibration stage, our method protects both the federated training and conformal calibration phases. During training, partial sharing inherently restricts the attack surface and attenuates poisoned updates while reducing communication. During calibration, clients compress their non-conformity scores into histogram-based characterization vectors, enabling the server to detect Byzantine clients via distance-based maliciousness scores and to estimate the conformal quantile using only benign contributors. Experiments across diverse Byzantine attack scenarios show that the proposed method achieves closer-to-nominal coverage with substantially tighter prediction intervals than standard FCP, establishing a robust and communication-efficient approach to federated uncertainty quantification.

</details>


### [165] [Shaping Zero-Shot Coordination via State Blocking](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.11688)
*Mingu Kang,Sunwoo Lee,Yonghyeon Jo,Seungyul Han*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出状态阻塞协调（SBC）框架，通过状态阻塞生成虚拟环境族来产生多样化的交互场景，无需直接修改环境即可提升零样本协调性能，在多个基准测试中表现优异，包括对人类的泛化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有零样本协调方法主要通过增加训练时的伙伴多样性来提升泛化能力，但在面对未见过的合作伙伴时仍难以实现可靠的泛化，亟需更有效的解决方案。

Method: 提出State-Blocked Coordination (SBC)框架，通过状态阻塞（state blocking）生成一系列虚拟环境，使智能体能够体验多样化的次优伙伴策略，从而无需直接修改环境即可增强交互场景的多样性。

Result: SBC在多个零样本协调基准测试中取得优异性能，特别是在对未见过的智能体伙伴以及人类伙伴的泛化上表现突出。

Conclusion: SBC作为一种简单有效的框架，通过状态阻塞诱导多样化交互场景，显著提升了零样本协调的泛化能力，为无需环境修改的协调方法提供了新思路。

Abstract: Zero-shot coordination (ZSC) aims to enable agents to cooperate with independently trained partners without prior interaction, a key requirement for real-world multi-agent systems and human-AI collaboration. Existing approaches have largely emphasized increasing partner diversity during training, yet such strategies often fall short of achieving reliable generalization to unseen partners. We introduce State-Blocked Coordination (SBC), a simple yet effective framework that improves ZSC by inducing diverse interaction scenarios without direct environment modification. Specifically, SBC generates a family of virtual environments through state blocking, allowing agents to experience a wide range of suboptimal partner policies. Across multiple benchmarks, SBC demonstrates superior performance in zero-shot coordination, including strong generalization to human partners.

</details>


### [166] [Augmented Lagrangian Method for Last-Iterate Convergence for Constrained MDPs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.11694)
*Michael Lu,Max Qiushi Lin,Mo Chen,Sharan Vaswani*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文针对无限时域折扣约束马尔可夫决策过程（CMDPs），提出了一种基于经典不精确增广拉格朗日方法的通用框架，实现了无需混合策略的最终迭代收敛保证。在表格设置中结合投影Q上升法（PQA），并推广到log-linear策略和非线性策略，在连续控制任务上进行了验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有CMDP理论保证通常针对混合策略，但其部署计算和内存开销大；实际中需部署单一（最终迭代）策略，但现有最终迭代收敛理论多局限于表格设置或实践中少用的算法变体。本文旨在弥合理论与实际之间的差距。

Method: 采用经典的不精确增广拉格朗日方法（AL）作为核心框架；在表格设置中结合投影Q上升法（PQA）求解AL子问题；将结果推广到log-linear策略，使用投影变体PQA；并扩展到非线性策略，在连续控制任务上评估。

Result: 在表格设置中建立了全局最终迭代收敛保证；对于log-linear策略，实现了与先前工作相当的最终迭代收敛保证；在连续控制任务上验证了框架对复杂非线性策略的可扩展性。

Conclusion: 提出的基于不精确AL的通用框架能够有效解决CMDP的最终迭代收敛问题，覆盖从表格到非线性策略的多种设置，兼具理论保证与实践可行性。

Abstract: We study policy optimization for infinite-horizon, discounted constrained Markov decision processes (CMDPs). While existing theoretical guarantees typically hold for the mixture policy, deploying such a policy is computationally and memory intensive. This leads to a practical mismatch where a single (last-iterate) policy must be deployed. Recent theoretical works have thus focused on proving last-iterate convergence, but are largely limited to the tabular setting or to algorithmic variants that are rarely used in practice. To address this, we use the classic inexact augmented Lagrangian ($\texttt{AL}$) method from constrained optimization, and propose a general framework with provable last-iterate convergence for CMDPs. We first focus on the tabular setting and propose to solve the $\texttt{AL}$ sub-problem with projected Q-ascent ($\texttt{PQA}$). Combining the theoretical guarantees of $\texttt{PQA}$ and the standard $\texttt{AL}$ analysis enables us to establish global last-iterate convergence. We generalize these results to handle log-linear policies, and demonstrate that an efficient, projected variant of $\texttt{PQA}$ can achieve last-iterate convergence with comparable guarantees as prior work. Finally, we demonstrate that our framework scales to complex non-linear policies, and evaluate it on continuous control tasks.

</details>


### [167] [GRAFT: Graph-Tokenized LLMs for Tool Planning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.11706)
*Xinyi Gao,Xinyu Ren,Junliang Yu,Tong Chen,Quoc Viet Hung Nguyen,Hongzhi Yin*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出GRAFT框架，通过将工具图内化为特殊token表示并采用on-policy工具上下文蒸馏，解决了LLM在多步工具规划中依赖关系对齐和错误累积的问题，在精确序列匹配和依赖合法性上达到SOTA。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法通过检索、序列化或提示注入等外部图使用策略（匹配范式）将工具依赖图与LLM结合，但难以将工具选择与子任务意图对齐，导致语义上看似合理但违反图约束的计划；且早期错误选择会引发错误累积，使后续预测偏离有效执行路径。

Method: 1) 图标记化：将每个工具节点映射为专用特殊token，在表示空间内学习有向工具依赖关系；2) On-policy工具上下文蒸馏：在模型自身采样的轨迹上训练，逐步蒸馏规划信号。

Result: 在精确序列匹配和依赖合法性指标上达到SOTA性能，支持更可靠的LLM工具规划。

Conclusion: 将工具图内化为模型表示空间的GRAFT框架有效解决了外部图使用策略的匹配范式缺陷和错误累积问题，提升了复杂工作流中工具规划的可靠性。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) are increasingly used to complete complex tasks by selecting and coordinating external tools across multiple steps. This requires aligning tool choices with subtask intent while satisfying directional execution dependencies among tools. To do this, existing methods model these dependencies as tool graphs and incorporate the graphs with LLMs through retrieval, serialization, or prompt-level injection. However, these external graph-use strategies all follow a matching paradigm, which often fails to align tool choices with the underlying subtask structure, producing semantically plausible plans that violate graph constraints. This issue is further exacerbated by error accumulation, where an early incorrect tool selection shifts the plan into an invalid graph state and causes subsequent predictions to drift away from the valid execution path. To address these challenges, we propose GRAFT, a graph-tokenized language model framework for dependency-aware tool planning. GRAFT internalizes the tool graph by mapping each tool node to a dedicated special token and learning directed tool dependencies within the representation space. It further introduces on-policy tool context distillation, training the model on its own sampled trajectories while distilling stepwise planning signals. Experiments show that GRAFT achieves state-of-the-art performance in exact sequence matching and dependency legality, supporting more reliable LLM tool planning in complex workflows.

</details>


### [168] [Debiased Model-based Representations for Sample-efficient Continuous Control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.11711)
*Jiafei Lyu,Zichuan Lin,Scott Fujimoto,Kai Yang,Yangkun Chen,Saiyong Yang,Zongqing Lu,Deheng Ye*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: DR.Q算法通过最大化当前状态-动作对与下一状态之间的互信息，并结合衰减优先级经验回放，解决了现有基于模型表示方法中信息捕获不足和对早期经验过拟合的问题，在连续控制基准测试中达到或超越强基线水平。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于模型的表示方法在离线策略的演员-评论家学习中，存在未能捕获足够的相关变量信息以及对回放缓冲区中早期经验过拟合的问题，导致表示学习和演员-评论家学习产生偏差，性能不佳。

Method: 提出DR.Q算法：1）显式最大化当前状态-动作对表示与下一状态之间的互信息，同时最小化它们的偏差；2）采用衰减优先级经验回放（faded prioritized experience replay）来采样经验。

Result: 在多个连续控制基准任务上，使用单组超参数，DR.Q能够匹配或超越近期强基线方法，有时以较大优势胜出。

Conclusion: DR.Q通过互信息最大化和衰减优先级采样有效消除了表示学习和演员-评论家学习中的偏差，提升了基于模型表示方法的性能。

Abstract: Model-based representations recently stand out as a promising framework that embeds latent dynamics information into the representations for downstream off-policy actor-critic learning. It implicitly combines the advantages of both model-free and model-based approaches while avoiding the training costs associated with model-based methods. Nevertheless, existing model-based representation methods can fail to capture sufficient information about relevant variables and can overfit to early experiences in the replay buffer. These incur biases in representation and actor-critic learning, leading to inferior performance. To address this, we propose Debiased model-based Representations for Q-learning, tagged DR.Q algorithm. DR.Q explicitly maximizes the mutual information between the representations of the current state-action pair and the next state besides minimizing their deviations, and samples transitions with faded prioritized experience replay. We evaluate DR.Q on numerous continuous control benchmarks with a single set of hyperparameters, and the results demonstrate that DR.Q can match or surpass recent strong baselines, sometimes outperforming them by a large margin. Our code is available at https://github.com/dmksjfl/DR.Q.

</details>


### [169] [Persona-Conditioned Adversarial Prompting: Multi-Identity Red-Teaming for Adversarial Discovery and Mitigation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.11730)
*Cristian Morasso,Anisa Halimi,Muhammad Zaid Hameed,Douglas Leith*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出Persona-Conditioned Adversarial Prompting (PCAP)方法，通过在对抗搜索中引入多样化的攻击者角色和策略集，系统性地发现LLM的越狱漏洞并生成丰富的防御数据。实验表明，PCAP将攻击成功率从57%提升至97%，生成的提示多样性提高2-6倍，经微调后模型召回率从0.36提升至0.99，F1分数从0.53提升至0.96。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有自动化红队测试方法通常发现窄域攻击切片，难以覆盖多样的现实世界威胁，且生成的数据不足以支撑安全微调。

Method: PCAP方法将对抗搜索条件化为多样的攻击者角色（如医生、学生、恶意行为者）和策略集，通过并行角色条件化搜索发现可迁移越狱漏洞，并自动生成带有元数据标注的防御数据集。

Result: 在GPT-OSS 120B上，攻击成功率从57%升至97%，提示多样性提升2-6倍；微调后的轻量适配器在保持极低假阳率的同时，召回率从0.36提升至0.99，F1分数从0.53提升至0.96。

Conclusion: PCAP提供了一种从漏洞发现到自动化对齐的实用闭环方法，能有效提升LLM的鲁棒性。

Abstract: Automated red-teaming for LLMs often discovers narrow attack slices, missing diverse real-world threats, and yielding insufficient data for safety fine-tuning. We introduce Persona-Conditioned Adversarial Prompting (PCAP), which conditions adversarial search on diverse attacker personas (e.g., doctors, students, malicious actors) and strategy sets to explore realistic attack scenarios. By running parallel persona-conditioned searches, PCAP discovers transferable jailbreaks across different contexts and generates rich defense datasets with automatic metadata tracking. On GPT-OSS 120B, PCAP increases attack success from 57\% to 97\% while producing 2-6$\times$ more diverse prompts covering varied real-world scenarios. Critically, fine-tuning lightweight adapters on PCAP-generated data significantly improves model robustness (recall: 0.36 $\rightarrow$ 0.99, F1: 0.53 $\rightarrow$ 0.96) with minimal false positives, demonstrating a practical closed-loop approach from vulnerability discovery to automated alignment.

</details>


### [170] [Online Continual Learning with Dynamic Label Hierarchies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.11742)
*Xinrui Wang,Shao-Yuan Li,Bartłomiej Twardowski,Alexandra Gomez-Villa,Songcan Chen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出动态层次在线持续学习（DHOCL）新问题，并设计HALO方法，通过自适应组合分类头与层次原型正则化，解决了动态层级演化中的部分监督和粒度依赖干扰问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有在线持续学习（OCL）方法假设扁平标签空间，忽略了现实世界中概念在水平（兄弟类）和垂直（粗/细粒度）方向上动态演化的层次组织结构。

Method: 提出HALO（层次自适应学习与组织化原型），核心包括：(i) 自适应组合互补的分类头， (ii) 可学习的层次原型进行正则化，实现快速适应、层次一致性和结构化知识整合。

Result: 在多个基准测试中，HALO在层次准确率、错误严重性和持续学习性能上持续优于现有方法。

Conclusion: DHOCL设置更贴近真实场景，HALO通过层次原型正则化有效缓解了动态层次下的部分监督与灾难性遗忘问题。

Abstract: Online Continual Learning (OCL) aims to learn from endless non\text{-}stationary data streams, yet most existing methods assume a flat label space and overlook the hierarchical organization of real\text{-}world concepts that evolves both horizontally (sibling classes) and vertically (coarse or fine categories). To better reflect this context, we introduce a new problem setting, DHOCL (Online Continual Learning from Dynamic Hierarchies), where taxonomies evolve across granularities and each sample provides supervision at a single hierarchical level. In this setting, we find two fundamental issues: (i) partial supervision under mixed granularities provides only point-wise signals over an evolving path-wise hierarchy, which constrains plasticity and undermines cross-level semantic consistency, and (ii) the dynamically evolving hierarchies induce granularity-dependent interference, destabilizing popular replay and regularization mechanisms and thereby exacerbating catastrophic forgetting. To tackle these issues, we propose HALO (Hierarchical Adaptive Learning with Organized Prototypes), which adaptively combines complementary classification heads, regularized by organized learnable hierarchical prototypes, enabling rapid adaptation, hierarchical consistency, and structured knowledge consolidation as the taxonomy evolves. Extensive experiments on multiple benchmarks demonstrate that HALO consistently outperforms existing methods across hierarchical accuracy, mistake severity, and continual performance.

</details>


### [171] [Learning Feature Encoder with Synthetic Anomalies for Weakly Supervised Graph Anomaly Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.11749)
*Yingjie Zhou,Yuqin Xie,Fanxing Liu,Dongjin Song,Ce Zhu,Lingqiao Liu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种弱监督图异常检测方法SAWGAD，通过多任务学习框架利用合成异常（对正常图进行多种扰动生成）来学习对异常敏感的特征表示，并采用两阶段训练策略（先仅用合成样本预热，再结合真实数据联合训练），在多个公开数据集上取得了优越性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有弱监督图异常检测面临的主要挑战是如何学习既能缩小正常数据类内方差、又能对异常保持高敏感性的潜在特征表示。虽然已有工作应用自监督特征学习，但其策略并未针对图异常检测的特殊需求进行定制。

Method: 提出多任务学习方案：1) 通过对正常图进行多种扰动生成合成异常；2) 为每种异常类型分配专用的检测头，确保所学特征对偏离正常模式的潜在偏差保持敏感；3) 两阶段学习策略：初始仅用合成样本的预热阶段，随后是整合两类任务的完整训练阶段。

Result: 在多个公开数据集上的大量实验表明，该方法优于现有竞争方法。

Conclusion: 即使在合成异常不完全匹配真实模式的情况下，它们仍能为有效的特征学习提供有价值的辅助数据（类比ImageNet分类特征迁移到下游视觉任务），证明了该领域特定特征学习策略的有效性。

Abstract: Weakly supervised graph anomaly detection aims to unveil unusual graph instances, e.g., nodes, whose behaviors significantly differ from normal ones, given only a limited number of annotated anomalies and abundant unlabeled samples. A major challenge is to learn a meaningful latent feature representation that reduces intra-class variance among normal data while remaining highly sensitive to anomalies. Although recent works have applied self-supervised feature learning for graph anomaly detection, their strategies are not specifically tailored to its unique requirements, motivating our exploration of a more domain-specific approach. In this paper, we introduce a weakly supervised graph anomaly detection method that leverages a feature learning strategy tailored for graph anomalies. Our approach is built upon a multi-task learning scheme that extracts robust feature representations through synthesized anomalies. We generate synthetic anomalies by perturbing the normal graph in various ways and assign a dedicated detection head to each anomaly type, ensuring that learned features are sensitive to potential deviations from normal patterns. Although synthetic anomalies may not perfectly replicate real-world patterns, they provide valuable auxiliary data for effective feature learnin, much like features learned from ImageNet classification transfer to downstream vision tasks. Additionally, we adopt a two-phase learning strategy: an initial warm-up phase using only synthetic samples, followed by a full-training phase integrating both tasks, to balance the influence of synthetic and real data. Extensive experiments on public datasets demonstrate the superior performance of our method over its competitors. Code is available at https://github.com/yj-zhou/SAWGAD.

</details>


### [172] [Federated Client Selection under Partial Visibility: A POMDP Approach with Spatio-Temporal Attention](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.11752)
*Qijun Hou,Yuchen Shi,Pingyi Fan,Khaled B. Letaief*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 针对联邦学习中部分可见场景（服务器每轮只能访问部分客户端），本文将客户端选择问题建模为部分可观测马尔可夫决策过程（POMDP），并提出基于时空注意力强化学习的框架，通过集成历史全局模型和客户端身份嵌入来捕获训练的时间上下文和客户端的持久特征，在多个数据集上优于现有基线方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有联邦学习客户端选择方法大多假设服务器每轮通信可访问所有客户端，但在大规模或边缘部署场景中，由于通信、移动性或可用性限制，服务器只能观察到部分客户端子集，这种部分可见性导致的性能退化问题亟待解决。

Method: 将部分可见下的联邦客户端选择问题建模为部分可观测马尔可夫决策过程（POMDP），并提出一个基于时空注意力强化学习的框架，该框架通过整合历史全局模型和客户端身份嵌入，同时捕获训练的时间演化上下文和客户端的持久特征。

Result: 在多个数据集上的实验结果表明，所提方法在异构和部分可见场景下均优于现有基线方法，验证了其在处理实际联邦学习系统中不完整观测挑战方面的有效性。

Conclusion: 本文提出的时空注意力强化学习方法有效解决了部分可见场景下的联邦客户端选择问题，为实际部署中客户端不完全可观测的情况提供了一种可行的解决方案。

Abstract: Federated learning relies on effective client selection to alleviate the performance degradation caused by data heterogeneity. Most existing methods assume full visibility of all clients at each communication round. However, in large-scale or edge-based deployments, the server can only access a subset of clients due to communication, mobility, or availability constraints, resulting in partial visibility where only a subset of clients is observable for aggregation in each communication round. In this paper, we formulate federated client selection under partial visibility as a Partially Observable Markov Decision Process (POMDP) and propose a Spatial-Temporal attention-based reinforcement learning framework. By integrating historical global models and client identity embeddings, the proposed method captures both the temporal contexts of training and the persistent characteristics of clients. Experimental results across multiple datasets demonstrate that our approach achieves superior performance compared to existing baselines in heterogeneous and partially visible settings, validating its effectiveness in addressing the challenges of incomplete observations in practical federated learning systems.

</details>


### [173] [One-Step Generative Modeling via Wasserstein Gradient Flows](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.11755)
*Jiaqi Han,Puheng Li,Qiushan Guo,Renyuan Xu,Stefano Ermon,Emmanuel J. Candès*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: W-Flow提出了一种基于Wasserstein梯度流的单步生成框架，通过Sinkhorn散度定义能量泛函，将多步扩散过程压缩为单步生成，在ImageNet 256×256上实现1.29 FID，采样速度比多步扩散模型快约100倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 扩散模型和流模型采样时需要大量迭代更新，计算成本高昂。作者希望设计一种既能保持生成质量又能显著加速采样的方法。

Method: 两阶段方法：首先通过最小化能量泛函的Wasserstein梯度流定义从参考分布到目标分布的演化过程；然后训练一个静态神经生成器将该演化压缩为单步生成。能量泛函采用Sinkhorn散度，得到基于最优传输的高效更新规则。

Result: 在ImageNet 256×256单步生成任务上达到1.29 FID，创下新纪录；与FID相近的多步扩散模型相比，采样速度提升约100倍；在模式覆盖和域迁移方面也有改进。

Conclusion: Wasserstein梯度流为快速高保真生成建模提供了原则性且有效的基础，证明了单步生成可以在保持高质量的同时大幅提升采样效率。

Abstract: Diffusion models and flow-based methods have shown impressive generative capability, especially for images, but their sampling is expensive because it requires many iterative updates. We introduce W-Flow, a framework for training a generator that transforms samples from a simple reference distribution into samples from a target data distribution in a single step. This is achieved in two steps: we first define an evolution from the reference distribution to the target distribution through a Wasserstein gradient flow that minimizes an energy functional; second, we train a static neural generator to compress this evolution into one-step generation. We instantiate the energy functional with the Sinkhorn divergence, which yields an efficient optimal-transport-based update rule that captures global distributional discrepancy and improves coverage of the target distribution. We further prove that the finite-sample training dynamics converge to the continuous-time distributional dynamics under suitable assumptions. Empirically, W-Flow sets a new state of the art for one-step ImageNet 256$\times$256 generation, achieving 1.29 FID, with improved mode coverage and domain transfer. Compared to multi-step diffusion models with similar FID scores, our method yields approximately 100$\times$ faster sampling. These results show that Wasserstein gradient flows provide a principled and effective foundation for fast and high-fidelity generative modeling.

</details>


### [174] [Is Monotonic Sampling Necessary in Diffusion Models?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.11773)
*Muhammad Haris Khan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文系统性地检验了扩散模型中单调采样这一未被质疑的假设，通过设计四类非单调噪声调度并在三种架构模型上测试90种配置，发现所有非单调调度均未优于单调基线，但惩罚程度在不同模型中差异显著（DDPM最大，Flow Matching中等，EDM几乎为零），并提出了调度敏感性系数作为模型质量诊断工具。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 扩散模型的采样过程中噪声水平单调递减已成为默认做法，但这一假设从未被系统性检验过。本文旨在探索单调采样是否是模型性能的必要条件，还是仅仅是领域内的惯例。

Method: 设计了四类结构化的非单调噪声调度，在DDPM、EDM和Flow Matching三种架构上测试，涵盖10到200次函数评估的预算范围，并在CIFAR-10上进行了42个超参数消融实验，总计90种配置。

Result: 所有非单调调度均未改善单调基线性能；惩罚幅度跨越近三个数量级：DDPM显著且持久，Flow Matching居中，EDM几乎为零。惩罚程度是训练好的去噪器的结构性属性，而非噪声。

Conclusion: 验证了领域对单调调度的默认依赖是合理的；提出了调度敏感性系数（Schedule Sensitivity Coefficient）作为廉价、架构无关的诊断工具，用于提供在关键噪声水平未收敛到贝叶斯最优去噪器的证据，补充了FID等样本质量指标。

Abstract: Diffusion models generate samples by iteratively denoising a Gaussian prior, traversing a sequence of noise levels that, in every published sampler, decreases monotonically. Six years of intensive work has refined nearly every aspect of this recipe, including the corruption operator, the training objective, the schedule shape, the architecture, and the ODE solver. Yet the assumption of monotonicity itself has never been systematically tested. Here we ask whether monotonic sampling is load-bearing or merely conventional. We design four families of structured nonmonotonic schedules and apply them to three architecturally distinct generative models, DDPM, EDM, and Flow Matching, across NFE budgets ranging from 10 to 200 function evaluations, plus a 42-cell hyperparameter ablation, on CIFAR-10. Across all 90 tested configurations, no tested nonmonotonic schedule improves on the monotonic baseline. The magnitude of the penalty, however, spans nearly three orders of magnitude: persistent and substantial in DDPM, intermediate in Flow Matching, and indistinguishable from zero in EDM. We show that this variation is not noise but a structural property of each trained denoiser, and we formalize it as the Schedule Sensitivity Coefficient, a cheap, architecture-agnostic diagnostic that provides evidence of non-convergence to the Bayes-optimal denoiser at the critical noise level. Our findings justify the field's tacit reliance on monotonic schedules and supply a new probe of diffusion model quality complementary to sample-quality metrics such as Frechet Inception Distance.

</details>


### [175] [Stop Marginalizing My Dreams: Model Inversion via Laplace Kernel for Continual Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.11804)
*Patryk Krukowski,Jacek Tabor,Przemysław Spurek,Marek Śmieja,Łukasz Struski*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出REMIX框架，通过拉普拉斯核参数化实现可扩展的全协方差建模，替代传统对角协方差假设，显著提升数据无关持续学习中伪样本合成质量与知识保留效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有数据无关持续学习（DFCIL）中的模型反演方法普遍采用对角协方差假设，忽略了特征相关性，导致合成伪样本保真度低、知识保留受限。

Method: 提出REMIX框架，利用拉普拉斯核参数化实现结构化协方差建模，在不进行稠密矩阵求逆和对数行列式计算的前提下，以线性存储和附加对数因子计算开销实现全协方差建模。

Result: REMIX能够生成更连贯的合成样本，在标准DFCIL基准测试中持续提升性能，验证了超越对角协方差假设的有效性和可扩展性。

Conclusion: 建模特征依赖性是实现有效数据无关持续学习的关键，REMIX证明了结构化协方差建模在可扩展性和性能上的优势。

Abstract: Data-free continual learning (DFCIL) relies on model inversion to synthesize pseudo-samples and mitigate catastrophic forgetting. However, existing inversion methods are fundamentally limited by a simplifying assumption: they model feature distributions using diagonal covariance, effectively ignoring correlations that define the geometry of learned representations. As a result, synthesized samples often lack fidelity, limiting knowledge retention. In this work, we show that modeling feature dependencies is a key ingredient for effective DFCIL. We introduce REMIX, a structured covariance modeling framework that enables scalable full-covariance modeling without the prohibitive cost of dense matrix inversion and log-determinant computation. By leveraging a Laplace kernel parameterization, REMIX captures structured feature dependencies using memory that scales linearly with the feature dimensionality, while requiring only an additional logarithmic factor in computation. Modeling these correlations produces more coherent synthetic samples and consistently improves performance across standard DFCIL benchmarks. Our results demonstrate that moving beyond diagonal assumptions is essential for effective and scalable data-free continual learning. Our code is available at https://github. com/pkrukowski1/REMIX-Model-Inversion-via-Laplace-Kernel.

</details>


### [176] [Martingale-Consistent Self-Supervised Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.11846)
*Moritz Gögl,Hanwen Xing,Christopher Yau*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一个鞅一致性的自监督学习框架，确保粗视图和精炼视图的预测在期望上保持一致，防止系统性漂移，并在部分观测下提升鲁棒性和校准性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有自监督学习在信息变化（如缺失特征、部分观测图像）场景下，粗视图和精炼视图的预测缺乏一致性保证。标准SSL目标（如不变性）未能约束预测随信息揭示时的期望更新，导致系统性漂移。

Method: 提出鞅一致性SSL框架，包含预测空间和潜空间两种变体，以及基于随机精炼的无偏双样本蒙特卡洛估计器。

Result: 在合成数据、真实时间序列、表格数据和图像基准的部分观测场景下，该方法在部分观测下提升了鲁棒性和校准性，产生更稳定的表示。

Conclusion: 鞅一致性比不变性目标更适合部分观测下的自监督学习，能有效防止系统性漂移，提升模型可靠性。

Abstract: Self-supervised learning (SSL) is often deployed under changing information, such as shorter histories, missing features, or partially observed images. In these settings, predictions from coarse and refined views should be coherent: before refinement, the coarse-view prediction should match the average prediction expected after refinement. Martingales formalize this coherence principle, but standard SSL objectives do not enforce it. Unlike invariance objectives that pull views together, martingale consistency constrains only the expected refined prediction, allowing predictions to update as information is revealed while preventing systematic drift. We introduce a martingale-consistent SSL framework that closes this gap, with practical prediction- and latent-space variants and an unbiased two-sample Monte Carlo estimator based on stochastic refinement. We evaluate the approach on synthetic and real time-series, tabular, and image benchmarks under partial-observation regimes, in both semi-self-supervised and fully label-free settings. Across these experiments, our framework improves robustness and calibration under partial observation, yielding more stable representations as information is revealed.

</details>


### [177] [Improving the Performance and Learning Stability of Parallelizable RNNs Designed for Ultra-Low Power Applications](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.11855)
*Julien Brandoit,Arthur Fyon,Damien Ernst,Guillaume Drion*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文针对Bistable Memory Recurrent Unit (BMRU)中梯度阻塞问题，提出累积更新机制（CMRU及其变体αCMRU），在保持量化状态、持久记忆和模拟硬件兼容性的同时，恢复梯度流动、提升收敛稳定性，在多个小规模基准上匹配或超越LRU和minGRU。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: BMRU虽能实现超低功耗的硬件-软件协同设计，但其在复杂序列任务上的性能落后于可并行化的RNN。作者识别出状态更新过程中的梯度阻塞是关键限制因素。

Method: 提出累积更新公式（Cumulative Update Formulation），通过引入跨时间的跳跃连接（skip-connections）来恢复梯度流动，同时保留持久记忆。由此得到Cumulative Memory Recurrent Unit (CMRU)及其松弛变体αCMRU。

Result: 累积公式显著提升了收敛稳定性并降低了对初始化的敏感性。在小模型规模下，CMRU和αCMRU在多个基准上匹配或超越了Linear Recurrent Units (LRUs)和minimal Gated Recurrent Units (minGRUs)，在需要离散长程保留的任务上尤其有优势。

Conclusion: CMRU保留了量化状态、持久记忆和对抗噪声的鲁棒动力学，这些特性对于模拟硬件实现至关重要，同时在性能上取得了突破性提升。

Abstract: Sequence learning is dominated by Transformers and parallelizable recurrent neural networks (RNNs) such as state-space models, yet learning long-term dependencies remains challenging, and state-of-the-art designs trade power consumption for performance. The Bistable Memory Recurrent Unit (BMRU) was introduced to enable hardware-software co-design of ultra-low power RNNs: quantized states with hysteresis provide persistent memory while mapping directly to analog primitives. However, BMRU performance lags behind parallelizable RNNs on complex sequential tasks. In this paper, we identify gradient blocking during state updates as a key limitation and propose a cumulative update formulation that restores gradient flow while preserving persistent memory, creating skip-connections through time. This leads to the Cumulative Memory Recurrent Unit (CMRU) and its relaxed variant, the $α$CMRU. Experiments show that the cumulative formulation dramatically improves convergence stability and reduces initialization sensitivity. The CMRU and $α$CMRU match or outperform Linear Recurrent Units (LRUs) and minimal Gated Recurrent Units (minGRUs) across diverse benchmarks at small model sizes, with particular advantages on tasks requiring discrete long-range retention, while the CMRU retains quantized states, persistent memory, and noise-resilient dynamics essential for analog implementation.

</details>


### [178] [Beyond Parameter Aggregation: Semantic Consensus for Federated Fine-Tuning of LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.11857)
*Amr Abourayya,Jens Kleesiek,Michael Kamp*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于行为共识而非参数聚合的联邦大模型微调范式：客户端在本地微调模型后，将公共提示集的生成输出发送至服务器，服务器在语义空间中形成共识并返回伪标签用于进一步微调。该方法通信量仅取决于公共提示集规模和行为大小，与模型参数无关，可支持异构架构，理论分析表明在Llama3.1-405B上通信量减少约1006倍，同时降低运行时和能耗。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统联邦微调依赖参数聚合，但存在通信开销巨大、要求模型架构对齐、需要白盒访问模型参数等限制。随着模型规模增长和部署环境异构性增加，这些假设与实际约束日益脱节，亟需一种更适合生成式基础模型的联邦适配抽象。

Method: 客户端在本地微调模型，基于共享的公共提示集生成输出；服务器将这些输出映射到语义表示空间，形成每个提示的语义共识，生成伪标签；客户端利用伪标签进行进一步本地微调。通信内容仅为提示集上的生成行为，而非模型参数。

Result: 该方法在匹配强联邦微调基线的同时，大幅降低通信量（理论分析显示Llama3.1-405B上减少约1006倍），并降低运行时和能耗。可自然支持异构架构和开放式文本生成。

Conclusion: 对于生成式基础模型，行为级共识比参数聚合更适合作为联邦适配的抽象层次，从根本上改变了联邦大模型微调的通信扩展模式。

Abstract: Federated fine-tuning of large language models is commonly formulated as a parameter aggregation problem. However, even parameter-efficient methods require transmitting large collections of trainable weights, assume aligned architectures, and rely on white-box access to model parameters. As model sizes continue to grow and deployments become increasingly heterogeneous, these assumptions become progressively misaligned with practical constraints. We consider an alternative formulation in which collaboration is mediated through model behavior rather than parameters. Clients fine-tune local models on private data and exchange generated outputs on a shared, public prompt set. The server maps these outputs into a semantic representation space, forms a per-prompt semantic consensus, and returns pseudo-labels for further local fine-tuning. This formulation fundamentally changes the communication scaling of federated LLM fine-tuning. The amount of information exchanged depends only on the public prompt budget and the size of the communicated behaviors, independent of model size. As a consequence, the protocol naturally accommodates heterogeneous architectures and applies directly to open-ended text generation. We present a theoretical analysis and empirical results demonstrating that this approach can match strong federated fine-tuning baselines while substantially reducing communication by orders of magnitude (e.g., analytically by a factor of $1006$ for Llama3.1-405B), as well as reductions in runtime and energy consumption. These results suggest that, for generative foundation models, behavior-level consensus provides a more appropriate abstraction for federated adaptation than parameter aggregation.

</details>


### [179] [Information theoretic underpinning of self-supervised learning by clustering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.11870)
*Josef Kittler,Sara Atito,Muhammad Awais*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文通过将自监督学习（SSL）形式化为KL散度优化问题，从理论上解释了深度聚类方法中的蒸馏和中心化操作，证明了这些启发式方法源于对教师分布施加优化约束以防止模式坍塌，并通过Jensen不等式简化为批次中心化过程。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为自监督学习（特别是深度聚类方法）建立理论基础，弥补该领域理论支撑不足的问题，从而解释现有成功方法中的启发式实践。

Method: 通过类比监督学习，将SSL形式化为KL散度优化问题；通过对教师分布施加优化约束来防止模式坍塌，导出使用逆聚类先验的归一化；利用Jensen不等式将该归一化简化为批次中心化过程。

Result: 理论模型不仅支持了现有的成功SSL方法（如蒸馏和中心化），而且为未来研究方向提供了指导。

Conclusion: 本文为SSL中的深度聚类方法提供了坚实的理论基础，揭示了蒸馏和中心化等启发式实践背后的数学原理，并为后续理论研究指明了方向。

Abstract: Self-supervised learning (SSL) is recognized as an essential tool for building foundation models for Artificial Intelligence applications. The advances in SSL have been made thanks to vigorous arguments about the principles of SSL and through extensive empirical research. The aim of this paper is to contribute to the development of the underpinning theory of SSL, focusing on the deep clustering approach. By analogy to supervised learning, we formulate SSL as K-L divergence optimization.
  The mode collapse is prevented by imposing an optimisation constraint on the teacher distribution. This leads to normalization using inverse cluster priors. We show that using Jensen inequality this normalization simplifies to the popular batch centering procedure. Distillation and centering are common {heuristics-based} practices in SSL, {but our work underpins them theoretically.} The theoretical model developed not only supports specific existing successful SSL methods, but also suggests directions for future investigations.

</details>


### [180] [LOFT: Low-Rank Orthogonal Fine-Tuning via Task-Aware Support Selection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.11872)
*Lanxin Zhao,Bamdev Mishra,Pratik Jawanpuria,Lequan Lin,Dai Shi,Junbin Gao,Andi Han*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: LOFT是一个低秩正交微调框架，将正交参数高效微调解耦为适应子空间和子空间内变换两个独立设计维度，并提出基于训练信号的任务感知支持选择策略，在多个任务上提升了效率-性能权衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有正交PEFT方法混淆了适应发生的子空间（support）与子空间内应用的变换（transformation）这两个独立的设计选择，导致支持选择被当作特定参数化的副产品而非核心设计轴。

Method: 将正交适应建模为乘法子空间旋转，提出LOFT框架统一归纳坐标型、蝴蝶型、Householder型和主子空间型等正交PEFT变体；通过一阶分析证明有效的适应支持应受下游训练信号引导，提出任务感知的支持选择策略。

Result: 在语言理解、视觉迁移、数学推理和多语言分布外适应任务中，LOFT恢复了主子空间正交适应的性能，同时梯度信息引导的支持选择在匹配参数、内存和计算预算下改善了效率-性能权衡。

Conclusion: 原则性的支持选择是改进正交PEFT的重要方向。

Abstract: Orthogonal parameter-efficient fine-tuning (PEFT) adapts pretrained weights through structure-preserving multiplicative transformations, but existing methods often conflate two distinct design choices: the subspace in which adaptation occurs and the transformation applied within that subspace. This paper introduces LOFT, a low-rank orthogonal fine-tuning framework that explicitly separates these two components. By viewing orthogonal adaptation as a multiplicative subspace rotation, LOFT provides a unified formulation that recovers representative orthogonal PEFT methods, including coordinate-, butterfly-, Householder-, and principal-subspace-based variants. More importantly, this perspective exposes support selection as a central design axis rather than a byproduct of a particular parameterization. We develop a first-order analysis showing that useful adaptation supports should be informed by the downstream training signal, motivating practical task-aware support selection strategies. Across language understanding, visual transfer, mathematical reasoning, and multilingual out-of-distribution adaptation, LOFT recovers principal-subspace orthogonal adaptation while gradient-informed supports improve the efficiency-performance trade-off under matched parameter, memory, and compute budgets. These results suggest that principled support selection is an important direction for improving orthogonal PEFT.

</details>


### [181] [Sobolev Regularized MMD Gradient Flow](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.11884)
*Chenyang Tian,Bharath K. Sriperumbudur,Arthur Gretton,Zonghao Chen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出Sobolev正则化最大均值差异（SrMMD）梯度流，通过在见证函数上添加梯度惩罚来正则化MMD目标，缓解了MMD的非凸性，并提供了连续和离散时间下MMD的全局收敛保证，且不需要对目标分布做等周假设。该方法同时适用于采样和生成建模任务。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: MMD梯度流的目标函数是非凸的，且现有方法需要等周假设才能保证收敛；此外，以往梯度流方法要么仅适用于生成建模，要么仅适用于采样，缺乏统一框架。

Method: 在MMD梯度流的见证函数上添加梯度惩罚（Sobolev正则化），得到SrMMD梯度流。在采样设置中使用Stein核，在生成建模中使用标准核。

Result: 提供了连续时间和离散时间下MMD的全局收敛保证，且不依赖目标分布的等周假设。在生成建模和采样任务上均验证了有效性。

Conclusion: SrMMD梯度流通过简单的梯度惩罚正则化，实现了无需等周假设的全局收敛保证，并且统一了采样和生成建模两种场景。

Abstract: We propose Sobolev-regularized Maximum Mean Discrepancy (SrMMD) gradient flow, a regularized variant of maximum mean discrepancy (MMD) gradient flow based on a gradient penalty on the witness function. The proposed regularization mitigates the non-convexity of the MMD objective and yields provable \emph{global} convergence guarantees in MMD in both continuous and discrete time. A more surprising appeal is that our convergence analysis does not rely on isoperimetric assumptions on the target distribution. Instead, it is based on a regularity condition on the difference between kernel mean embeddings. A key highlight of the proposed flow is that it is applicable in both sampling (from an unnormalized target distribution) -- using Stein kernels -- and generative modeling settings, unlike previous works, where a gradient flow is suitable for only generative modeling or sampling but not both. The effectiveness of the proposed flow is empirically verified on a broad range of tasks in both generative modelling and sampling.

</details>


### [182] [Incentivizing Truthfulness and Collaborative Fairness in Bayesian Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.11889)
*Rachael Hwee Ling Sim,Jue Fan,Xiao Tian,Xinyi Xu,Patrick Jaillet,Bryan Kian Hsiang Low*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出首个能同时保证贝叶斯模型协作公平性（F）并激励数据真实性（T）的机制，通过结合半值（如Shapley值）和基于未知验证集的可信数据估值函数（DVF），证明数据源可通过提交真实知识最大化其预期数据价值和半值。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有数据估值方法虽能公平奖励数据贡献，但无法验证或激励数据真实性，数据源可通过提交重复或噪声数据人为提高估值和奖励，或阻碍他人获益。

Method: 结合半值（确保公平性）与基于未知验证集的可信数据估值函数（DVF），引入额外条件证明数据源通过提交真实知识可最大化其联合博弈中的预期数据价值和半值。

Result: 在合成和真实数据集上验证了理论结果；讨论了有限预算或缺少验证集时的松弛条件。

Conclusion: 首次实现贝叶斯模型下协作公平性与数据真实性激励的均衡机制，为协作学习中的防操纵数据估值提供了理论基础。

Abstract: Collaborative machine learning involves training high-quality models using datasets from a number of sources. To incentivize sources to share data, existing data valuation methods fairly reward each source based on its data submitted as is. However, as these methods do not verify nor incentivize data truthfulness, the sources can manipulate their data (e.g., by submitting duplicated or noisy data) to artificially increase their valuations and rewards or prevent others from benefiting. This paper presents the first mechanism that provably ensures (F) collaborative fairness and incentivizes (T) truthfulness at equilibrium for Bayesian models. Our mechanism combines semivalues (e.g., Shapley value), which ensure fairness, and a truthful data valuation function (DVF) based on a validation set that is unknown to the sources. As semivalues are influenced by others' data, we introduce an additional condition to prove that a source can maximize its expected data values in coalitions and semivalues by submitting a dataset that captures its true knowledge. Additionally, we discuss the implications and suitable relaxations of (F) and (T) when the mediator has a limited budget for rewards or lacks a validation set. Our theoretical findings are validated on synthetic and real-world datasets.

</details>


### [183] [Procedural-skill SFT across capacity tiers: A W-Shaped pre-SFT Trajectory and Regime-Asymmetric Mechanism on 0.8B-4B Qwen3.5 Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.11907)
*Igor Strozzi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文在三种规模（0.8B/2B/4B）的Qwen3.5模型上测量过程性技能SFT的贡献，发现SFT带来的提升大致均匀（约+0.04~+0.075），但过程性模板本身对基座模型存在不对称影响（0.8B和4B受损，2B受益），SFT在基座模型表现最差的配置下最有效。同时揭示了基准格式合规性伪影和负迭代序列对绝对通过率上限的约束。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量化过程性技能SFT在不同规模模型上的真实贡献，澄清此前“格式仅有效于0.8B”和“SFT在4B上贡献递减”等结论是否源于路径不匹配的伪影。

Method: 使用353条（任务+过程性技能块, Opus思维链, 评判通过）示范数据，在200任务/40技能的保留集上，对Qwen3.5三种规模（0.8B, 2B, 4B）进行SFT训练，以Claude Haiku 4.5作为前沿参考。采用匹配路径的LLM-only评分、GPT-5.4交叉验证，并分析格式合规性伪影和迭代序列变体。

Result: SFT归因的过程性提升在不同规模上大致均匀（0.8B:+0.070, 2B:+0.040, 4B:+0.075）。但后SFT的Delta变化（-0.005, +0.100, +0.065）主要由W形的前SFT基座轨迹主导：5步过程性模板对0.8B和4B有害，对2B有益，对Haiku 4.5有适度帮助。SFT在基座模型处理过程性模板最困难的配置下绝对提升最大，呈现“机制不对称”模式。GPT-5.4交叉验证Cohen's κ≥0.754，一致率≥93.25%。

Conclusion: 过程性技能SFT的效果并非随规模单调变化，而是与基座模型对过程性模板的固有能力高度相关；此前“格式仅有效于小模型”和“SFT在大模型上收益递减”的结论是路径不匹配伪影，本文予以修正。单种子设置，威胁在§\ref{sec:threats}中讨论。

Abstract: We measure procedural-skill SFT contribution across three Qwen3.5 dense scales (0.8B, 2B, 4B) on a 200-task / 40-skill holdout, with Claude Haiku 4.5 as a frontier reference. The corpus is 353 rows of (task + procedural-skill block, Opus chain-of-thought, judge-pass) demonstrations.
  \textbf{Main finding.} Under matched-path LLM-only scoring, the SFT-attributable procedural-$Δ$ lift is roughly uniform across sizes: $+0.070$ / $+0.040$ / $+0.075$ at 0.8B / 2B / 4B. Variation in post-SFT $Δ$ ($-0.005$, $+0.100$, $+0.065$) is dominated by a W-shaped pre-SFT base trajectory ($-0.075$, $+0.060$, $-0.010$, Haiku-4-5 at $+0.030$): the 5-step procedure hurts 0.8B and 4B, helps 2B, and helps frontier Haiku modestly. SFT works hardest in absolute terms where the base struggles with the procedure -- a regime-asymmetric pattern with a falsifiable prediction at 8B/14B.
  \textbf{Methodology.} (i) A bench format-compliance artifact: 83.5\% of the holdout uses a deterministic \texttt{ANSWER}-line extractor that under-counts free-form conclusions; an LLM-only re-judge reveals it was systematically biased against \CU. (ii) A negative-iteration sequence at 0.8B: five recipe variants cluster post-SFT \CU{} pass-rate within a 2\,pp band, constraining the absolute-pass-rate ceiling to base capacity rather than recipe.
  \textbf{Cross-family validation.} GPT-5.4 via OpenRouter on all 7 configurations (2800 paired episodes) agrees on the direction of every per-student finding: Cohen's $κ\geq 0.754$, agreement $\geq 93.25\%$.
  Earlier ``format-only at 0.8B'' and ``shrinking SFT at 4B'' framings were path-mismatch artifacts; this paper supersedes both (Appendix~\ref{sec:appendix-path}). Single-seed; threats in §\ref{sec:threats}.

</details>


### [184] [STAGE: Tackling Semantic Drift in Multimodal Federated Graph Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.11919)
*Zekai Chen,Xun Wu,Xunkai Li,Yihan Sun,Rong-Hua Li,Guoren Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出STAGE框架，通过先构建共享语义空间翻译异构多模态特征，再规范局部图结构上的表示传播，解决了多模态联邦图学习中客户端表征不一致的问题，在8个多模态图数据集的5类任务上取得SOTA性能并降低通信开销。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多模态联邦图学习中，不同模态域的客户端在协作前缺乏共享语义空间，导致本地编码器对同一概念产生不一致表征，直接参数协调不可靠，进而引发虚假语义一致性和图消息传递放大残差不一致两个下游问题。

Method: 提出STAGE协议优先框架：首先将异构多模态特征翻译为可比表征以构建共享语义空间，然后规范这些表征在局部图结构上的传播方式，避免直接参数平均。

Result: 在8个多模态属性图上的5类图中心和模态中心任务中持续达到SOTA性能，同时降低每轮通信负载。

Conclusion: STAGE通过先翻译后规范传播的两阶段策略，有效缓解了跨客户端语义校准问题并抑制了不一致性放大，验证了协议优先方法在多模态联邦图学习中的有效性。

Abstract: Federated graph learning (FGL) enables collaborative training on graph data across multiple clients. As graph data increasingly contain multimodal node attributes such as text and images, multimodal federated graph learning (MM-FGL) has become an important yet substantially harder setting. The key challenge is that clients from different modality domains may not share a common semantic space: even for the same concept, their local encoders can produce inconsistent representations before collaboration begins. This makes direct parameter coordination unreliable and further causes two downstream problems: forcing heterogeneous client representations into a naively shared semantic space may create false semantic agreement, and graph message passing may amplify residual inconsistency across neighborhoods. To address this issue, we propose \textbf{STAGE}, a protocol-first framework for MM-FGL. Instead of relying on direct parameter averaging, STAGE builds a shared semantic space that first translates heterogeneous multimodal features into comparable representations and then regulates how these representations propagate over local graph structures. In this way, STAGE not only improves cross-client semantic calibration, but also reduces the risk of inconsistency amplification during graph learning. Extensive experiments on 8 multimodal-attributed graphs across 5 graph-centric and modality-centric tasks show that STAGE consistently achieves state-of-the-art performance while reducing per-round communication payload.

</details>


<div id='math.OC'></div>

# math.OC [[Back]](#toc)

### [185] [A Unified Approach for Computing Wasserstein Barycenters of Discrete and Continuous Measures](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.11270)
*Peng Xu,Changbo Zhu,Xiaohui Chen*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于Fisher-Rao几何的原始镜像下降算法，用于计算精确的Wasserstein重心，该算法统一适用于离散和绝对连续测度，具有收敛保证，并通过合成与真实数据验证了其准确性和计算效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有Wasserstein重心算法要么针对离散测度（点云），要么针对规则网格上离散化的连续测度，缺乏统一框架。本文旨在设计一种能同时处理离散和绝对连续输入测度的通用算法。

Method: 提出一种在Fisher-Rao几何下的原始镜像下降算法。对于离散输入测度，算法从任意概率密度初始化，通过求解一系列半离散最优传输子问题，产生绝对连续的迭代序列，并收敛至离散重心。

Result: 算法在离散和绝对连续两种设定下均具有收敛保证。合成与真实数据实验表明，该方法在精度和计算成本方面取得了有竞争力的结果。

Conclusion: 所提算法是一个灵活统一的Wasserstein重心计算框架，能覆盖离散和连续测度，理论上有收敛保证，实验上验证了有效性和效率。

Abstract: Computing the unregularized Wasserstein barycenter for measure-valued data is a challenging optimization task. Recent algorithms have been tailored to either discrete measures as point clouds or continuous measures discretized on regular grids. In this work, we propose a primal mirror descent algorithm for computing the exact Wasserstein barycenter in the Fisher-Rao geometry. Our algorithm is a unified approach that is flexible enough to simultaneously cover discrete and absolutely continuous input measures, with convergence guarantees in both settings. In particular, when all input measures are discrete, our algorithm, initialized from any probability density, solves a sequence of semi-discrete optimal transport subproblems and produces absolutely continuous iterates that converge to the discrete barycenter. We use synthetic and real data examples to demonstrate the promising result in terms of accuracy and computational cost.

</details>


### [186] [Byzantine-Resilient Consensus via Active Reputation Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.11357)
*Rui Huang,Changxin Liu,Wen-Hua Chen,Yang Shi*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种拜占庭弹性共识框架，通过将主动声誉学习机制嵌入共识循环，同时实现拜占庭智能体识别与正常智能体弹性共识的双重目标。实验表明，该方法在拜占庭检测精度、共识可靠性和可扩展性方面均优于经典方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法将对抗缓解视为被动过滤过程，未能主动识别拜占庭智能体，且缺乏将声誉学习与共识控制协同设计的机制。

Method: 在共识循环中嵌入主动声誉学习机制：使用异常鲁棒损失函数和历史信息评估邻居行为，通过平衡损失最小化与多样性保持探索的机制在概率单纯形上构建声誉向量，然后利用声誉形成加权局部更新以抑制对抗影响。

Result: 在多种分布式系统实验中，该方法在拜占庭检测精度上优于经典弹性共识方法，同时实现了更可靠和可扩展的共识。

Conclusion: 提出的学习-控制协同设计形成了闭环双目标：改进的共识状态增强拜占庭可识别性，而精炼的声誉又反过来改善共识。

Abstract: This paper proposes a Byzantine-resilient consensus framework that simultaneously pursues two tightly coupled objectives: actively identifying Byzantine agents and guaranteeing resilient consensus among normal agents. Unlike existing methods that treat adversary mitigation as a passive filtering process, our approach embeds an active reputation learning mechanism into the consensus loop. Agents evaluate neighbors' behaviors using outlier-robust loss functions and historical information, and construct a reputation vector on a probability simplex via a mechanism that balances loss minimization with diversity-preserving exploration, representing dynamic beliefs over neighbor trustworthiness. These reputations are then used to form weighted local updates that suppress adversarial influence and improve agreement among normal agents, thereby reducing the bias in local loss evaluations and enabling more reliable subsequent reputation estimation.
