This learning-control co-design yields a closed-loop dual objective: improved consensus states enhance Byzantine identifiability, while refined reputations in turn improve consensus. A range of distributed systems experiments, benchmarking against classical resilient consensus methods, demonstrate superior Byzantine detection accuracy and significantly more reliable and scalable consensus.

</details>


### [187] [The Grimmer-Shu-Wang Certificate and the Drori-Teboulle Minimax Nonnegative Constant-Stepsize Bound for N >= 3](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.11421)
*Lixing Zhang*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文证明对于所有N≥3，Grimmer-Shu-Wang提出的低秩性能估计证书在Drori-Teboulle极小极大非负常数步长梯度下降问题中存在，从而建立了该问题的精确最优值。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Drori-Teboulle问题中的低秩PEP证书存在性此前尚未得到严格证明。

Method: 通过约化残差系统、单纯形存在性论证、末项残差恒等式及尾平方卷积论证，证明系数向量的正性。

Result: 证明了GSW低秩PEP证书对每个N≥3存在，并得到Drori-Teboulle上界。

Conclusion: 结合一维二次与Huber下界例子，完整建立了N≥3时梯度下降的Drori-Teboulle极小极大非负常数步长值。

Abstract: We prove, for every horizon N >= 3, the existence of the strengthened low-rank performance-estimation certificate proposed by Grimmer, Shu, and Wang for the Drori-Teboulle minimax nonnegative constant-stepsize problem for gradient descent. Let rho_N in (0,1) be the unique solution of rho_N^{2N}(2N rho_N+2N+1)=1. We show that the GSW certificate equations admit positive vectors a, b, c, d satisfying all residual equations. The proof proceeds through a reduced residual system in the variables d, a simplex existence argument for a positive reduced zero, a terminal residual completion identity, and a tail-square convolution argument proving the cumulative margins that force positivity of the certificate coefficients. Consequently, the GSW low-rank PEP certificate exists for every N >= 3 and yields the Drori-Teboulle upper bound. Together with the one-dimensional quadratic and Huber lower-bound examples, this establishes the Drori-Teboulle minimax nonnegative constant-stepsize value for gradient descent for every N >= 3.

</details>


### [188] [From Schrodinger Bridge to Optimal Transport over Sub-Riemannian Manifolds](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.11429)
*Daniel Owusu Adu,Karthik Elamvazhuthi,Bahman Gharesifard*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文研究了在亚黎曼几何约束下，通过熵正则化方法实现概率分布的最小能量重塑，将问题转化为Schrödinger桥问题，并提出了Sinkhorn型算法进行求解。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 受欠驱动系统密度控制需求驱动，研究在水平约束（亚黎曼几何）下概率分布的最优传输与分布转向问题。

Method: 引入熵正则化，向控制方向添加小噪声，构建退化的亚黎曼扩散过程作为参考过程；利用括号生成条件获得光滑正转移密度，建立前向-后向最优桥刻画，提出Sinkhorn型算法求解Schrödinger势。

Result: 获得了光滑严格正的转移密度；提出了实用的Sinkhorn算法求解Schrödinger势；当噪声趋于零时，可恢复确定性亚黎曼最优传输问题；数值实验验证了方法有效性。

Conclusion: 熵正则化框架有效桥接了亚黎曼最优传输与Sinkhorn算法，为欠驱动系统密度控制提供了可行数值方案。

Abstract: We study the least-energy way to reshape a probability distribution when motion is constrained to a horizontal bundle, that is, optimal transport and distribution steering in sub-Riemannian geometry, motivated by density control over underactuated systems. To obtain a continuous and numerically tractable formulation, we introduce an entropic regularization by adding small noise aligned with the control directions and study the associated Schrodinger bridge problem. The resulting reference process is a degenerate diffusion on the sub-Riemannian manifold. Under bracket-generating hypotheses we obtain smooth, strictly positive transition densities and a forward--backward characterization of the optimal bridge. This leads to a practical Sinkhorn-type algorithm for the Schrodinger potentials and, as the noise level vanishes, a recovery of the deterministic sub-Riemannian optimal transport problem. We demonstrate with a numerical example.

</details>


### [189] [Exactness of the DNN Relaxation for Random Standard Quadratic Programs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.11456)
*Xin Chen*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文研究标准二次优化问题的双非负（DNN）松弛，证明了在随机对称矩阵的独立对角/非对角元条件下，缺陷图各分支大小至多为4的概率趋于1，此时DNN松弛精确、全局解唯一且秩1，并给出了高斯正交系综下的显式概率下界及O(n²)恢复算法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究随机数据下标准二次优化问题的DNN松弛的精确性，旨在刻画DNN松弛何时能够精确求解这一NP-难问题，并给出高效恢复算法。

Method: 1) 利用负非对角元定义缺陷图G_n^-；2) 通过图论分析（每个≥5的连通分支必含5顶点树）证明缺陷分支大小至多4；3) 结合DNN与完全正定锥在维数≤4时等价的已知结论，证明每个局部松弛精确；4) 利用有限KKT候选点论证和绝对连续性证明全局解唯一且秩1。

Result: 概率趋于1时，DNN松弛的解唯一且秩1。对高斯正交系综，该事件概率至少为1 - K(ln n)²/n³。缺陷图构造和常数规模KKT求解可在O(n²)时间恢复精确解。尾条件适用于方差调谐高斯Wigner模型、重尾分布和有限下端点分布。

Conclusion: 在广泛的随机矩阵模型下，标准二次优化问题的DNN松弛以高概率精确且可高效求解，揭示了随机数据结构下的可解性条件。

Abstract: We study the doubly nonnegative (DNN) relaxation of the standard quadratic optimization problem \[ \min\{x^\top Qx:\ x\inΔ^{n-1}\},\qquad Δ^{n-1}:=\{x\in\R_+^n:\ \1^\top x=1\}, \] for random symmetric matrices with independent diagonal and off-diagonal entries. Let $m_n:=\min_{1\le i\le n} Q_{ii}$ and set $M:=Q-m_nE$, where $E$ is the all-ones matrix. The negative off-diagonal entries of $M$ define a defect graph $G_n^-$. Under entrywise independence, absolute continuity, and the tail-decay condition $n^5\E[F_O(m_n)^4]\to 0$, where $F_O$ is the off-diagonal distribution function, we prove that with probability tending to one every defect component has size at most $4$. On this event, the shifted DNN value decomposes over defect components. Since the DNN and completely positive cones coincide in dimensions at most four, each local relaxation is exact. A finite KKT-candidate argument gives local uniqueness, and absolute continuity rules out ties, so the global DNN optimizer is unique and rank one. The graph estimate uses the fact that every connected component of size at least five contains a tree on exactly five vertices. For Gaussian orthogonal ensemble data, we prove the explicit bound \[ \Prob\bigl(\text{the DNN optimizer is unique and rank one}\bigr) \ge 1-K\frac{(\ln n)^2}{n^3}. \] On the same event, the exact optimizer can be recovered in $O(n^2)$ time by constructing the defect graph and solving constant-size local KKT systems. We also verify the tail condition for variance-tuned Gaussian Wigner models, heavy-tailed laws, and finite-lower-endpoint laws.

</details>


### [190] [Boundary observability for gas giant metrics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.11620)
*Maarten V. de Hoop,Antti Kykkänen,Emmanuel Trélat*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文研究了气态巨行星流形（一类边界奇异的黎曼流形）上波的能观性，通过Neumann型边界迹建立了能观性不等式，证明分为两步：先通过摄动论证将一般情形归约为可分离情形，再利用均匀切向频率分析和Ingham不等式证明可分离情形下的能观性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 气态巨行星内部声波传播的数学建模需要处理边界奇异的黎曼度量，这类流形上的波动方程能观性尚未建立，而这是反问题与控制的数学基础。

Method: 1) 通过摄动论证将一般气态巨行星度量的能观性问题归约到可分离度量情形；2) 在可分离情形中，采用均匀切向频率分析结合Ingham不等式来建立能观性估计。

Result: 在气态巨行星流形上，利用Neumann型全边界测量迹，成功建立了波动方程的能观性不等式。

Conclusion: 该结果验证了气态巨行星流形上波动方程能观性的成立，为后续气态巨行星内部声波反问题及控制理论提供了关键的数学工具。

Abstract: We study the observability of waves on gas giant manifolds which are a class of Riemannian manifolds whose metrics are singular at the boundary. Such manifolds arise naturally in modeling of acoustic wave propagation in gas giant planets.We establish an observability inequality using full boundary measurements given by a Neumann-type trace that is natural in the gas giant setting. The proof proceeds in two steps. First, observability for a general gas giant metric is reduced to the so-called separable case via a perturbation argument. In the separable case, we employ a uniform-in-tangential-frequency analysis combined with an Ingham inequality to prove observability.

</details>


### [191] [Proximal Limited-Memory Quasi-Newton Methods for Nonsmooth Nonconvex Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.11627)
*Simeon vom Dahl,Alberto De Marchi,Christian Kanzow*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 提出了一种近端有限记忆拟牛顿方法，用于最小化光滑函数与非光滑函数的和，两者均可非凸。通过自适应正则化参数实现全局化，在温和假设下证明全局收敛，并在Kurdyka-Lojasiewicz性质下建立全序列收敛（含速率）。利用有限记忆拟牛顿更新的紧致表示高效求解子问题，并推导了有限记忆Kleinmichel公式的紧致表示。数值结果表明相比其他方法显著加速。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有近端拟牛顿方法多假设函数凸性，或缺乏对非凸问题的全局收敛性理论保证。为解决光滑+非光滑（可能非凸）组合优化问题，需要一种既能利用二阶信息加速、又能保证全局收敛的算法。

Method: 1) 基于缩放近端算子的近端有限记忆拟牛顿框架；2) 通过自适应更新正则化参数（基于充分下降准则）实现全局化；3) 利用有限记忆拟牛顿更新的紧致表示高效求解子问题；4) 推导了有限记忆Kleinmichel公式（一种保持正定性的秩一拟牛顿方案）的紧致表示。

Result: 在温和假设下证明了全局收敛性；在Kurdyka-Lojasiewicz性质下建立了全序列收敛及其收敛速率；数值实验表明相比其他方法有显著加速。

Conclusion: 该近端有限记忆拟牛顿方法能有效处理光滑+非光滑（可能非凸）的组合优化问题，兼具拟牛顿方法的加速效果和严格的全局收敛保证，数值性能优越。

Abstract: We introduce a proximal limited--memory quasi--Newton scheme for minimizing the sum of a continuously differentiable function and a proper, lower semicontinuous and prox-bounded, possibly nonsmooth, function. Both functions might be nonconvex. The method builds upon the computation of scaled proximal operators and is globalized by adaptively updating a regularization parameter based on a criterion of sufficient decrease. We prove global convergence under mild assumptions and then establish convergence of the entire sequence (with rates) under the Kurdyka--Lojasiewicz property. To efficiently solve the subproblems, we exploit the compact representation of limited-memory quasi-Newton updates. We derive also a compact representation of the limited--memory Kleinmichel formula, a rank-one quasi-Newton scheme that preserves positive definiteness under the same condition as the BFGS update. Numerical results show a significant speed up compared to other methods.

</details>


### [192] [Constrained Stochastic Spectral Preconditioning Converges for Nonconvex Objectives](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.11850)
*Konstantinos Oikonomidis,Jan Quan,Kimon Antonakopoulos,Antonio Silveti-Falls,Volkan Cevher,Panagiotis Patrinos*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文提出近端预条件梯度方法族，将Muon和Scion优化器扩展到近端设置，支持凸/非凸约束，分析重尾噪声下的收敛性，并提出方差缩减版本加速收敛。此外，证明Muon中的多项式迭代更接近非线性预条件子而非理想矩阵符号函数，从而给出更贴合实际实现的收敛分析。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有Muon和Scion优化器缺乏对约束处理的支持，且其理论分析基于理想矩阵符号函数，与实际实现中使用的多项式迭代存在差距；同时需要处理重尾噪声下的收敛性问题。

Method: 提出近端预条件谱梯度方法（Proximal Preconditioned Spectral Gradient Methods），扩展Muon和Scion到带约束场景；设计适应方法几何结构的新分析技术来处理重尾噪声；引入方差缩减版本提升标准噪声假设下的收敛速度；重新审视Muon的多项式迭代并建立非线性预条件子的理论框架。

Result: 在重尾噪声下证明了收敛性；方差缩减版本在标准噪声假设下取得更快收敛；揭示了Muon多项式迭代与非线性预条件子之间的准确关系，改进了理论分析与实际实现的一致性。

Conclusion: 提出的方法族统一了预条件梯度方法的近端扩展与谱梯度方法，为处理复杂约束和重尾噪声提供了理论基础，并为Muon类优化器建立了更符合实际的分析范式。

Abstract: In this work, we develop proximal preconditioned gradient methods with a focus on spectral gradient methods providing a proximal extension to the Muon and Scion optimizers. We introduce a family of stochastic algorithms that can handle a wide variety of convex and nonconvex constraints and study its convergence under heavy-tailed noise, through a novel analysis tailored to the geometry of the proposed methods. We further propose a variance-reduced version, which achieves faster convergence under standard noise assumptions. Finally, we show that the polynomial iterations used in Muon are more accurately captured by a nonlinear preconditioner than by the ideal matrix sign, leading to a convergence analysis that more faithfully reflects practical implementations.

</details>


### [193] [Convergence of zeroth-order proximal point algorithms in the high-temperature regime](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.11929)
*Emanuele Naldi,Hippolyte Labarrière,Cesare Molinari,Silvia Villa*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文针对非凸黑箱优化中的零阶近邻算子（ZOPO）及相应算法（ZOPPA）展开全面分析。以往研究依赖温度参数趋于零来获得理论保证，但该机制在采样估计时会导致蒙特卡洛估计器出现严重方差问题。本文提出在任意固定正温度下分析ZOPO，证明ZOPPA可被解释为对辅助平滑目标的精确近端点方法，而非原函数的不精确方法，并进一步建立了平滑问题与原目标之间的显式保证，以及固定温度下采样方法（S-ZOPPA）的收敛结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于零阶近邻算子的方法在理论上依赖温度参数趋于零以获得不精确近端点方法的保证，然而当通过采样估计ZOPO时，该机制会导致蒙特卡洛估计器方差过大，计算上不可持续。因此需要一种在固定正温度下仍能保证收敛的理论分析框架。

Method: 本文将ZOPPA重新解释为对辅助平滑目标的精确近端点方法（而非原函数的不精确方法），从而绕开温度趋于零的要求。在此基础上，推导了平滑问题与原目标函数之间的显式性能保证，并对固定温度下的采样版本S-ZOPPA建立了收敛性分析。

Result: 证明了在任意固定正温度下ZOPPA的收敛性，所需假设条件极弱；建立了平滑目标与原目标之间的显式理论联系；证明了采样方法S-ZOPPA在固定温度下的收敛结果。

Conclusion: 固定正温度下的ZOPPA和S-ZOPPA具有坚实的理论保证，避免了温度趋于零带来的计算问题，为非凸黑箱优化提供了更实用的理论工具。

Abstract: Efficient methods for non-convex black-box optimization largely rely on sampling. In this context, the Zeroth-Order Proximal Operator (ZOPO) and the corresponding Zeroth-Order Proximal Point Algorithm (ZOPPA) have attracted significant interest, as they combine the advantage of requiring only objective evaluations with the powerful theoretical framework of proximal point algorithms. ZOPO depends on a temperature parameter which, when going to zero, reduces ZOPO to the exact proximal operator. By exploiting this property, the vanishing-temperature regime has been leveraged in several works to obtain theoretical guarantees via inexact proximal methods. However, this regime is computationally unsustainable when sampling is used to estimate ZOPO, since the corresponding Monte Carlo estimators suffer from severe variance issues. We therefore propose a comprehensive analysis of ZOPO for any fixed positive temperature, and prove convergence of ZOPPA under minimal assumptions on the objective function. We do so by demonstrating that ZOPPA can be interpreted as an exact proximal point method applied to an auxiliary smoothed objective, rather than an inexact method on the original function. Importantly, we further derive explicit guarantees connecting this smoothed problem back to the original objective and establish convergence results for the sampled method (S-ZOPPA) at a fixed temperature.

</details>


### [194] [Generative Transfer for Entropic Optimal Transport with Unknown Costs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.11944)
*Antoine Debouchage,Xiaozhen Wang,Zhenjie Ren,Francois Buet-Golfouse*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种生成式迁移框架，通过迭代路径倾斜算法，从参考最优耦合样本中学习潜在的地面成本，从而为新边际分布恢复熵最优传输（EOT）耦合。该方法结合条件流匹配（CFM）生成配对数据样本，并证明了W_1距离下的全局收敛率O(δ)。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 熵最优传输中潜在地面成本函数通常是不可观测的，传统方法假设固定几何成本，而实际应用中成本是未知的。本文旨在从参考最优耦合的样本中学习潜在成本，并泛化到新的边际分布。

Method: 提出生成式迁移框架，使用迭代路径倾斜算法，沿边际传输路径联合演化耦合，使质量能够超出参考支撑集。推导了无穷小更新的样本级学习规则，得到传输向量场的协方差型演化方程，并与条件流匹配（CFM）结合生成配对数据采样器。

Result: 建立了全局收敛率O(δ)，确保生成的耦合在W_1距离下收敛到目标EOT计划。

Conclusion: 该方法有效解决了潜在成本下跨边际分布的最优耦合迁移问题，为EOT提供了一种数据驱动的实用解决方案，具有理论收敛保证。

Abstract: This paper addresses the practical challenge in Entropic Optimal Transport (EOT) where the underlying ground cost function is typically latent and unobserved. Rather than assuming a fixed geometric cost, we adopt a data-driven approach where a shared cost is revealed only through samples from a reference optimal coupling. The question is then: given samples from a reference optimal coupling, can we recover the optimal coupling for new marginals under the same latent cost? We introduce a generative transfer framework that recovers the optimal coupling for new marginals by utilizing an iterative path-wise tilting algorithm. Unlike static importance reweighting, this method evolves the coupling jointly with a marginal transport path, allowing mass to move beyond the reference support. We derive sample-level learning rules for these infinitesimal updates, which yield covariance-type evolution equations for the associated transport vector fields. By integrating this dynamics with Conditional Flow Matching (CFM), we produce a practical sampler for paired data. Finally, we provide theoretical guarantees establishing a global convergence rate of \mathcal{O}(δ), ensuring the generated coupling converges to the target EOT plan in W_1 distance.

</details>


### [195] [Stochastic control with self-exciting processes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.12035)
*Heidar Eyjolfsson,Kristina Rognlien Dahl*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文研究了带自激励随机过程的随机最优控制问题，应用随机最大值原理，推导了充分性最大值原理，给出了自激励过程及其二次变差的鞅表达式，推导了必要性最大值原理（等价原理），并应用于对数效用函数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 由于自激励随机过程驱动的SDE具有非马尔可夫性，经典的动态规划方法不再适用，因此需要采用随机最大值原理这一替代方法来解决该随机最优控制问题。

Method: 采用随机最大值原理（Stochastic Maximum Principle）框架，推导充分性条件；利用鞅方法给出自激励过程及其二次协变差的表达式；进一步推导必要性条件（等价原理）。

Result: 得到了在自激励随机过程框架下的充分随机最大值原理和必要随机最大值原理（等价原理），并给出了自激励过程及其二次协变差的鞅表示。

Conclusion: 所提出的随机最大值原理框架能够有效处理带有自激励过程的非马尔可夫随机最优控制问题，并通过对数效用函数的应用验证了理论结果的有效性。

Abstract: We analyze the problem of stochastic optimal control of SDEs where the driver includes a self-exciting stochastic process. Due to the non-Markovian nature of the problem, we apply the stochastic maximum principle approach. We derive a sufficient stochastic maximum principle under this framework. We also derive an expression via martingales of both the self-exciting process and its quadratic covariation. Furthermore, we derive a necessary maximum (equivalence principle) for the self-exciting stochastic control problem. Finally, we look at an application to log-utility.

</details>


### [196] [A Moment-QSOS Hierarchy for a Class of Quaternion Polynomial Optimization Problems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.12210)
*Yanqing Liu,Jie Wang*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种直接在四元数域上构建的矩-四元数-平方和（QSOS）层级结构，用于求解四元数多项式优化问题。该层级通过一系列半定规划（SDP）松弛提供单调递增的下界，并通过引入关联稀疏性提升大规模稀疏问题的可扩展性，同时提出一种加强的QSOS松弛以增强紧度。数值实验表明，该方法在保证与现有方法相当精度的前提下，显著降低了计算时间和内存消耗，并在四元数最大间隔准则和方向同步问题中验证了实际有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 四元数多项式优化问题在计算机视觉、机器人学等领域具有广泛应用，但现有方法通常将四元数问题转化为实数域求解，导致变量维度和约束数量膨胀，计算效率低下。本文旨在直接在四元数域构建优化框架，避免冗余转换的同时，解决大规模稀疏问题下的计算瓶颈问题。

Method: 提出Moment-QSOS层级结构，直接在四元数域构建半定规划松弛序列；引入关联稀疏性（correlative sparsity）利用问题的稀疏结构减少SDP规模；设计加强型QSOS松弛，通过可控方式扩展单项式基以提升松弛紧度。

Result: 数值实验表明，该方法在解质量上与现有方法相当，但在计算时间和内存使用上显著降低；在四元数最大间隔准则问题和方向同步问题中展示了实际有效性。

Conclusion: 本文的直接四元数域QSOS层级为四元数多项式优化提供了一种高效且可扩展的求解框架，关联稀疏性和加强松弛策略进一步提升了其实用性，在相关应用领域具有显著优势。

Abstract: This paper introduces a Moment-Quaternion-Sum-of-Squares (QSOS) hierarchy for solving a class of quaternion polynomial optimization problems. This hierarchy is formulated directly in the quaternion domain and consists of a sequence of semidefinite programming (SDP) relaxations that provide monotonic lower bounds on the optimal value. To improve scalability, we incorporate correlative sparsity, which can significantly reduce the size of the resulting SDPs for large-scale sparse problems. Furthermore, we introduce a strengthened QSOS relaxation, which enhances the tightness of the standard relaxation by enlarging the monomial basis in a controlled manner. Our various Numerical experiments show that our approach provides comparable bounds to existing approaches, while significantly reducing computation time and memory usage. In particular, applications to the quaternion-based maximum margin criterion problem and the classical orientation synchronization problem illustrate the practical effectiveness of the framework.

</details>


### [197] [Convergence Analysis of Hessian-Damped Tikhonov Regularized Dynamics with Oscillation Control for Convex-Concave Bilinear Saddle Point Problems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.12223)
*Bohan Zhang,Xiaojun Zhang*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文提出一类带有Tikhonov正则化和Hessian驱动阻尼的广义二阶原始-对偶动力系统，用于求解凸-凹双线性鞍点问题，证明了轨迹强收敛到最小范数解，并验证了Hessian驱动阻尼可有效缓解振荡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为解决凸-凹双线性鞍点问题，设计具有更好收敛性质的动力系统，特别是实现强收敛到最小范数解并抑制振荡。

Method: 构建包含粘性阻尼、时间缩放、外推、Tikhonov正则化和Hessian驱动阻尼五个时变参数的二阶原始-对偶动力系统，通过构造合适的Lyapunov函数进行渐近收敛性分析。

Result: 获得了原始-对偶间隙的收敛率及轨迹的有界性，提供了积分估计，理论上证明了轨迹强收敛到最小范数解，并展示了Hessian驱动阻尼可有效缓解振荡。

Conclusion: 提出的广义二阶动力系统能有效求解凸-凹双线性鞍点问题，Hessian驱动阻尼在抑制振荡方面具有显著优势，数值实验验证了理论结果的有效性。

Abstract: In this paper, we propose a class of general second-order primal-dual dynamical systems with Tikhonov regularization and Hessian-driven damping for solving convex-concave bilinear saddle point problems. The proposed dynamical system incorporates five general time-varying terms: viscous damping, time scaling, extrapolation, Tikhonov regularization, and Hessian-driven damping parameters. Under suitable parametric conditions, we analyze the asymptotic convergence properties of the dynamical system by constructing appropriate Lyapunov functions. Specifically, we obtain the convergence rate of the primal-dual gap and the boundedness of trajectories in the proposed dynamical system, and provide some integral estimates. Furthermore, we theoretically prove that the trajectories generated by the dynamical system converge strongly to the minimum-norm solution of the saddle point problem, and fully demonstrate that Hessian-driven damping can effectively alleviate oscillations. Finally, numerical experiments are conducted to verify the validity of the above theoretical results.

</details>


### [198] [Geometric Asymptotics of Score Mixing and Guidance in Diffusion Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.12231)
*Kang Liu,Enrique Zuazua*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文从数学上严格分析了混合分数引导的扩散模型在小时间尺度下的生成动力学行为，通过拉普拉斯-瓦拉丹原理将奇异、非自治的分数驱动动力学约化为自治的克拉克次梯度包含，揭示了生成动力学由显式几何势函数 Φ_λ=λd_1^2+(1-λ)d_2^2 控制，该势函数仅依赖于初始测度的支撑集和混合参数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 扩散模型在实践中常通过组合多个分数场进行引导，但分数混合的数学结构至今尚不清晰。本文旨在建立混合分数引导下小时间生成动力学的严格数学理论，统一理解混合专家（MoE）和无分类器引导（CFG）两种范式。

Method: 在热流框架下研究由 s=λ∇log u_1+(1-λ)∇log u_2 定义的混合分数驱动动力学，其中 u_1,u_2 为两个紧支撑概率测度的热演化。利用相似性时间重缩放下拉普拉斯-瓦拉丹原理，将奇异非自治系统约化为自治的克拉克次梯度包含，并分析有限狄拉克混合下的Voronoi结构。

Result: 1) 小时间生成动力学由几何势函数 Φ_λ=λd_1^2+(1-λ)d_2^2 控制；2) 实现了从奇异非自治分数驱动到自治克拉克次梯度包含的严格约化；3) 有限狄拉克混合下势函数为分段二次的Voronoi型结构；4) 所有自治极限轨迹收敛到临界点；5) 原始生成流有条件地收敛到势函数的局部极小值，光滑稳定情形下收敛率为 O(√t)。

Conclusion: 本文为混合分数引导的扩散模型提供了严格的数学基础，揭示了其小时间生成动力学的几何本质，统一了混合专家和无分类器引导两种实践范式，并为理解分数混合的动力学行为提供了理论工具。

Abstract: Diffusion models are routinely guided in practice by combining multiple score fields, yet the mathematical structure of score mixing is still poorly understood. We study the small-time generation dynamics driven by mixed scores $$ s=λ\,\nabla\log u_1+(1-λ)\,\nabla\log u_2,\qquad λ\ge 0, $$ in the heat-flow framework, where $u_1,u_2$ are heat evolutions of two compactly supported probability measures. This single formulation covers both the mixture-of-experts regime $(0\leq λ\leq 1)$ and the classifier-free guidance regime $(λ>1)$. Exploiting a Laplace-Varadhan principle under a similarity-time rescaling, we show that the small-time generation dynamics is governed by the explicit geometric potential $$ Φ_λ=λd_1^2+(1-λ)d_2^2, $$ which depends only on the supports of the initial measures and on the mixing parameter. This gives a rigorous reduction from a singular, non-autonomous score-driven dynamics to autonomous Clarke-type subgradient inclusions. In the empirical setting of finite Dirac mixtures, the limiting potential is piecewise quadratic with a Voronoi-type structure; this rigidity yields convergence of all autonomous limiting trajectories to critical points and a conditional convergence criterion for the original generation flow toward local minimizers of the potential, with rate $\mathcal O(\sqrt t)$ in the smooth stable case.

</details>


### [199] [Stochastic block coordinate and function alternation for multi-objective optimization and learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.12432)
*Trang H. Tran,Luis Nunes Vicente*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 提出了一种交替优化多目标的框架，通过交替优化每个目标并对每个变量块进行（随机）梯度更新，降低每步计算成本，同时保证收敛性，并在多目标回归等任务中优于非交替方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多目标优化中，现有的基于多梯度的优化方法每步需计算所有目标对所有变量的梯度，在大规模场景下计算成本高昂。

Method: 提出一个交替优化框架，同时交替优化每个目标和对每个变量块进行（随机）梯度更新，通过为每个目标分配指定数量的梯度步来探索Pareto前沿。

Result: 在凸、非凸和Polyak-Lojasiewicz条件下建立了严格的收敛保证，恢复经典单目标方法的收敛率；数值实验表明在多目标回归任务中优于非交替方法，生成具有竞争力的Pareto前沿近似。

Conclusion: 该框架在降低计算成本的同时保持了收敛性能，具有良好的计算效率和实际有效性。

Abstract: Multi-objective optimization is central to many engineering and machine learning applications, where multiple objectives must be optimized in balance. While multi-gradient based optimization methods combine these objectives in each step, such methods require computing gradients with respect to all variables at every iteration, resulting in high computational costs in large-scale settings. In this work, we propose a framework that simultaneously alternates the optimization of each objective and the (stochastic) gradient update with respect to each variable block. Our framework reduces per-iteration computational cost while enabling exploration of the Pareto front by allocating a prescribed number of gradient steps to each objective. We establish rigorous convergence guarantees across several stochastic smooth settings, including convex, non-convex, and Polyak-Lojasiewicz conditions, recovering classical convergence rates of single-objective methods. Numerical experiments demonstrate that our framework outperforms non-alternating methods on multi-target regression and produces a competitive Pareto front approximation, highlighting its computational efficiency and practical effectiveness.

</details>


<div id='cs.AI'></div>

# cs.AI [[Back]](#toc)

### [200] [A Cascaded Generative Approach for e-Commerce Recommendations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.11118)
*Moein Hasani,Hamidreza Shahidi,Trace Levinson,Yuan Zhong,Guanghua Shu,Vinesh Gudla,Tejaswi Tenneti*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出了一种级联商品陈列框架，将电商个性化店铺首页构建分解为两阶段生成任务：版位主题生成和约束关键词生成，结合师生微调提升可扩展性，并与传统排序模型融合，在线实验获得+2.7%加购率提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统个性化店铺首页由多个独立组件（静态主题、检索系统、点式排序器）拼装而成，这种范式僵化，难以实现页面级语义连贯性和动态目标优化，也难以及时响应不断变化的商品陈列需求。

Method: 将店铺首页构建分解为两个生成任务：(i) 版位级主题生成 (ii) 每个版位的约束关键词生成以驱动产品检索；采用师生微调（teacher-student fine-tuning）提升生产环境下的可扩展性；引入AI驱动的内容评估和质量过滤框架实现安全自动化部署；将生成输出与传统排序模型融合以保持混合架构。

Result: 微调模型消融实验表明其性能可接近闭源大语言模型；在线实验中，该框架相比强基线在每次页面浏览的加购量上实现了+2.7%的提升。

Conclusion: 级联生成框架能有效打破传统独立组件范式的局限性，在保持混合基础设施兼容性的同时显著提升个性化效果和动态适应性，支持大规模安全自动化部署。

Abstract: Personalized storefronts in large e-commerce marketplaces are often assembled from many independent components: static themes per page section ("placement"), retrieval systems to fetch eligible products per placement, and pointwise rankers to order content. While effective in optimizing for aggregate preferences, this paradigm is rigid and can limit personalization and semantic cohesion across the page. This makes it poorly suited to support dynamic objectives and merchandising requirements over time. To address this, we introduce a cascaded merchandising framework that decomposes storefront construction into two generative tasks: (i) placement-level theme generation and (ii) constrained keyword generation per placement to power product retrieval. Teacher-student fine-tuning is leveraged to improve scalability of this framework under production latency and cost constraints. Fine-tuned model ablations are shown to approach closed-weight LLM performance. We further contribute frameworks for AI-driven content evaluation and quality filtering, enabling safe and automated deployment of dynamic content at scale. Generative output is fused with traditional ranking models to preserve hybrid infrastructure. In online experiments, this framework yields an estimated +2.7% lift in cart adds per page view over a strong baseline.

</details>


### [201] [EVOCHAMBER: Test-Time Co-evolution of Multi-Agent System at Individual, Team, and Population Scales](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.11136)
*Yaolun Zhang,Tianyi Xu,Shengyu Dai,Zhenwen Shao,Qingyun Wu,Huazheng Wang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: EVOCHAMBER是一个无需训练的多智能体测试时进化框架，通过三级进化（个体级CODREAM反思、团队级在线组装、种群级生命周期管理）实现非对称知识流动，在数学、代码和多领域推理任务上显著超越基线，并涌现出稳定的专业分化现象。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有测试时方法要么将经验限制在单个智能体内（丧失跨智能体学习），要么对称广播给所有智能体（抹除专业化分工），缺乏真正的多智能体进化机制。

Method: 提出EVOCHAMBER框架，包含三个层次：(1) 个体级CODREAM——团队失败或分歧后的后任务协议，智能体协作反思、提取洞察，并从强智能体向弱智能体非对称传递知识；(2) 团队级——在线组建基于失败领域的团队并选择协作结构；(3) 种群级——在性能压力下进行智能体的分叉、合并、剪枝和播种。

Result: 在Qwen3-8B上，竞赛数学达63.9%，代码达75.7%，多领域推理达87.1%；数学任务相对最优基线提升32%；消融实验证实非对称跨智能体迁移是主要驱动因素；从相同初始化的智能体开始，自发涌现4-5个稳定的领域专家。

Conclusion: 多智能体测试时进化不仅仅是单智能体进化的N次重复，其独特的协作结构演化和非对称知识流动机制能够产生单智能体无法表达的结构性现象（如专业分化），在异构任务上效果显著。

Abstract: We argue that multi-agent test-time evolution is not single-agent evolution replicated N times. A single-agent learner can only evolve its own context and memory. A multi-agent system additionally evolves who collaborates, how they collaborate, and how knowledge flows across the population. These components have no single-agent counterpart and can produce phenomena such as emergent specialization. Yet prior test-time methods either confine experiences to individual agents, forfeiting cross-agent learning, or broadcast symmetrically to all agents, erasing the specialization that makes collaboration valuable. We present EVOCHAMBER, a training-free framework that instantiates test-time evolution at three levels over a coevolving agent pool. At its core is CODREAM (Collaborative Dreaming), a post-task protocol triggered on team failure or disagreement, in which agents collaboratively reflect, distill insights, and route them asymmetrically from strong to weak agents on the failed niche, preserving specialization while filling knowledge gaps. Team-level operators assemble niche-conditioned teams and select collaboration structures online. Population-level lifecycle operators fork, merge, prune, and seed agents under performance pressure. On three heterogeneous task streams with Qwen3-8B, EVOCHAMBER reaches 63.9% on competition math, 75.7% on code, and 87.1% on multi-domain reasoning, outperforming the best baseline by 32% relative on math and confirming asymmetric cross-agent transfer as the primary driver in ablation. Starting from several identically initialized agents, four to five stable niche specialists spontaneously emerge, a structural signature of multi-agent evolution that no single-agent learner can express. See our code at: https://github.com/Mercury7353/EvoChamber

</details>


### [202] [RankQ: Offline-to-Online Reinforcement Learning via Self-Supervised Action Ranking](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.11151)
*Andrew Choi,Wei Xu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: RankQ提出了一种自监督的多项排序损失函数，通过学习相对动作偏好而非统一惩罚OOD动作，改进了离线到在线强化学习中的Q函数学习，在D4RL基准和视觉机器人学习中取得了显著性能提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 离线到在线强化学习中，在大状态-动作空间且数据集覆盖有限的情况下学习精确的critic是一个关键挑战。现有方法通过悲观主义下加权OOD动作来抑制价值高估，但这本质上相当于行为克隆锚点，当数据集动作次优时会阻碍后续在线策略改进。

Method: 提出RankQ目标，在时序差分学习基础上增加自监督的多项排序损失，通过学习相对动作偏好而非统一惩罚未见过的动作来塑造Q函数，使动作梯度指向更高质量的行为。

Result: 在稀疏奖励D4RL基准上，RankQ性能达到或优于七种先前方法。在视觉机器人学习中，低数据场景下仿真成功率达42.7%的提升；高数据场景下提升13.7%，并将真实世界堆叠方块成功率从43.1%提升至84.7%。

Conclusion: RankQ通过结构化动作排序学习替代统一悲观惩罚，有效改善了离线到在线强化学习中的策略优化，在仿真和真实机器人任务中均展现出优异性能。

Abstract: Offline-to-online reinforcement learning (RL) improves sample efficiency by leveraging pre-collected datasets prior to online interaction. A key challenge, however, is learning an accurate critic in large state--action spaces with limited dataset coverage. To mitigate harmful updates from value overestimation, prior methods impose pessimism by down-weighting out-of-distribution (OOD) actions relative to dataset actions. While effective, this essentially acts as a behavior cloning anchor and can hinder downstream online policy improvement when dataset actions are suboptimal. We propose RankQ, an offline-to-online Q-learning objective that augments temporal-difference learning with a self-supervised multi-term ranking loss to enforce structured action ordering. By learning relative action preferences rather than uniformly penalizing unseen actions, RankQ shapes the Q-function such that action gradients are directed toward higher-quality behaviors. Across sparse reward D4RL benchmarks, RankQ achieves performance competitive with or superior to seven prior methods. In vision-based robot learning, RankQ enables effective offline-to-online fine-tuning of a pretrained vision-language-action (VLA) model in a low-data regime, achieving on average a 42.7% higher simulation success rate than the next best method. In a high-data setting, RankQ improves simulation performance by 13.7% over the next best method and achieves strong sim-to-real transfer, increasing real-world cube stacking success from 43.1% to 84.7% relative to the VLA's initial performance.

</details>


### [203] [OLIVIA: Online Learning via Inference-time Action Adaptation for Decision Making in LLM ReAct Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.11169)
*Sheldon Yu,Junda Wu,Xintong Li,Nikki Lijing Kuang,Sizhe Zhou,Tong Yu,Jiawei Han,Jingbo Shang,Julian McAuley*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出OLIVIA框架，将ReAct风格LLM智能体的动作选择层建模为上下文线性bandit，实现部署时的在线动作级自适应。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM智能体部署时依赖提示或检索进行推理时自适应，缺乏显式决策层来评分候选动作、表示不确定性或从动作级反馈在线更新，导致小错误累积、工具调用浪费和可靠性下降。

Method: 将LLM最终动作选择层建模为上下文线性bandit，以冻结的隐藏状态作为决策上下文；采用上置信界(UCB)探索策略，实现样本高效的在线策略更新。

Result: 在四个基准上，OLIVIA在任务性能上一致优于静态ReAct和基于提示的推理时基线方法。

Conclusion: 显式在线决策层为LLM智能体部署时提供了一种优于纯提示/检索自适应的有效替代方案。

Abstract: Large language model agents interleave reasoning, action selection, and observation to solve sequential decision-making tasks. In deployed settings where agents repeatedly handle related multi-step tasks, small action-selection errors can accumulate into wasted tool calls, latency, and reduced reliability. Despite this need for deployment-time improvement, existing inference-time adaptation methods for LLM agents mainly rely on prompting or retrieval, which influence behavior indirectly through context manipulation. For ReAct-style agents, such approaches do not expose an explicit decision layer that can score candidate actions, represent uncertainty, or be updated online from action-level feedback. As a result, they provide limited support for trackable, fine-grained, and uncertainty-aware adaptation during deployment. We propose OLIVIA, an inference-time action adaptation framework for ReAct-style agents. OLIVIA models the LLM's final action-selection layer as a contextual linear bandit over candidate actions, with frozen hidden states as decision contexts. This choice is particularly suitable for deployment because it adapts behavior directly at the action-selection interface, preserves the underlying reasoning process, and provides explicit uncertainty estimates and lightweight online updates from action-level feedback. With upper-confidence-bound exploration, OLIVIA improves the policy sample-efficiently with minimal computational overhead. We instantiate OLIVIA on four benchmarks and show that it consistently improves task performance over static ReAct and prompt-based inference-time baselines. Our results suggest that explicit online decision layers provide an effective alternative to purely prompt- or retrieval-based adaptation for LLM agents during deployment.

</details>


### [204] [The Many Faces of On-Policy Distillation: Pitfalls, Mechanisms, and Fixes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.11182)
*Siqi Zhu,Xuyan Ye,Hongyu Lu,Weiye Shi,Ge Liu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该论文系统研究了on-policy蒸馏(OPD)和on-policy自蒸馏(OPSD)在大语言模型后训练中的效果，发现OPD对教师模型选择和损失函数高度敏感，OPSD在缺乏实例特定特权信息时失效。论文识别了三种失败机制：师生分布不匹配、TopK反向KL梯度的优化不稳定性、以及OPSD特有的策略聚合问题，并提出了相应的缓解方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有关于OPD和OPSD有效性的研究结果不一致，既有成功的案例（如系统提示和知识内化），也有不稳定和性能退化的报告，需要系统性地理解它们何时有效、何时失效及其根本原因。

Method: 通过大规模实证研究，分析OPD和OPSD在不同场景下的表现，识别失败机制，并验证三种缓解策略（stop-gradient TopK目标、RLVR-adapted教师、SFT-stabilized学生）。

Result: OPD在数学推理任务上对教师选择和损失函数高度敏感；OPSD在缺乏实例特定特权信息时失败，但在特权信息表示共享潜在规则（如系统提示或对齐偏好）时有效。识别了三种失败机制。

Conclusion: stop-gradient TopK目标、RLVR-adapted教师和SFT-stabilized学生可以有效缓解OPD和OPSD的失败问题。

Abstract: On-policy distillation (OPD) and on-policy self-distillation (OPSD) have emerged as promising post-training methods for large language models, offering dense token-level supervision on trajectories sampled from the model's own policy. However, existing results on their effectiveness remain mixed: while OP(S)D has shown promise in system prompt and knowledge internalization, recent studies also report instability and degradation. In this work, we present a comprehensive empirical study of when OPD and OPSD work, when they fail, and why. We find that OPD on mathematical reasoning is highly sensitive to teacher choice and loss formulation, whereas OPSD fails in our tested settings due to test-time absence of instance-specific privileged information (PI). In contrast, OPSD is effective when PI represents a shared latent rule, such as a system prompt or alignment preference. We identify three failure mechanisms: (1) distribution mismatch between teacher and student caused by conditioning on student-generated prefixes, (2) optimization instability from biased TopK reverse-KL gradients, and (3) an OPSD-specific limitation where the student learns a PI-free policy that aggregates PI-conditioned teachers, which is insufficient when PI is instance-specific. We further show that stop-gradient TopK objectives, RLVR-adapted teachers, and SFT-stabilized students mitigate these failures.

</details>


### [205] [Don't Look at the Numbers: Visual Anchoring Bias and Layer-wise Representation in VLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.11218)
*M. Shalankin*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 嵌入在图像上的数字锚点会系统性偏置视觉-语言模型的质量判断，其影响幅度是严重图像质量退化的2.5倍。通过逐层探针分析发现，锚点分类饱和层（L12-L34）对质量预测是次优的，而更深的层表现更优（R²=0.69-0.91）。融合分析揭示了不同架构间差异化的锚点-质量特征整合模式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究视觉-语言模型中存在的视觉锚定偏差现象的因果机制，验证锚点效应是否可归因于简单的视觉变化，并揭示其背后的表征动力学过程。

Method: 在六种VLM（来自五个架构家族）上嵌入数字锚点，使用ANOVA分析效应量；通过逐层探针（layer-wise probing）分析锚点分类与质量预测的层间关系；采用融合分析（fusion analysis）考察锚点与质量特征的整合模式。

Result: 锚点效应量（eta²=0.18-0.77）是严重图像质量退化的2.5倍；锚点分类饱和层（L12-L34）对质量预测是次优的，最佳质量预测层更深（R²=0.69-0.91）；融合分析显示两种模型在L1-L2即实现即时融合，另三种模型为部分融合或无融合。

Conclusion: 建立了视觉锚定偏差的因果解释，将行为层面的偏差易感性与模型内部的表征动力学联系起来。

Abstract: Embedded numeric anchors on images systematically bias Vision-Language Model quality judgments across six VLMs from five architectural families (ANOVA eta^2 = 0.18-0.77, all p < 0.001). Anchor effects are 2.5x larger than severe image quality degradation, confirming bias is not reducible to visual changes. Layer-wise probing reveals consistent dissociation: layers where anchor classification saturates (L12-L34) are suboptimal for quality prediction, with optimal layers deeper (R^2 = 0.69-0.91). Fusion analysis identifies architecture-dependent integration -- instant fusion at L1-L2 in two models versus partial or no fusion in three others. These results establish a causal account of visual anchoring bias, linking behavioral susceptibility to representation dynamics.

</details>


### [206] [Do Vision-Language-Models show human-like logical problem-solving capability in point and click puzzle games?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.11223)
*Dominik Helfenstein,Marco Menner,Maximilian Triebel*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了VLATIM基准测试，用于评估视觉语言模型在经典物理解谜游戏《不可思议的机器2》中的人性化逻辑推理与精确交互能力。实验发现，大型专有模型虽具备较强规划能力，但在视觉精确定位方面存在显著短板，尚未达到人类水平的问题解决能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基准测试往往忽略了点击式解谜游戏中所需的复杂物理推理能力，存在高级逻辑推理与连续动作空间（需精确鼠标交互）之间的关键鸿沟。

Method: 基于经典物理解谜游戏《不可思议的机器2》构建VLATIM基准，设计五个渐进式评估部分，涵盖基础视觉定位、领域理解、多步操作到完整解谜能力。

Result: 大型专有模型在规划能力上表现优越，但在精确视觉定位上存在显著困难，推理与执行之间存在明显差距，尚未展现类人级问题解决能力。

Conclusion: 当前VLM在物理解谜任务中的逻辑推理与精确执行能力仍存在显著差距，尤其体现在视觉精确定位方面，距实现人类水平的交互式问题解决还有较大距离。

Abstract: Vision-Language(-Action) Models (VLMs) are increasingly applied to interactive environments, yet existing benchmarks often overlook the complex physical reasoning required for point-and-click puzzle games. This paper introduces Vision-Language Against The Incredible Machine (VLATIM), a benchmark designed to evaluate human-like logical problem-solving capabilities within the classic physics puzzle game The Incredible Machine 2 (TIM). Unlike existing benchmarks, VLATIM specifically targets the critical gap between high-level logical reasoning and continuous action spaces requiring precise mouse interactions. This benchmark is structured into five progressive parts, assessing capabilities that range from basic visual grounding and domain understanding to multi-step manipulation and full puzzle solving. Our results reveal a significant disparity between reasoning and execution. While large proprietary models demonstrate superior planning abilities, they struggle with precise visual grounding. Consequently, they do not yet show human-like problem-solving capabilities.

</details>


### [207] [PIVOT: Bridging Planning and Execution in LLM Agents via Trajectory Refinement](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.11225)
*Tuo Zhang,Alin-Ionut Popa,Yan Xu,Rui Song,Dimitrios Dimitriadis*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: PIVOT提出了一种自监督的轨迹优化框架，通过规划-检查-进化-验证四个阶段迭代改进智能体生成的轨迹，有效缓解了LLM智能体中计划与执行之间的不一致问题，在DeepPlanning和GAIA上实现了SOTA性能，且计算效率更高。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基于LLM的智能体经常生成看似合理的计划，但在实际执行中因动作不可行、约束违反以及长期任务中的累积错误而失败，存在严重的计划-执行不匹配问题。

Method: PIVOT包含四个阶段：PLAN（生成候选轨迹）、INSPECT（执行轨迹并计算结构化的文本梯度损失）、EVOLVE（利用反馈信号改进轨迹）、VERIFY（最终全局约束检查），并采用单调接受机制确保解质量不下降。

Result: 在DeepPlanning和GAIA上达到SOTA：HITL模式下约束满足度相对提升达94%；纯自主变体仍保持显著增益；token消耗比竞争方法少3到5倍。

Conclusion: （自监督或人工监督的）基于反馈的轨迹优化是缓解自主智能体系统中计划-执行差距的一种原理性方法。

Abstract: Large language model (LLM)-based agents frequently generate seemingly coherent plans that fail upon execution due to infeasible actions, constraint violations, and compounding errors over extended horizons. PIVOT (Plan-Inspect-eVOlve Trajectories) addresses this plan-execution misalignment through a self-supervised framework that treats trajectories as optimizable objects iteratively refined via environment interaction. The framework comprises four stages: PLAN generates candidate trajectories; INSPECT executes them and computes structured losses with textual gradients encoding plan-execution discrepancies; EVOLVE applies these signals to produce improved trajectories; and VERIFY performs a final global check against task constraints. A monotonic acceptance process ensures a non-decreasing solution quality. Empirical evaluations on DeepPlanning and GAIA demonstrate state-of-the-art performance: with human-in-the-loop (HITL) feedback, PIVOT establishes a strong upper bound up to 94% relative improvement in constraint satisfaction, while its fully autonomous variant retains substantial gains, showing that the core trajectory-refinement mechanism remains effective without external supervision. At the same time, PIVOT remains computationally efficient, requiring up to 3x to 5x fewer tokens than competing refinement methods. These findings establish that (self- or human-supervised) feedback-based trajectory optimization is a principled methodology for mitigating plan-execution gaps in autonomous agent systems.

</details>


### [208] [The Semantic Training Gap: Ontology-Grounded Tool Architectures for Industrial AI Agent Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.11234)
*Grama Chethan*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文发现大模型驱动的AI智能体在制造业中存在"语义训练鸿沟"——虽能流利使用领域术语，但缺乏对设备标识、工艺参数、故障代码等操作语义的结构化理解。为此提出一种将制造本体直接嵌入AI工具层的架构，通过"解析-语境化-标注"三操作接口和AIOps编排层在运行时强制语义约束。实验表明，未约束的工具参数产生43%的领域标识符幻觉，本体约束后降至0%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM在制造业中虽能流利使用术语，但缺乏对操作语义（设备标识、工艺参数、故障代码与监管约束之间的本体关系结构）的扎根理解，导致输出在语言学上精准但在操作上错误，且在多智能体配置中产生"语义漂移"这一复合失效模式。

Method: 提出一种将制造本体直接嵌入AI工具层的架构，将本体作为类型化关系配置在运行时强制语义约束，而非依赖模型训练。该架构形式化为三个操作接口契约：resolve（解析）、contextualize（语境化）、annotate（标注），由AIOps编排层强制执行不变量。跨6个行业配置、72次工具调用（Qwen3-32B）进行对照实验验证。

Result: 未约束的工具参数产生43%的领域标识符幻觉率；经本体约束后幻觉率降至0%。通过数字孪生分析平台验证：单一代码库配合领域特定本体配置即可消除工具调用幻觉，并在无需修改应用代码的前提下实现跨领域可配置性。

Conclusion: 将制造本体嵌入AI工具层作为运行时语义约束，能有效弥合LLM在制造业中的语义训练鸿沟，消除工具调用幻觉，并实现跨领域适配而无需变更应用代码。

Abstract: Large language model (LLM)-based AI agents are increasingly deployed in manufacturing environments for analytics, quality management, and decision support. These agents demonstrate statistical fluency with domain terminology but lack grounded understanding of operational semantics -- the relational structure that connects equipment identifiers, process parameters, failure codes, and regulatory constraints within a specific production context. This paper identifies and formalizes the semantic training gap: a structural disconnect between how AI systems acquire domain vocabulary through training and how manufacturing operations define meaning through ontological relationships. We demonstrate that this gap causes operationally incorrect outputs even when model responses are linguistically precise, and that in multi-agent configurations it produces a compounding failure mode we term semantic drift. To close this gap, we present an architecture that embeds manufacturing ontology directly into the AI tool layer as a typed relational configuration, enforcing semantic constraints at runtime rather than relying on model training. The architecture is formalized as a three-operation interface contract -- resolve, contextualize, annotate -- with invariants enforced by an AIOps orchestration layer. In a controlled experiment across six industry configurations (72 tool invocations using Qwen3-32B), unconstrained tool parameters produced a 43% hallucination rate for domain identifiers; ontology-grounded parameters reduced this to 0%. We validate the approach through a digital twin analytics platform demonstrating that a single codebase with domain-specific ontology configurations eliminates tool-call hallucination and achieves cross-domain configurability without application code changes.

</details>


### [209] [Unlocking LLM Creativity in Science through Analogical Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.11258)
*Andrew Shen,Shaul Druckmann,James Zou*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出类比推理方法（AR）解决LLM在开放性问题解决中模式崩溃（生成低多样性方案）的问题。AR通过跨领域类比生成新颖多样化方案，在多个生物医学任务上取得显著提升：多样性指标提升90-173%，新颖方案生成率超50%（基线最低仅1.6%），并在扰动效应预测、细胞通讯预测、脑区交互推断和寡核苷酸性质预测等四个实际生物医学问题中验证了有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自主科学需要在开放性问题中持续生成新颖多样的解决方案，但现有LLM存在模式崩溃问题（生成方案趋于同质化、多样性低），限制了其在复杂生物医学领域的应用潜力。

Method: 提出类比推理（AR）方法：首先生成与目标问题共享关系结构的跨领域类比，然后利用这些类比搜索新颖解决方案，从而突破单一领域搜索空间的局限。

Result: 多样性指标提升90-173%；新颖方案生成率超过50%（基线最低仅1.6%）；在四个生物医学应用中：扰动效应预测提升近13倍分布指标、细胞通讯预测AUPRC超越所有基线、脑区交互推断与已有方法高相关（ρ=0.729）、寡核苷酸性质预测在两个数据集上达到SOTA。

Conclusion: AR生成的新颖多样化方案可以有效扩充现有方案生成方法的搜索空间，为自主科学中AI系统的开放性创新能力提升提供了可行路径。

Abstract: Autonomous science promises to augment scientific discovery, particularly in complex fields like biomedicine. However, this requires AI systems that can consistently generate novel and diverse solutions to open-ended problems. We evaluate LLMs on the task of open-ended solution generation and quantify their tendency to mode collapse into low-diversity generations. To mitigate this mode collapse, we introduce analogical reasoning (AR) as a new approach to solution generation. AR generates analogies to cross-domain problems based on shared relational structure, then uses those analogies to search for novel solutions. Compared to baselines, AR discovers significantly more diverse generations (improving solution diversity metrics by 90-173%), generates novel solutions over 50% of the time (compared to as little as 1.6% for baselines), and produces high-quality analogies. To validate the real-world feasibility of AR, we implement AR-generated solutions across four biomedical problems, yielding consistent quantitative gains. AR-generated approaches achieve a nearly 13-fold improvement on distributional metrics for perturbation effect prediction, outperform all baselines on AUPRC when predicting cell-cell communication, infer brain region interactions with a high Spearman correlation ($ρ$=0.729) to published methods, and establish state-of-the-art performance on 2 datasets for oligonucleotide property prediction. The novel and diverse solutions produced by AR can be used to augment the search space of existing solution generation methods.

</details>


### [210] [LatentRouter: Can We Choose the Right Multimodal Model Before Seeing Its Answer?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.11301)
*Xueqi Cheng,Yushun Dong*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: LatentRouter 是一种将多模态大语言模型路由问题形式化为反事实多模态效用预测的方法。它通过提取多模态路由胶囊、为每个候选模型分配能力标记、进行潜在状态通信来估计各模型的表现，并采用分布性结果头和边界胶囊校正机制实现性能导向和性能-成本导向的路由。实验表明其在 MMR-Bench 和 VL-RouterBench 上优于基线方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多模态大语言模型在 OCR、图表理解、空间推理、视觉问答、成本和延迟等方面具有异构优势，有效的路由不仅需要估计查询难度，还需要匹配图像-问题输入的多模态需求与各候选模型的特定能力。

Method: LatentRouter 将 MLLM 路由构建为反事实多模态效用预测问题。具体包括：(1) 提取学习到的多模态路由胶囊；(2) 为每个候选 MLLM 分配模型能力标记；(3) 在状态间进行潜在通信以估计各模型若被选中的表现；(4) 使用分布性结果头预测模型特定的反事实质量；(5) 采用边界胶囊校正机制优化临界决策，防止残差信号主导预测。路由策略支持性能导向和性能-成本导向路由，并通过共享的逐模型评分与可用性掩码处理候选模型池的变化。

Result: 在 MMR-Bench 和 VL-RouterBench 上，LatentRouter 优于固定模型、特征级和基于学习路由器的基线方法。增益在依赖视觉、布局敏感或推理导向需求的多模态任务组中最显著，潜在通信是主要贡献因素。

Conclusion: LatentRouter 通过反事实效用预测和潜在通信机制，有效实现了多模态大语言模型的路由选择，在多种路由场景下均取得了优于现有方法的性能，且具有较好的可扩展性。

Abstract: Multimodal large language models (MLLMs) have heterogeneous strengths across OCR, chart understanding, spatial reasoning, visual question answering, cost, and latency. Effective MLLM routing therefore requires more than estimating query difficulty: a router must match the multimodal requirements of the current image-question input with the capabilities of each candidate model. We propose LatentRouter, a router that formulates MLLM routing as counterfactual multimodal utility prediction. Given an image-question query, LatentRouter extracts learned multimodal routing capsules, represents each candidate MLLM with a model capability token, and performs latent communication between these states to estimate how each model would perform if selected. A distributional outcome head predicts model-specific counterfactual quality, while a bounded capsule correction refines close decisions without allowing residual signals to dominate the prediction. The resulting utility-based policy supports performance-oriented and performance-cost routing, and handles changing candidate pools through shared per-model scoring with availability masking. Experiments on MMR-Bench and VL-RouterBench show that LatentRouter outperforms fixed-model, feature-level, and learned-router baselines. Additional analyses show that the gains are strongest on multimodal task groups where model choice depends on visual, layout-sensitive, or reasoning-oriented requirements, and that latent communication is the main contributor to the improvement. The code is available at: https://github.com/LabRAI/LatentRouter.

</details>


### [211] [Constraint-Data-Value-Maximization: Utilizing Data Attribution for Effective Data Pruning in Low-Data Environments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.11312)
*Danilo Brajovic,David A. Kreplin,Marco F. Huber*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文指出Shapley-based数据值在低数据量剪枝场景中表现不佳，并提出约束数据值最大化（CDVM）方法，通过将剪枝转化为约束优化问题——既最大化总影响力又惩罚过度的每测试贡献——在仅保留少量数据时实现稳健性能，在OpenDataVal基准上表现出色且运行时具有竞争力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于Shapley的数据值方法在仅保留少量数据的低数据场景下，不适合用于剪枝低价值数据。

Method: 提出约束数据值最大化（CDVM）方法，将剪枝问题建模为约束优化：最大化总影响力的同时惩罚过度的每测试贡献。

Result: 在OpenDataVal基准上，CDVM展现出强劲性能和具有竞争力的运行时间。

Conclusion: CDVM方法在低数据量剪枝场景下有效利用了数据归因，克服了Shapley-based方法的局限性。

Abstract: Attributing model behavior to training data is an evolving research field. A common benchmark is data removal, which involves eliminating data instances with either low or high values, then assessing a model's performance trained on the modified dataset. Many existing studies leverage Shapley-based data values for this task. In this paper, we demonstrate that these data values are not optimally suited for pruning low-value data when only a limited amount of data remains. To address this limitation, we introduce the Constraint-Data-Value-Maximization (CDVM) approach, which effectively utilizes data attributions for pruning in low-data scenarios. By casting pruning as a constrained optimization that both maximizes total influence and penalizes excessive per-test contributions, CDVM delivers robust performance when only a small fraction of the data is retained. On the OpenDataVal benchmark, CDVM shows strong performance and competitive runtime.

</details>


### [212] [Rethinking Evaluation for LLM Hallucination Detection: A Desiderata, A New RAG-based Benchmark, New Insights](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.11330)
*Wenbo Chen,Veena Padmanabhan,Tootiya Giyahchi,Elaine Wong,Leman Akoglu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文指出现有幻觉检测基准（HDBs）存在两大缺陷：缺乏基于RAG的长上下文基准以及缺少真实标签噪声。为此，作者构建了新的RAG基准TRIVIA+，其上下文长度最长，并提供了四种不同噪声模式的标签集。实验表明当前检测器在RAG基准上远未达到性能上限，LLM-as-a-Judge基线表现有竞争力，且标签噪声会降低检测性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM幻觉检测基准缺乏长上下文RAG场景的覆盖，且未提供现实场景中普遍存在的标签噪声用于压力测试，导致评估与实际应用脱节。

Method: 首先建立幻觉检测基准应具备的理想属性体系，发现现有基准的不足；然后构建新基准TRIVIA+（基于RAG、人工标注、最长上下文），并设计四种噪声标签方案（样本相关与样本无关）。

Result: TRIVIA+是当前上下文最长的RAG幻觉检测基准，提供了四种噪声标签模式；实验发现现有SOTA检测器在RAG基准上远未达到性能上限，LLM-as-a-Judge基线有竞争力，标签噪声显著降低检测性能。

Conclusion: RAG场景下的幻觉检测仍有很大提升空间，TRIVIA+基准将推动该领域研究。

Abstract: Hallucination, broadly referring to unfaithful, fabricated, or inconsistent content generated by LLMs, has wide-ranging implications. Therefore, a large body of effort has been devoted to detecting LLM hallucinations, as well as designing benchmark datasets for evaluating these detectors. In this work, we first establish a desiderata of properties for hallucination detection benchmarks (HDBs) to exhibit for effective evaluation. A critical look at existing HDBs through the lens of our desiderata reveals that none of them exhibits all the properties. We identify two largest gaps: (1) RAG-based grounded benchmarks with long context are severely lacking (partly because length impedes human annotation); and (2) Existing benchmarks do not make available realistic label noise for stress-testing detectors although real-world use-cases often grapple with label noise due to human or automated/weak annotation. To close these gaps, we build and open-source a new RAG-based HDB called T RIVIA+ that underwent a rigorous human annotation process. Notably, our benchmark exhibits all desirable properties including (1) T RIVIA+ contains samples with the longest context in the literature; and (2) we design and share four sets of noisy labels with different, both sample-dependent and sampleindependent, noise schemes. Finally, we perform experiments on RAG-based HDBs, including our T RIVIA+, using popular SOTA detectors that reveal new insights: (i) ample room remains for current detectors to reach the performance ceiling on RAG-based HDBs, (ii) the basic LLM-as-a-Judge baseline performs competitively, and (iii) label noise hinders detection performance. We expect that our findings, along with our proposed benchmark 1 , will motivate and foster needed research on hallucination detection for RAG-based tasks.

</details>


### [213] [CPEMH: An Agentic Framework for Prompt-Driven Behavior Evaluation and Assurance in Foundation-Model Systems for Mental Health Screening](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.11341)
*Giuliano Lorenzoni,Ivens Portugal,Paulo Alencar,Donald Cowan*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出 CPEMH，一个用于评估基于提示的基座模型行为（特别是在心理健康筛查转录数据集上）的智能体框架，通过编排式架构实现提示策略的自主设计、评估和选择，确保行为可控、可追溯、可复现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 面向大规模语言系统在对话式和临床敏感领域的部署需求，现有的提示策略缺乏系统性的行为保障方法，需要一种能够自动化设计、评估和选择提示策略的工程方法论，以控制和审计基座模型的行为变异性和稳定性。

Method: 提出一个模块化智能体框架 CPEMH，由编排智能体（orchestrator）、推理智能体（inference）和评估智能体（evaluation）组成，自主完成提示策略的设计、评估和选择，并在抑郁症自动筛查数据集上进行了案例验证。

Result: 案例研究表明该框架能够稳定和审计基座模型在对话式和临床敏感领域的行为；强调了模块化编排对行为保障的作用、稳定性优先于架构复杂性的原则，以及将 F1、偏差和鲁棒性作为核心验收标准。

Conclusion: CPEMH 为大语言系统提供了一种有效的行为保障工程方法论，模块化编排设计确保了提示生命周期的可追溯性、可复现性和鲁棒性，在心理健康筛查等临床敏感场景具有重要应用价值。

Abstract: This paper presents CPEMH, an agentic framework designed to evaluate prompt-driven behavior in foundation-model systems operating on transcript-based datasets for mental-health screening. CPEMH serves as an engineering methodology for behavioral assurance in large-scale language systems, introducing an orchestrated architecture that autonomously performs the design, evaluation, and selection of prompt strategies, enabling systematic control of behavioral variability across contexts. Its modular agentic design, combining orchestrator, inference, and evaluation agents, ensures traceability, reproducibility, and robustness throughout the prompting lifecycle. A case study on automated depression screening from interview transcripts demonstrates the framework's capacity to stabilize and audit foundation-model behavior in conversational and clinically sensitive domains. Lessons learned emphasize the role of modular orchestration in behavioral assurance, the prioritization of stability over architectural complexity, and the integration of F1, bias, and robustness as core acceptance criteria.

</details>


### [214] [CVEvolve: Autonomous Algorithm Discovery for Unstructured Scientific Data Processing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.11359)
*Ming Du,Xiangyu Yin,Yanqi Luo,Dishant Beniwal,Songyuan Tang,Hemant Sharma,Mathew J. Cherukara*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: CVEvolve是一个零代码自主智能体框架，通过多轮搜索策略（交替发现与改进动作）和谱系感知随机候选采样，为科学数据处理自动发现算法，在X射线荧光显微图像配准、布拉格峰检测和高能衍射显微图像分割三个任务上优于基线方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 科学数据处理通常需要任务特定算法或AI模型，这对缺乏计算和图像处理专业知识的领域科学家构成障碍，尤其是在数据存在噪声、高动态范围、稀疏标记或松散定义的情况下。

Method: CVEvolve是一个自主智能体框架，采用零代码接口，结合多轮搜索策略（交替进行发现和改进动作）、代码执行、评估实现、历史管理、留出测试和可选的科学数据/视觉输出检查工具，并使用谱系感知随机候选采样来平衡探索与利用。

Result: 在X射线荧光显微图像配准、布拉格峰检测和高能衍射显微图像分割三个任务上，CVEvolve发现的算法均优于基线方法，且留出测试追踪有助于识别泛化能力更好的候选算法。

Conclusion: 零代码、自主的LLM驱动算法开发能够帮助领域科学家将非结构化的科学图像数据转化为实用算法，推动下游科学发现。

Abstract: Scientific data processing often requires task-specific algorithms or AI models, creating a barrier for domain scientists who need to analyze their data but may not have extensive computing or image-processing expertise. This barrier is especially pronounced when data are noisy, have a high dynamic range, are sparsely labeled, or are only loosely specified. We introduce CVEvolve, an autonomous agentic harness with a zero-code interface for scientific data-processing algorithm discovery. CVEvolve combines a multi-round search strategy with tools for code execution, evaluation implementation, history management, holdout testing, and optional inspection of scientific data and visual outputs. The search alternates between discovery and improvement actions, and uses lineage-aware stochastic candidate sampling to balance exploration and exploitation. We demonstrate CVEvolve on x-ray fluorescence microscopy image registration, Bragg peak detection, and high-energy diffraction microscopy image segmentation. Across these tasks, CVEvolve discovers algorithms that improve over baseline methods, while holdout test tracking helps identify candidates that generalize better than later over-optimized alternatives. These results show that zero-code, autonomous LLM-powered algorithm development can help domain scientists turn unstructured scientific image data into practical algorithms and downstream scientific discoveries.

</details>


### [215] [Causal Bias Detection in Generative Artifical Intelligence](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.11365)
*Drago Plecko*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文形式化了生成式AI中的因果公平性问题，提出了统一标准ML与生成式AI的公平性理论框架，推导了新的因果分解方法以量化不同路径和机制替换带来的公平性影响，并建立了可识别的因果估计量，在LLM上验证了种族和性别偏见分析。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有因果公平性工作主要针对标准机器学习场景（单一预测机制），而生成式AI能够从任意条件分布采样、隐式构建所有因果机制，这种根本性差异导致现有方法不再适用，亟需新的因果公平性方法论。

Method: 1) 形式化生成式AI中的因果公平性问题，并将其与标准ML场景统一到共同理论框架下；2) 推导新的因果分解结果，能够沿（a）不同因果路径和（b）真实机制被生成模型机制替换两个维度对公平性影响进行细粒度量化；3) 建立识别条件并引入高效因果估计量。

Result: 在多个数据集上使用大语言模型分析种族和性别偏见，验证了所提出方法论的有效性。

Conclusion: 生成式AI的因果公平性需要区别于标准ML场景的全新方法论，本文提出的理论框架和分解方法能够有效量化生成式AI中不同因果路径及机制替换带来的公平性影响，为生成式AI的公平性审计提供了因果基础。

Abstract: Automated systems built on artificial intelligence (AI) are increasingly deployed across high-stakes domains, raising critical concerns about fairness and the perpetuation of demographic disparities that exist in the world. In this context, causal inference provides a principled framework for reasoning about fairness, as it links observed disparities to underlying mechanisms and aligns naturally with human intuition and legal notions of discrimination. Prior work on causal fairness primarily focuses on the standard machine learning setting, where a decision-maker constructs a single predictive mechanism $f_{\widehat Y}$ for an outcome variable $Y$, while inheriting the causal mechanisms of all other covariates from the real world. The generative AI setting, however, is markedly more complex: generative models can sample from arbitrary conditionals over any set of variables, implicitly constructing their own beliefs about all causal mechanisms rather than learning a single predictive function. This fundamental difference requires new developments in causal fairness methodology. We formalize the problem of causal fairness in generative AI and unify it with the standard ML setting under a common theoretical framework. We then derive new causal decomposition results that enable granular quantification of fairness impacts along both (a) different causal pathways and (b) the replacement of real-world mechanisms by the generative model's mechanisms. We establish identification conditions and introduce efficient estimators for causal quantities of interest, and demonstrate the value of our methodology by analyzing race and gender bias in large language models across different datasets.

</details>


### [216] [Causal Algorithmic Recourse: Foundations and Methods](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.11373)
*Drago Plecko,Collin Wang,Elias Bareinboim*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种因果框架，将算法追索（algorithmic recourse）建模为个体在干预前后可能经历不同潜在条件下的重复决策过程，引入后追索稳定性条件，并开发了基于copula的推断方法及无分布算法，用于从观测数据或追索数据中估计追索效应。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有算法追索方法将追索结果视为固定单元的反事实，忽略了现实世界中追索涉及同一个体在不同潜在条件下多次决策的情况，缺乏对追索过程动态性的建模能力。

Method: 1. 构建因果框架，对干预前和干预后结果进行建模，允许部分稳定性和潜在变量的重采样。2. 引入后追索稳定性条件，使仅从观测数据即可推理追索效应。3. 提出基于copula的算法以推断追索效应。4. 当存在追索数据（同一体干预前后配对观测）时，开发copula参数推断和拟合优度检验方法。5. 当copula模型被拒绝时，提供无分布算法直接从追索数据学习追索效应。

Result: 在真实数据集和半合成数据集上验证了所提方法的有效性。

Conclusion: 本文为算法追索问题提供了更贴近现实的因果建模框架，克服了传统反事实方法的静态局限，并通过多种推断方法实现了灵活的追索效应估计。

Abstract: The trustworthiness of AI decision-making systems is increasingly important. A key feature of such systems is the ability to provide recommendations for how an individual may reverse a negative decision, a problem known as algorithmic recourse. Existing approaches treat recourse outcomes as counterfactuals of a fixed unit, ignoring that real-world recourse involves repeated decisions on the same individual under possibly different latent conditions. We develop a causal framework that models recourse as a process over pre- and post-intervention outcomes, allowing for partial stability and resampling of latent variables. We introduce post-recourse stability conditions that enable reasoning about recourse from observational data alone, and develop a copula-based algorithm for inferring the effects of recourse under these conditions. For settings where paired observations of the same individual before and after intervention are available (called recourse data), we develop methods for inferring copula parameters and performing goodness-of-fit testing. When the copula model is rejected, we provide a distribution-free algorithm for learning recourse effects directly from recourse data. We demonstrate the value of the proposed methods on real and semi-synthetic datasets.

</details>


### [217] [LLM-X: A Scalable Negotiation-Oriented Exchange for Communication Among Personal LLM Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.11376)
*Giuliano Lorenzoni,Paulo Alencar,Donald Cowan*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出了一种个人LLM交换系统(LLM-X)，这是一个可扩展的基于协商的环境，支持多个个人智能体(LLM)之间的直接结构化通信，通过消息总线和路由机制实现LLM到LLM的协调，并保证模式有效性和策略执行。实验在5、9、12个智能体规模下，不同协商策略和短期/长期负载条件下验证了系统的稳定性与策略-性能权衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有工具中心化协议主要关注智能体与API的交互，缺乏支持多个个人LLM智能体之间直接、结构化通信的可扩展协商环境，因此需要一种新的架构来实现LLM间的协调。

Method: 提出了LLM-X架构，包括：(1)联邦网关、基于主题的路由和策略执行；(2)支持能力协商和合同网协调的类型化消息协议；(3)在5、9、12个智能体规模下，在不同协商策略(低、中、高)和短期(分钟级)及长期(2小时、12小时)负载条件下进行实证评估。

Result: 更严格的策略提高了鲁棒性和公平性，但增加了延迟和消息量；长期运行表明LLM-X在持续负载下保持稳定，延迟漂移有界。

Conclusion: LLM-X能够有效支持大规模多智能体协商，存在清晰的策略-性能权衡，系统在长期负载下保持稳定。

Abstract: We propose a personal-LLM exchange (LLM-X), a scalable negotiation-oriented environment that enables direct, structured communication across populations of personal agents (LLMs), each representing an individual user. Unlike existing tool-centric protocols that focus on agent-API interaction, LLM-X introduces a message bus and routing substrate for LLM-to-LLM coordination with guarantees around schema validity and policy enforcement. We contribute: (1) an architecture for LLM-X comprising federated gateways, topic-based routing, and policy enforcement; (2) a typed message protocol supporting capability negotiation and contract-net-style coordination; and (3) the first empirical evaluation of LLM-based multi-agent negotiation at scale. Experiments span 5, 9, and 12 agents, under distinct negotiation policies (Low, Medium, High), and across both short-run (minutes) and long-run (2h, 12h) load conditions. Results highlight clear policy-performance trade-offs: stricter policies improve robustness and fairness but increase latencies and message volume. Extended runs confirm that LLM-X remains stable under sustained load, with bounded latency drift.

</details>


### [218] [Revisiting Privacy Preservation in Brain-Computer Interfaces: Conceptual Boundaries, Risk Pathways, and a Protection-Strength Grading Framework](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.11386)
*Lei Sun,Xiuqing Mao,Shuai Zhang,Qingyu Zeng,Min Zhao,Jiyuan Li,Wenle Dong*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文围绕脑机接口（BCI）的隐私保护问题，提出一个三维分析框架（保护对象、生命周期阶段、保护强度等级），将现有工作划分为四个保护强度级别，并强调隐私保护不仅应模糊数据，还应解缠任务无关的敏感信息以保持下游效用，同时将心理隐私和神经伦理风险作为开放问题讨论。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: BCI技术正从实验室快速走向临床和现实应用，其隐私风险远超原始神经信号泄露——神经数据、衍生表征、模型资产以及解码输出在采集、传输、存储、训练、推理和反馈等全生命周期中均可被重新关联至个人，或用于推断超出任务所需的信息。现有研究缺乏统一的隐私保护边界、保护对象以及用户数据隐私与模型隐私之间共享风险路径的清晰定义。

Method: 首先从通用BCI范式出发，定义隐私保护边界、保护对象，以及用户数据隐私与模型隐私在共享风险路径中的关系。然后提出一个三维分类框架（保护对象、生命周期阶段、主导保护强度等级），将现有工作划分为四个保护强度级别。

Result: 现有BCI隐私保护工作可被系统性地归类到四个保护强度等级中；同时识别出心理隐私和神经伦理风险作为尚未解决的开放问题。

Conclusion: BCI隐私保护不应仅停留在数据模糊化层面，更应实现任务无关敏感信息的解缠，在保护隐私的同时保持下游任务效用。心理隐私与神经伦理风险是未来需要重点关注的方向。

Abstract: Brain-computer interfaces (BCIs) are moving rapidly from laboratory research into clinical, edge, and real-world settings. Under ISO/IEC 8663:2025, a BCI is a direct communication link between central nervous system activity and external software or hardware systems. This link expands privacy risk beyond raw neural-signal leakage: neural data, derived representations, model assets, and decoded outputs can be re-associated with individuals across collection, transmission, storage, training, inference, and feedback, or used to infer information beyond what a task requires. Starting from the general BCI paradigm, this review deffnes privacy-protection boundaries, protection objects, and the relationship between user data privacy and model privacy within a shared risk pathway. It then proposes a three-dimensional framework - protection object, lifecycle stage, and dominant protection-strength level - to classify existing work into four levels of protection strength. Finally, mental privacy and neuroethical risks are treated as open issues, emphasizing that BCI privacy protection should not only obscure data but also disentangle task-irrelevant sensitive information while preserving downstream utility. Keywords: Brain-computer interface, Neural data privacy, User data privacy, Model privacy, Disentanglement of task-irrelevant sensitive information, Protection-strength grading, Neuroethical risks

</details>


### [219] [Transformer Interpretability from Perspective of Attention and Gradient](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.11392)
*Yongjin Cui,Xiaohui Fan,Huajun Chen*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出通过引导梯度方向（即注意力方向）来增强Transformer模型的可解释性，并发现利用ViT与人类感知差异可实现对图像类别的微小改写，带来潜在安全风险。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管Transformer模型性能备受关注，但其可解释性研究同样重要。现有基于梯度的解释方法存在不足，需要更全面地解释特征区域并理解Transformer机制。

Method: 从注意力和梯度两个视角深入研究Transformer可解释性，提出通过引导梯度方向（更精确地说是注意力方向）来实现可解释性的方法。

Result: 该方法能够更全面地解释特征区域，提供细节级别的解释，并帮助更好理解Transformer机制。同时发现，利用ViT与人类感知差异，可以几乎不可察觉地改变图像类别，实现类别重写。

Conclusion: 引导梯度/注意力方向可以有效增强Transformer可解释性；ViT与人类感知差异导致的类别重写现象在某些场景下可能带来安全风险。

Abstract: Although researchers' attention is more focused on the performance of Transformer models, the interpretation of Transformer can never be ignored. Gradient is widely utilized in Transformer interpretation. From the perspective of attention and gradient, we conduct an in-depth study of Transformer interpretation and propose a method to achieve it by guiding the gradient direction, or more precisely, the attention direction. The method enables more comprehensive interpretation of feature regions, offers detail interpretation, and helps to better understand Transformer mechanism. Leveraging the difference in how Vision Transformer (ViT) and humans perceive images, we alter the class of an image in a way that is almost imperceptible to the human eye. This class rewriting phenomenon may potentially pose security risks in certain scenarios.

</details>


### [220] [AcuityBench: Evaluating Clinical Acuity Identification and Uncertainty Alignment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.11398)
*Robin Linzmayer,Georgianna Lin,Di Coneybeare,Jason Chu,Trudi Cloyd,Manish Garg,Miles Gordon,Elizabeth Hartofilis,Benjamin Hong,Ashraf Hussain,Eugene Y. Kim,Oluchi Iheagwara King,Ross McCormack,Erica Olsen,John K. Riggins,Mustafa N. Rasheed,Dana L. Sacco,Vinay Saggar,Osman R. Sayan,Amit Shembekar,Janice Shin-Kim,Wendy W. Sun,Bernard P. Chang,David Kessler,Noémie Elhadad*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: AcuityBench是一个评估语言模型识别医疗紧急程度能力的基准，包含914个病例和两种任务格式。测试12个前沿模型发现：模型在清晰病例上的准确率差异显著；对话式回复减少过度分诊但增加分诊不足；在模糊病例中，模型预测无法匹配医生判断的分布。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有健康基准侧重于医学问答、广泛健康交互或狭窄的特定工作流分诊任务，缺乏跨场景统一评估模型识别医疗紧急程度（acuity）的能力，而这一能力对于安全关键的医疗应用至关重要。

Method: 整合5个公开数据集（用户对话、在线论坛帖子、临床 vignettes、患者门户消息），统一为四级紧急程度框架（家庭监护到急诊）。构建914个病例，其中697个共识病例用于标准评估，217个医生确认的模糊病例用于不确定性感知评估。支持两种任务格式：显式四分类（QA）和自由对话式回复（基于rubric评估）。

Result: 12个前沿模型在清晰病例的紧急程度准确率和错误方向上存在显著差异。QA格式与对话格式存在系统性权衡：对话减少过度分诊但增加分诊不足。在模糊病例中，模型预测无法匹配医生判断的分布，且模型预测比专家临床不确定性更集中。

Conclusion: 紧急程度识别是一项独立的安全关键能力，AcuityBench能够系统比较和压力测试模型在真实医疗场景中引导用户获得适当护理水平的能力。

Abstract: We introduce AcuityBench, a benchmark for evaluating whether language models identify the appropriate urgency of care from user medical presentations. Existing health benchmarks emphasize medical question answering, broad health interactions, or narrow workflow-specific triage tasks, but they do not offer a unified evaluation of acuity identification across these settings. AcuityBench addresses this gap by harmonizing five public datasets spanning user conversations, online forum posts, clinical vignettes, and patient portal messages under a shared four-level acuity framework ranging from home monitoring to immediate emergency care. The benchmark contains 914 cases, including 697 consensus cases for standard accuracy evaluation and 217 physician-confirmed ambiguous cases for uncertainty-aware evaluation. It supports two complementary task formats: explicit four-way classification in a QA setting, and free-form conversational responses evaluated with a rubric-based judge anchored to the same framework. Across 12 frontier proprietary and open-weight models, we find substantial variation in clear-case acuity accuracy and error direction. Comparing task formats reveals a systematic tradeoff: conversational responses reduce over-triage but increase under-triage relative to QA, especially in higher-acuity cases. In ambiguous cases, no model closely matches the distribution of physician judgments, and model predictions are more concentrated than expert clinical uncertainty. We also compare expert and model adjudication on a subset of maximally ambiguous cases, using those cases to examine the role of clinical uncertainty in label disagreement. Together, these results position acuity identification as a distinct safety-critical capability and show that AcuityBench enables systematic comparison and stress-testing of how well models guide users to the right level of care in real-world health use.

</details>


### [221] [Attributing Emergence in Million-Agent Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.11404)
*Ling Tang,Jilin Mei,Qian Chen,Qihan Ren,Linfeng Zhang,Quanshi Zhang,Jing Shao,Xia Hu,Dongrui Liu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文针对大规模语言模型驱动的多智能体系统（MAS）中宏观涌现的归因问题，提出了基于Aumann-Shapley路径积分的归因方法，可在百万智能体规模下运行，比传统Shapley采样方法快4-5个数量级。通过对Bluesky平台14天数据（1,671,587活跃用户）的实验，发现全量归因与小规模采样归因存在结构性差异：全量尺度下长尾和中层用户共同承担主要归因，而小规模偏差样本将归因集中于高粉丝账户。进一步证明了归因缩放偏差定理：对于任何非线性宏观指标，无法通过全局缩放因子调和全量与小规模的归因结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有归因方法在N ≤ 10³规模下运行，而LLM驱动的MAS模拟的社会现象（如极化、信息级联、市场恐慌）发生在N ≥ 10⁶规模，存在数量级上的归因方法论空白。

Method: 将Aumann-Shapley路径积分归因方法适配到LLM驱动的MAS中，使其满足全部四条公理（对称性、线性性、虚拟性、有效性），并在百万智能体规模上高效运行。

Result: 该方法比采样Shapley快4-5个数量级；全量归因与小规模采样归因存在结构性差异；证明归因缩放偏差定理：非线性宏观指标下，无法通过全局缩放因子调和不同规模的归因结果。

Conclusion: 全量归因不是方法选择问题，而是针对非线性宏观指标的理论要求。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) can simulate human-like reasoning and decision-making in individual agents. LLM-powered multi-agent systems (MAS) combine such agents to simulate population-scale social phenomena such as polarization, information cascades, and market panics. Such studies require attributing macro emergence to individual agents, but existing axiomatic methods scale combinatorially in $N$ and have been confined to $N \lesssim 10^3$, while the phenomena they explain occur at $N \geq 10^6$. We address this gap by adapting Aumann--Shapley path-integral attribution to LLM-powered MAS at million-agent scale; the resulting method satisfies all four axioms, runs four to five orders of magnitude faster than sampled Shapley on the same hardware. We use this method to test the scale gap empirically: across 14 days of public Bluesky data ($1{,}671{,}587$ active users), we compute the attribution at both full scale and the visibility-biased $N = 10^2$ convenience sample used by small-scale studies, and the two disagree structurally. At full scale the long tail and middle tier jointly carry the majority; the biased small panel attributes almost everything to a few high-follower accounts. We then prove that under any nonlinear macro indicator the disagreement cannot be reduced by post-hoc rescaling: an Attribution Scaling Bias theorem shows that no global rescaling factor can reconcile small-scale and full-scale attribution. Full-scale attribution is therefore not a methodological choice but a theoretical requirement for any nonlinear macro indicator.

</details>


### [222] [What Do EEG Foundation Models Capture from Human Brain Signals?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.11410)
*Ling Tang,Qian Chen,Jilin Mei,Houshi Xu,Quanshi Zhang,Jing Shao,Na Zou,Xia Hu,Dongrui Liu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文系统审计了三个EEG基础模型(CSBrain, CBraMod, LaBraM)与传统手工特征词典(6类63个特征)在五个临床任务上的表征对齐程度。通过层级岭回归探测、LEACE子空间擦除和透明分类器基准测试，发现945个(模型,任务,特征)单元中68.6%是表征因果性的，21.1%仅被编码。频率域特征主导，但所有特征家族都贡献了显著的因果质量。确认的特征平均恢复了基础模型相对于随机基线79.3%的优势，任务难度梯度明显(MDD≈0.99到Stress≈0.56)。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 临床EEG分析依赖数十年积累的手工特征(如频带功率、连通性、复杂度等)，而现代EEG基础模型通过自监督预训练直接从原始信号学习，在大多数临床基准上匹配或超越基于特征工程的方法。这两类表征是否对齐是一个未解决的关键问题，本文将其分解为三个子问题：模型学到了什么、模型使用了什么、以及有多少可以被解释。

Method: 采用三层分析方法：(1)层级岭回归探测(layer-wise ridge probing)检查模型各层对每个手工特征的编码程度；(2)LEACE风格交叉协方差子空间擦除检验特征对模型预测的因果重要性；(3)透明分类器与随机特征基线对比评估表征可解释性。

Result: 945个单元中648个(68.6%)是表征因果性(RC)的，199个(21.1%)仅被编码。50个特征在多个任务中跨所有三个架构具有强支持(通用候选)。频率域特征占主导但其他五个家族也贡献大量因果质量。确认的特征恢复基础模型79.3%的优势，MDD任务接近完全恢复(0.99)，Stress任务恢复较少(0.56)。

Conclusion: 传统手工特征词典能解释基础模型大部分性能，但存在任务依赖的残余差距，为未来发现新的临床相关特征指明了方向。

Abstract: Clinical electroencephalogram (EEG) analysis rests on a hand-crafted feature catalog refined over decades, \emph{e.g.,} band power, connectivity, complexity, and more. Modern EEG foundation models bypass this catalog, learn directly from raw signals via self-supervised pretraining, and match or outperform feature-engineered baselines on most clinical benchmarks. Whether the two representations align is an open question, which we decompose into three sub-questions: \emph{what does the model learn}, \emph{what does the model use}, and \emph{how much can be explained}. We answer them with layer-wise ridge probing, LEACE-style cross-covariance subspace erasure, and a transparent classifier benchmarked against a random-feature baseline. The audit covers three foundation models (CSBrain, CBraMod, LaBraM), five clinical tasks (MDD, Stress, ISRUC-Sleep, TUSL, Siena), and a 6-family 63-feature lexicon. Of the $945$ (model, task, feature) units, $648$ ($68.6\%$) are representation-causal and $199$ ($21.1\%$) are encoded-only. Across tasks, $50$ features qualify as universal candidates with strong support (all three architectures RC) in two or more tasks. Frequency-domain features dominate, but the other five families each contribute substantial causal mass. Confirmed features recover, on average, $79.3\%$ of the foundation model's advantage over the random baseline, with a clean task gradient (MDD $\approx 0.99$ down to Stress $\approx 0.56$): tasks near ceiling are almost fully recovered by the lexicon, while harder tasks leave a non-trivial residual that pinpoints a concrete target for future concept discovery.

</details>


### [223] [Under the Hood of SKILL.md: Semantic Supply-chain Attacks on AI Agent Skill Registry](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.11418)
*Shoumik Saha,Kazem Faghih,Soheil Feizi*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文研究了AI Agent通过SKILL.md文件扩展能力时面临的语义供应链风险。实验表明，在技能发现、选择和治理三个阶段，攻击者可通过操纵SKILL.md中的自然语言描述，分别实现86%的对抗性检索胜率、77.6%的选择偏向率以及36.5%-100%的治理规避率，证明SKILL.md是影响Agent行为的操作性文本而非被动文档。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自主AI Agent通过Agent Skills（模块化文件系统包）扩展能力时，其SKILL.md文件中的自然语言元数据和指令会影响技能的准入、展示、选择和加载，从而引入语义层面的供应链安全风险。

Method: 针对Agent Skill生命周期的三个阶段（发现、选择、治理）设计SKILL.md-only攻击实验，使用真实ClawHub技能和真实注册机制进行评估。

Result: 发现阶段：短文本触发器操纵嵌入检索，实现86%的对抗性胜率和80%的Top-10放置率；选择阶段：描述偏差使对抗性变体在77.6%的配对试验中被选中；治理阶段：语义规避策略使恶意技能在36.5%-100%的情况下逃避阻止判定。

Conclusion: SKILL.md并非被动文档，而是塑造Agent发现、信任和使用第三方能力的操作性文本，揭示了自然语言元数据驱动的语义供应链攻击向量。

Abstract: Autonomous AI agents increasingly extend their capabilities through Agent Skills: modular filesystem packages whose SKILL.md files describe when and how agents should use them. While this design enables scalable, on-demand capability expansion, it also introduces a semantic supply-chain risk in which natural-language metadata and instructions can affect which skills are admitted, surfaced, selected, and loaded. We study SKILL.md - only attacks across three registry-facing stages of the Agent Skill lifecycle, using real ClawHub skills and realistic registry mechanisms. In Discovery, short textual triggers can manipulate embedding-based retrieval and improve adversarial skill visibility, achieving up to 86% pairwise win rate and 80% Top-10 placement. In Selection, description-only framing biases agents toward functionally equivalent adversarial variants, which are selected in 77.6% of paired trials on average. In Governance, semantic evasion strategies cause malicious skills to avoid a blocking verdict in 36.5%-100% of cases. Overall, our results show that SKILL.md is not passive documentation but operational text that shapes which third-party capabilities agents find, trust, and use.

</details>


### [224] [A Mechanistic Investigation of Supervised Fine Tuning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.11426)
*Ruhaan Chopra*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文利用预训练的稀疏自编码器（SAE）作为高分辨率诊断工具，发现虽然大语言模型在监督微调（SFT）前后隐藏层激活的余弦相似度很高，但底层的稀疏潜变量实际上发生了显著分化。研究揭示了SFT导致的特定任务、特定层级的语义特征分布变化，并识别出安全对齐特有的逐层更新模式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 表面上看SFT前后模型激活几何结构变化不大（余弦相似度高），但作者怀疑底层表征实际上发生了实质性变化。为了揭示这些隐藏的、细粒度的表示差异，作者引入SAE作为高分辨率诊断工具进行机制性分析。

Method: 提出一种新颖的研究管线：使用预训练在基座模型上的SAE作为高分辨率诊断工具，比较基座模型与SFT模型在相同输入下隐藏层激活的稀疏潜变量差异，从而在细粒度语义特征层面机制性地研究表征发散的原因。

Result: 发现SFT会系统性地改变特定任务和特定层级的精确语义特征分布；并且识别出安全对齐特有的逐层更新模式。

Conclusion: SAE可以作为揭示SFT对模型内部表征细粒度影响的有效工具，高余弦相似度掩盖了底层稀疏特征的实质性变化，且这些变化在任务和层级上具有特异性。

Abstract: The cosine similarity between a large language model's hidden activations before and after Supervised Fine-Tuning (SFT) remains very high. This, at first glance, suggests that SFT leaves the model's activation geometry largely undisturbed. However, projecting both sets of activations through a Sparse Autoencoder (SAE) pretrained on the base model reveals that the underlying sparse latents diverge significantly. We introduce a novel investigative pipeline which utilizes these pretrained SAEs as a high-resolution diagnostic tool to mechanistically investigate the drivers of this representational divergence. Through our analytical pipeline, we discover task-specific and layer-specific distributions of the precise semantic features that are systematically altered during supervised fine-tuning.
